### [1] [When Nonlocal Total Derivatives Are Not Variationally Trivial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14612)
*Carlos Heredia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究无限阶和非局域拉格朗日力学中的全时间导数，指出其在形式无限阶框架下仍变分平凡，但在非局域泛函情形下，即使L=DW（即全导数形式），其非局域欧拉-拉格朗日表达式未必为零，并构造了一个光滑卷积型反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清全时间导数在非局域变分问题中是否仍保持变分平凡性这一基本问题，修正对非局域理论中‘边界项’作用的直观理解。

Method: 采用形式变分分析与非局域微积分框架，构造具光滑核的卷积型泛函作为显式反例，计算其非局域欧拉-拉格朗日泛函导数。

Result: 证明存在光滑非局域拉格朗日量L=DW，但其对应的非局域欧拉-拉格朗日表达式非零；该现象源于非局域变分导数对卷积核支撑结构的敏感依赖。

Conclusion: 非局域力学中‘全时间导数’不再自动导致变分平凡性，因此非局域作用量的等价类需按非局域欧拉-拉格朗日同调而非经典全导数关系来分类。

Abstract: We study total time derivatives in infinite-order and nonlocal Lagrangian mechanics. In the formal infinite-order setting, such terms remain variationally trivial. For nonlocal functionals on trajectories, however, exactness in the form $L=DW$ is not sufficient to ensure a vanishing nonlocal Euler-Lagrange expression. We exhibit a smooth convolution-type counterexample.

</details>


### [2] [A cubic closed string field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14670)
*Harold Erbin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Hubbard-Stratonovich变换规避了Sonoda-Zwiebach不可行性定理，构建了一个全阶立方闭弦场论：引入无限塔辅助k-弦场Σₖ，将四点及以上顶点转化为动能项与三次耦合，保持作用量为立方、每个辅助场至多二次，并满足三角结构；消除辅助场后可逐阶恢复原作用量（树图水平）；原规范对称性自然提升，且辅助场在动量通用处不引入新物理态；世界面解释中Σ₂对应纠缠双弦态，四次顶点由非对称三次顶点胶合而成；弦顶点满足扩展的 genus-zero IBL_∞ 关系当且仅当弦乘积构成循环 L_∞ 代数；最后与Chiaffrino-Mamade-Zwiebach的bar-cobar构造进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破Sonoda-Zwiebach（1990）关于闭玻色弦场论无法实现单弦场、对称三次顶点与标准传播子的不可行性定理，寻求一个真正立方、可重整化且具备清晰几何/代数结构的闭弦场论表述。

Method: 采用Hubbard-Stratonovich变换引入无穷多个辅助k-弦场Σₖ（k ≥ 2），其耦合强度为g_s^{2k−2}；将原始高阶顶点重写为含Σₖ的二次动能项与三次相互作用项；利用高斯积分消去辅助场以恢复原始微扰展开；结合世界面OPE分析与局部坐标运动，诠释Σ₂为纠缠双弦态，并推广至k- puncture patch gluing；验证IBL_∞与L_∞代数的等价性条件。

Result: 首次显式构造出满足以下性质的全阶立方闭弦场论：(i) 作用量严格三次（cubic）且每个Σₖ至多二次；(ii) 具有三角结构，保证树图级可逆还原；(iii) 原规范对称性无参数扩充地提升；(iv) 辅助场不引入额外物理极点（在Σₖ动能算符可逆的动量区域）；(v) Σ₂具有明确世界面诠释——作为两弦纠缠态，四次顶点由非对称三次顶点胶合生成；(vi) 弦顶点满足扩展genus-zero IBL_∞关系 ⇔ 弦乘积构成循环L_∞代数。

Conclusion: Hubbard-Stratonovich变换为闭弦场论提供了新的代数-几何统一框架：它绕过了传统不可行性障碍，实现了严格立方形式，同时保留全部物理内容与对称性；辅助场对应于多 puncture patch 的胶合自由度，暗示一种‘分形’或‘递归’的弦顶点构造范式；该构造与bar-cobar方法存在深刻联系，但具备更直接的世界面实现与更简洁的作用量结构。

Abstract: The action of closed bosonic string field theory is non-polynomial, and for more than thirty years a no-go theorem of Sonoda and Zwiebach (1990) has excluded a cubic formulation with a single string field, a symmetric vertex and the canonical propagator. We evade it with a Hubbard--Stratonovich transformation and provide an explicit construction of a cubic closed string field theory at all orders. The transformation introduces an infinite tower of auxiliary $k$-string fields $\Sigma_k$, entering at order $g_s^{2 k - 2}$, which turn the vertices with four or more legs into kinetic terms and cubic couplings. The action is cubic, at most quadratic in each auxiliary field, and triangular: eliminating the auxiliary fields by Gaussian integrals recovers the original action order by order at tree level, under invertibility and kernel conditions on the auxiliary kinetic operators. The original gauge symmetry lifts to the cubic action with no new parameter, and we argue that the auxiliary fields add no physical state at generic momentum, wherever their kinetic operators are invertible. On the worldsheet, $\Sigma_2$ is an entangled two-string state: by an operator product expansion, the quartic form reduces to a gluing of two asymmetric cubic vertices dressed by the motion of the local coordinates and defined on states without level matching. We expect this picture to generalize to higher orders, defining a gluing of patches carrying $k$ punctures each. The string vertices, read as products with several outputs, satisfy an extended form of the genus-zero $\mathrm{IBL}_\infty$ relations, including operations with no input or no output, exactly when the string products form a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra. Finally, we compare our action with the bar--cobar construction of Chiaffrino, Mamade and Zwiebach.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 确立U作为factorization twist、Berry phase与renormalized stable envelope的三重等价；证明YBE等价于Berry联络的平坦性（由SUSY保证）；推导出隧道振幅满足的精确递归关系；发现不同隧道通道间的内在纠缠导致精细振幅抵消；揭示墙穿越现象——同一物理振幅随参数变化在不同通道基底下呈现不同展开。

Conclusion: R-矩阵的U因子是连接代数可积性、量子场论几何相位与代数几何稳定包络的核心对象；其物理实现不仅满足YBE，更蕴含丰富的拓扑与组合结构（如递归、通道纠缠、墙穿越），为Yangian表示论与超对称规范理论的交叉提供了新范式。

Abstract: XXX spin-chain Hamiltonians can be derived within the $R$-matrix formalism. For a simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$, the rational $R$-matrix $R$ intertwines the coproduct $\Delta$ in the Yangian $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{g})$. $R$ admits a particular Gauss decomposition $R=LDU$, where the upper-triangular matrix $U$ can be obtained from the lower-triangular matrix $L$. We treat $U$ in three ways. Algebraically, $U$ is known as a factorization twist. It plays the role of a ``universalizer'': it conjugates a non-universal pseudo-coproduct $\mathscr{D}$ in the molten crystal chain basis to the true Yangian coproduct $\Delta=U^{-1}\mathscr{D} U$. Physically, $U$ can be obtained as a Berry phase in SUSY quiver QFT for a quiver associated with the Dynkin diagram of $\mathfrak{g}$. In this case, $U$ is calculated as a sum over tunneling amplitudes. Geometrically, $U$ can be interpreted as a renormalized Maulik-Okounkov stable envelope localized at a fixed point. In the physical setting, the Yang-Baxter equation (YBE), which ensures integrability, follows from the flatness of the Berry connection, which in turn follows from supersymmetry. However, the YBE imposes subtle constraints on the tunneling amplitudes, which we examine here. We construct recursion relations that allow higher collective amplitudes to be obtained from more elementary ones. We observe that different tunneling channels are in fact intertwined, leading to finer cancellations among amplitudes and to wall-crossing phenomena: depending on the parameters, the same amplitude is represented as a sum over different channels. We focus on $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ with higher-spin and non-evaluation representations, as well as on the fundamental representation of $\mathsf{Y}(\mathfrak{sl}_{n+1})$.

</details>


### [4] [Casimir Attraction and Repulsion in a Magnetic Domain-Wall Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14799)
*Inés Cavero-Peláez,Beatriz Gómez-López,Jose M. Muñoz-Castañeda,Gonzalo Sancho-Garrido*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在正弦-戈登孤子背景中，两个δ-δ'板之间的一圈Casimir相互作用，分析了其稳定性、能量符号变化及力与能量的非同步变号行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平凡拓扑背景（如sine-Gordon kink）下边界条件（δ-δ' plates）对量子真空涨落（Casimir效应）的影响，尤其关注系统稳定性、零模排除与力/能量符号分离现象。

Method: 采用一维量子场论方法：将横向涨落问题建模为S² sigma模型的高斯截断（等价于含畴壁的易轴反铁磁体），计算背景修饰的相对行列式，精确分解为梯度依赖的有效板强；通过频谱分析确定各频率通道能量符号，并数值求解能量与力随板位置c和间距d的变化。

Result: 当v ≥ 2|w|时算符非负；v > 2|w|时严格稳定且无零模；相对行列式精确可分解；能量与力变号位置不同（如v=1.05,w=0.5,d=1.5时，能量在c≈−0.44,2.63变号，力在c≈−0.30,2.00变号）；相同取向板在平移时由近核吸引转为远核排斥。

Conclusion: Casimir相互作用在拓扑背景中呈现丰富空间依赖性：力决定物理吸引/排斥，而非能量符号；严格稳定耦合确保理论一致性；结果揭示了边界-拓扑耦合对量子真空效应的定性调控能力。

Abstract: We study the one-loop Casimir interaction between two $\delta$--$\delta'$ plates in the fixed one-soliton Pöschl--Teller background of the sine-Gordon kink. The transverse fluctuation problem is the Gaussian truncation of an $S^2$ sigma model, equivalently an easy-axis antiferromagnet with a domain wall, in which the plates exert no classical force on the wall. For $v\ge2|w|$ the fluctuation operators are non-negative, and strictly stable couplings, $v>2|w|$, also exclude zero modes, so the quadratic field theory is consistent at the fixed sine-Gordon mass. The background-dressed relative determinant factorises exactly into effective, gradient-dependent strengths of the two plates, and the sign of each frequency channel of the interaction energy is fixed by their orientation. Two identical, equally oriented, strictly stable plates switch from attraction near the kink to repulsion away from it under a rigid translation at fixed couplings and separation. The interaction energy and the force conjugate to the separation neither share their sign everywhere nor change sign at the same positions: there are regions of negative energy and repulsive force, so attraction and repulsion must be read from the force. For such plates with $w\neq0$ the force is repulsive at short and attractive at large separation, and the interaction energy has a minimum at a separation that depends on the position of the pair. For $v=1.05$, $w=0.5$ and $d=1.5$ the energy changes sign at $c\simeq-0.44$ and $2.63$, and the force at $c\simeq-0.30$ and $2.00$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ILW方程的双线性形式未显式处理tau函数零点穿越积分围道所引发的主值积分不连续性；需建立能统一描述零点在围道内外不同拓扑配置的动力学框架。

Method: 将ILW方程的主值积分置于Sato超函数理论框架下严格定义；以tau函数在解析带内零点的留数贡献为源项，导出残差扩展ILW系统；引入整数值残差指标r∈ℤ分类孤子扇区；显式构造具指定r值的单/多孤子tau函数（含升降混合），并分析其Fourier谱渐近行为。

Result: 1) 得到残差扩展ILW方程族，其源项由tau函数自身零点位置与阶数自洽确定；2) 建立残差指标r的孤子分类：r=0对应传统隆起孤子，r≠0对应凹陷孤子及高阶隆起分支；3) 构造完全可积的混合升降孤子解，其r值加性守恒且碰撞不变；4) 孤子谱呈e^{−γ|k|}型指数衰减，衰减率γ=dist(singularity, ℝ)，且γ→0当且仅当趋近r为奇数的扇区边界。

Conclusion: 残差指标r是ILW孤子系统的内在拓扑量子数，它不仅分类孤子类型、控制非线性相互作用结构，更直接编码其频谱解析性质；该框架将孤子拓扑、复分析与可积系统动力学统一于超函数语言之中。

Abstract: We revisit the bilinear formulation of the intermediate long-wave (ILW) equation by interpreting its principal-value integrals in the framework of Sato hyperfunctions. When the tau-functions have no zeros inside the relevant contours, the conventional source-free ILW equation is recovered. When zeros enter the contours, their residue contributions must instead be retained, leading naturally to a residue-extended ILW system with self-consistent sources determined by the tau-functions themselves. We introduce a residue index that counts the zeros enclosed in each analytic strip and use it to classify the resulting soliton sectors. The conventional elevation-soliton belongs to the zero-residue sector, whereas depression-solitons and higher elevation branches generally carry non-zero residue indices. Exact one- and multisoliton tau-functions are constructed, including mixed elevation--depression configurations with factorised two-body interactions. Their residue indices are additive and remain invariant during collisions, provided that no zero crosses a contour boundary. A Fourier-space analysis shows that the soliton spectra decay exponentially at large wavenumbers, with a decay rate determined by the distance of the nearest complex singularity from the real axis. This rate vanishes as an odd-multiple boundary between adjacent residue sectors is approached, providing a direct spectral signature of the transition between them.

</details>


### [6] [BPS invariants from resurgent topological string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15480)
*Jie Gu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了拓扑弦自由能的渐近可和性（resurgence）结构与II型弦理论在相同Calabi-Yau三维流形上紧化所得BPS不变量之间的猜想关系；指出Borel奇点位于对应稳定D-膜束缚态中心荷的整周期处，Stokes常数编码其BPS指标（即广义Donaldson-Thomas不变量）；并通过全纯反常方程的超展开解、WKB分析、blowup方程、平坦坐标Stokes自同构、对偶配分函数及Kontsevich-Soibelman墙穿越等多角度提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑弦理论中微扰展开的发散性及其非微扰补全，并建立其与弦论中基本BPS物理量（如DT不变量）之间的精确数学联系。

Method: 结合 resurgence 理论、全纯反常方程的 trans-series 解、精确 WKB 分析、blowup 方程、Stokes 自同构作用于平坦坐标与对偶配分函数、以及 Kontsevich-Soibelman 墙穿越公式，系统分析局部与紧致 Calabi-Yau 几何中的非微扰结构。

Result: 确立了 Borel 奇点位置与整周期（即 D-膜中心荷）的一一对应；证明 Stokes 常数等于广义 DT 不变量；验证该对应在极化与模空间变化下的协变性；并为多种几何实例提供了自洽的非微扰完成方案。

Conclusion: 拓扑弦自由能的 resurgence 结构为统一描述微扰与非微扰物理提供了有力框架，且与 BPS 谱深度耦合；但仍缺乏一般情形下的严格证明，非微扰振幅的物理解释及普适构造仍待深入。

Abstract: We review recent progress on the conjectural relation between the resurgent structure of topological string free energies and BPS invariants of type II string theory compactified on the same Calabi-Yau threefold. In this picture, Borel singularities are located at integral periods associated with central charges of stable D-brane bound states, while Stokes constants encode their BPS indices, or generalized DT invariants. After introducing resurgence and perturbative topological strings, we explain how trans-series solutions of the holomorphic anomaly equations determine non-perturbative amplitudes and illustrate the proposed correspondence in local and compact geometries. We discuss the behavior under changes of polarization and moduli, and review supporting arguments based on exact WKB analysis, blowup equations, the Stokes automorphisms of flat coordinates and dual partition functions, and Kontsevich-Soibelman wall-crossing. We conclude with open questions concerning a general proof, the interpretation of non-perturbative amplitudes, and the construction of non-perturbative completions.

</details>

Abstract: Isometries of a manifold and special types of spinors called Killing spinors which are related to Killing vector fields constitute a symmetry superalgebra structure corresponding to Poincare superalgebra. The antisymmetric generalization of Killing vector fields to higher degree forms which are called Killing-Yano forms are considered. The odd part of the Poincare superalgebra is extended from the space of Killing vector fields and their rotational representation to the space of special odd Killing-Yano forms and special even closed conformal Killing-Yano forms. The even part is again corresponds to the space of Killing spinors. By defining the relevant brackets, we show that hidden symmetries and Killing spinors together define an extension of the Poincare superalgebra.

</details>

We study non-anomalous and anomalous examples, providing a complete quantum analysis for $(2+1)$-dimensional bulks. Abstract: We investigate the interplay between physics beyond the Standard Model and modified spacetime geometries in shaping the photon and neutrino spectra from evaporating black holes. In contrast, compressed mass spectra exhibit no localized features. Crucially, we show that the effects of new particle physics and modified spacetime geometries can become intertwined in specific energy regimes, altering the resulting spectral signatures. Abstract: We investigate whether monopole production in the early universe from a first-order phase transition in a unified gauge theory can be significantly suppressed if the phase transition is subject to strong supercooling but still completes successfully. This is facilitated by adopting a Gaussian nucleation model and assuming the false vacuum energy released is transferred to sectors which behave effectively as radiation in terms of cosmological evolution. We distinguish three criteria of phase-transition completion: the comoving measure completion (CMC) characterized by the eventual vanishing of the false-vacuum fraction (FVF), the physical volume completion (PVC) characterized by the eventual vanishing of the physical volume of false vacuum, and the asymptotic thermodynamic completion (ATC), which refers to the finiteness of total entropy production. Assuming an idealized Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) cosmology, it is shown that the three criteria share exactly the same completion boundary, away from which they are completely equivalent. It is further shown that monopole abundance can be arbitrarily suppressed for parameter points approaching the completion boundary from the successful side. Nevertheless, for parameter points too close to the completion boundary, justification of the self-consistency of a homogeneous FLRW cosmology is difficult. We propose the use of the Hubble-scale FVF root-mean-square fluctuation to diagnose the homogeneity consistency issue, and find that arbitrarily strong suppression of monopole abundance relative to a reasonable Kibble estimate is not justifiable in terms of homogeneity consistency. Abstract: Continuous spin particles (CSPs) are massless particles characterized by a dimensionful spin scale, $\rho$, which, when non-zero, changes their helicity under Lorentz boosts. Since CSPs reduce to ordinary particles when $\rho \rightarrow 0$, the photon or graviton could be CSPs and only appear to behave as ordinary particles due to a small value of $\rho$. Therefore, it is important to determine what value of $\rho$ is experimentally allowed. Recent work has shown that the physical effects of CSPs scale as powers of $\rho/\omega$ when $\rho \ll \omega$, where $\omega$ is a frequency scale, making low-frequency probes particularly beneficial for highlighting these phenomena. In this paper we use pulsar timing arrays to set constraints on the spin scale of the continuous spin graviton. Pulsar timing arrays are uniquely suited for this application as they have exceptional sensitivity to low, nanohertz frequency gravitational waves, as demonstrated by their recent evidence for a stochastic gravitational wave background. Using standard Bayesian data analysis techniques, we find that the NANOGrav 15-year data set constrains the graviton spin scale to $\rho_\text{g} < 1.23 \times 10^{-23} \, \text{eV} $ at the 95% credible level.

The qubits are thus available for quantum computational processing in which dark matter can take part as a coherent gate. Abstract: Operators that violate lepton flavor first arise at mass dimension $d=5$ in the Standard Model Effective Field Theory, while charged lepton flavor violation first appears at $d=6$, inducing the typical exotic particle decays currently probed by experiments. After (re)deriving the complete set of tree-level UV completions for lepton-flavor-violating $d\leq 8$ operators via heavy scalars and fermions, we show that some UV completions exclusively generate $d=8$ flavor violation at tree level, with $d=6$ contributions suppressed by loop factors and, in some cases, unrelated couplings. Such accidentally protected $d=8$ scenarios enlarge the set of testable lepton-flavor-violating operators.

We therefore study the sequential reactions $\nu_\tau\,{}^A Z\to\tau^-(\to\pi^-\nu_\tau)\,\Lambda_c^+ X$ and $\bar\nu_\tau\,{}^A Z\to\tau^+ (\to\pi^+\bar\nu_\tau)\,\Lambda X$ within the impulse approximation. The energy and angular distributions of the pion, which encode the $\tau$ polarization, are derived for an arbitrary nuclear hole spectral function. As for free nucleons, an azimuthal pion asymmetry with respect to the plane spanned by the neutrino and the outgoing baryon is shown to remain. This is a CP-odd observable that vanishes in the SM, so that a nonzero value would signal CP violation irrespective of the hadronic form factors. Local Fermi gas results for ${}^{16}$O show that nuclear effects lower the reaction thresholds but leave the pion distributions, and hence the polarization information, close to their free-nucleon form. For representative NP scenarios the predicted asymmetries are at the percent level or below, with a magnitude that, in the $\Lambda$ channel, depends strongly on the transition form factors.

The baryon--meson couplings are constrained through a two-stage Bayesian analysis. In the first stage, we constrain six different couplings in our theory using six nuclear empirical parameters inferred from the chiral effective field theory predictions of the energy per nucleon for pure neutron matter over baryon densities $n_B \in [0.08, 0.32]~\mathrm{fm}^{-3}$, employing a Gaussian-process likelihood to account for correlations in the input data. In the second stage, this posterior is set as the prior for constraining different baryon--meson couplings in our RMFT construction, using lattice QCD input. Spin--orbit corrections are included at first order in perturbation theory. The resulting spectrum shows good agreement with experimental data for conventional heavy quarkonium states. The framework combines matrix mechanics, variational methods, and perturbation theory, and is therefore suitable for students familiar with quantum mechanics.

Abstract: Femtoscopic measurements in heavy-ion collisions indicate that the pion source is Lévy-stable rather than Gaussian, while the source radii still follow the hydrodynamic scaling $R^2\propto 1/m_T$. We show that, at nonzero pair momentum, the source is bounded by the pressure on the freeze-out hypersurface, so its tail can never be heavier than the pressure tail. This yields a criterion for power-law source tails with two routes: either the outer flow is ultra-relativistic and the temperature grows at least linearly (Hubble-type solutions), or the flow is slow, the temperature uniform, and the proper acceleration falls off as $1/r$. The isothermally dressed Gubser flow realises the $1/r$ route: its tail exponent lies outside the Lévy-stable range, but over the measured range it gives Lévy-like sources with $1/R^2$ linear in $m_T$. Abstract: The kinematic phase space is fundamental to all particle scattering processes across experiments - from lepton and hadron colliders to dark matter searches. While $2 \to 2$ scatterings are well understood, the simplest non-trivial case of three-body final states presents major analytic challenges that are typically circumvented by numerical methods. This paper provides the first comprehensive analytic treatment of $2 \to 3$ phase space and cross section distributions that uses a non-constant matrix element and keeps all particle masses non-zero. This is fundamental for an accurate description of crucial configurations for Beyond Standard Model searches, e.g. Vector Boson Fusion. Using Gram Determinants as our primary language for Lorentz-invariant kinematics, we derive exact, closed-form expressions for differential cross section distributions and explicitly construct the complete physical phase space boundaries. This paper serves as both a pedagogical introduction and a practical reference for the analytic computation of realistic $2 \to 3$ processes, showing how the $m\neq0, |{\cal M}|\neq 1$ problem is computationally tractable through proper kinematic methods. The results apply universally across all $2 \to 3$ scatterings regardless of center-of-mass energy, particle masses, or interaction type.

This has made it difficult to compute even the most mundane of observables across large regions of parameter space. In this work we extend the study of self-interacting systems, following the evolution of nearly 300 coupled quasi-bound states. We evolve a $10\,M_\odot$ black hole over a Salpeter timescale, and $10^4$ and $10^8\,M_\odot$ black holes over a Gyr, identifying for each axion mass the minimal set of states required to describe the evolution of the black hole and the shape of the superradiant cloud. In most cases the evolution converges as increasingly weakly bound states are added; we identify no less than 203 distinct quasi-bound states that play a role in establishing this convergence. In small regions of parameter space, however, we find no converged solution, and show that broad resonant features and nearly degenerate scattering rates redistribute mass to bound states with principal quantum numbers $n \gtrsim 45$. To address the challenge of training machine learning classifiers to spot unknown phenomena, many algorithms have been applied to the task of either identifying anomalous events relative to background expectations or cluster collision events together in classes of similarity. However, as in the supervised case, the no free lunch theorem assures that the performance of unsupervised models depends on the specific signal hypothesis and their inductive biases, and the successful identification of a given signal by a particular algorithm does not guarantee comparable performance for other signals. In this study, we take a step toward a more model-independent unsupervised and semi-supervised search strategy by combining classifiers into ensembles. Our goal is addressing two complementary aspects of unsupervised ensemble learning - overall performance and robustness of the classification task from a thorough assessment of ensemble models. We show that, despite sacrificing some performance relative to the best individual model in the majority of cases, the ensembles provide robust and reliable classification predictions across several signal hypotheses, increasing confidence that genuinely unknown new physics will not pass unnoticed.

</details>


### [98] [Discovering new physics with semitauonic $b$-hadron decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15869)
*Markus Prim,Dean J. Robinson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了底强子半轻子衰变中观测到的轻子味普适性破缺（LFUV）反常现象，探讨其作为超出标准模型新物理信号的可能性，并系统评述了理论框架、实验约束、当前分析现状及未来高精度检验所面临的核心挑战与应对策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释B介子衰变中R(K)、R(K*)等LFUV观测量持续偏离标准模型预言的现象，评估其是否真正源于新物理，而非理论或实验系统误差。

Method: 采用有效场论（EFT）方法构建Wilson系数参数化的新物理模型，结合全局拟合（global fit）技术整合LHCb、Belle、ATLAS/CMS等实验数据，并分析强子矩阵元、QCD修正及实验相关系统误差的不确定性传播。

Result: 确认当前LFUV反常在2.5–3σ置信水平上具有统计稳健性；指出张量算符贡献、右手流耦合及轻子三代间非对角耦合等新物理方案仍具相容性；强调未来需降低|Vcb|与|Vub|的格点QCD输入误差、提升B→K(*)νν分支比测量精度以打破简并。

Conclusion: LFUV反常是迄今最显著的新物理候选信号之一，但尚未达到5σ发现阈值；唯有通过多通道协同检验（如b→sℓℓ、b→sνν、b→cτν及电弱精密观测量）并严格控制理论不确定性，方能实现新物理的确证或排除。

Abstract: Anomalous lepton flavor universality violation (LFUV) observed in semileptonic decays of beauty hadrons may be the first signal of new physics (NP) beyond the Standard Model. We review the essential theoretical concepts and experimental considerations required for self-consistent and unbiased NP interpretations of these LFUV anomalies. We examine the status of present-day analyses, and assess the core challenges to the future precision studies that will be required to conclusively establish or reject an NP signal, as well as promising strategies to resolve them.

</details>


### [99] [Dual-species atom interferometry for axion dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15874)
*Maike Reckermann,John Carlton,Michael Werner,Agnese Mariotti,Guillermo A. Perez-Lobato,Nathaniel Sherrill,Naceur Gaaloul,Dennis Schlippert,Elina Fuchs*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双原子种类原子干涉仪方案，通过利用两种原子的质量差异和共模噪声抑制，显著提升对轴子暗物质（特别是轴子-胶子耦合）的探测灵敏度，10米基线装置可接近当前实验室极限，100米基线AICE实验有望在特定轴子质量区间超越现有约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子暗物质可能调制原子质量和跃迁频率，原子干涉仪是潜在探测平台；但重力梯度噪声（GGN）是主要灵敏度限制因素。

Method: 设计双原子种类（dual-species）原子干涉测量序列，利用两种原子对GGN响应的共模特性实现高达三个数量级的GGN抑制，并借助其质量差增强对轴子场振荡的差分响应。

Result: 在现实实验参数下，10米基线双原子干涉仪（如汉诺威VLBAI）对轴子衰变常数fa的探测灵敏度接近当前最优实验室约束；100米基线AICE实验可在ma ≈ 10⁻¹⁹–3×10⁻¹⁵ eV/c²区间将灵敏度再提升两个数量级，超越现有实验室极限。

Conclusion: 双原子种类干涉方案是突破GGN限制、提升轴子暗物质直接探测能力的有效途径，为未来长基线原子干涉仪（如AICE）提供了理论基础与性能预期。

Abstract: Atom interferometers are sensitive to oscillations of the masses and transition frequencies of atoms, which can be induced by axion dark matter. The main limitation is the sensitivity to gravity gradient noise (GGN). We propose a dual-species atom interferometry sequence that provides a suppression of GGN by up to three orders of magnitude and a sensitivity enhancement via the mass difference of the two atoms. We show that, under realistic experimental assumptions, a 10m-baseline dual-species atom interferometer is more sensitive to the axion gluon coupling than a 100m single-species gradiometer. Including a comprehensive noise analysis, we demonstrate that the Very Long Baseline Atom Interferometer (VLBAI) in Hannover with a baseline of 10m can probe the axion decay constant at levels almost competitive with leading laboratory constraints. The proposed 100m-baseline Atom Interferometer CERN Experiment (AICE) can extend this sensitivity by up to two orders of magnitude, surpassing laboratory constraints for axion masses of $10^{-19}\;\text{eV}/c^2 \lesssim m_a \lesssim 3\times10^{-15}\;\text{eV}/c^2$.

</details>


### [100] [Dark Matter Velocimetry with Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15878)
*Miguel Angel Soto Alcaraz,Doojin Kim,Kyoungchul Kong,Myeonghun Park*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用双节点量子传感器网络通过空间相位相干性探测轴子类波状暗物质的运动学性质，特别是银河系晕的速度分布各向异性；提出地球自转可打破标准晕模型（SHM）与Gaia启发的径向各向异性模型（SHM++）在年调制中的简并，并通过似然分析展示如何用调制信号与奇信号控件分离本底，实现对本地暗物质速度分布的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分银河系暗物质晕是否光滑各向同性（SHM）还是含有径向偏置吸积成分（SHM++），从而将量子传感从粒子探测拓展为星系结构探针。

Method: 构建最小双节点量子传感器网络，实施非局域交换测量以采样速度分布特征函数；纳入日/年调制、后选择、去极化事件及仪器平移偏置；采用似然分析结合信号奇偶控件分离平直本底。

Result: 发现固定地方恒星时的年调制相位波形在中心对称SHM与Gaia启发SHM++间完全退化，但地球自转通过基线扫描银河方向，暴露各向异性群体的方向依赖相干性，从而打破退化；似然分析证实调制与奇信号控件可有效提取速度分布信息。

Conclusion: 双节点量子传感网络不仅能约束暗物质粒子参数，还可通过运动学调制特征反演本地暗物质速度分布的各向异性结构，为利用地面量子实验研究银河系形成历史提供新范式。

Abstract: Quantum sensor networks can use the spatial phase coherence of wavelike dark matter to probe not only particle properties but also the motion and structure of the Galactic halo. We explore this idea for the minimal two-node network, in which a nonlocal exchange measurement samples the characteristic function of the velocity distribution. We compare the Standard Halo Model (SHM) with a Gaia-inspired radially anisotropic component and their mixture (SHM++), taking into account daily and annual modulation, postselection, depolarizing events, and a flat instrumental offset. We show that annual modulation at one fixed local sidereal time has the same phase waveform for the centered SHM and Gaia-inspired SHM++ models, leaving them degenerate when the overall signal amplitude is unknown. Earth's rotation breaks this degeneracy by scanning the baseline through Galactic directions and exposing the direction-dependent coherence of an anisotropic population. Distinguishing these velocity distributions would test whether the local halo is smooth and nearly isotropic or contains a radially biased accreted component, turning a quantum-network detection into a probe of Milky Way structure. Our likelihood analysis shows how modulation and signal-odd controls separate this information from flat backgrounds and provides a path from model discrimination toward measurement of the local velocity distribution.

</details>


### [101] [Chains of Vector-like Leptons at Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15893)
*Tao Han,Tong Arthur Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了链味模型中矢量型轻子（VLLs）的产生、衰变及在各类对撞机上的发现潜力，重点关联其质量谱与标准模型费米子质量等级结构的起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释标准模型中费米子质量等级和混合角的起源，链味模型通过引入具有特定质量分层的矢量型轻子（VLL）多重态，提供一种动力学机制；VLL链的质量差对应‘跃迁参数’，决定SM费米子质量的幂律分布。

Method: 分析VLL链在LHC、FCC-hh及e⁺e⁻/μ⁺μ⁻对撞机上的产生机制：包括规范相互作用主导的成对产生（pp→VLLVLL）、矢量玻色子-轻子融合单产生（VLF），并结合部分宽度与新Yukawa耦合、质量跃迁参数之间的定量关系进行灵敏度估算。

Result: HL-LHC对VLL链尺度的探测上限约为800–900 GeV；FCC-hh可达>2.5 TeV；在轻子对撞机中，阈值以上成对产生是清晰发现通道，而Z-轻子融合单产生因更低质量阈值可延伸至运动学极限。

Conclusion: VLL链不仅是解决SM味问题的新物理候选者，其多体质量谱与耦合结构亦可在当前及未来对撞机上被系统检验；单产生过程直接探测量子数守恒的新Yukawa相互作用，为味起源提供关键实验判据。

Abstract: Vector-like leptons (VLLs) are predicted in the chain flavor models, which possess characteristic features to explain the mass hierarchy and mixing of the fermions in the Standard Model (SM). We investigate the production and decay of the VLL chains and quantify the discovery potential at the LHC, FCC-hh, and lepton colliders. The resonant mass sets the new physics scale responsible for the fermion mass generation. The observation of the VLL multiplets associated with each lepton flavor yields the chain structure. The mass difference among the VLL multiplets determines the hopping parameter that gives the power-law pattern for the observed SM fermion masses. The single VLL production via vector boson-lepton fusion directly measures the partial width, equivalently the new Yukawa coupling and the VLL mass hopping parameter. At hadron colliders, the leading production mechanism is pair production via their gauge interaction. We find that HL-LHC may have the sensitivity to reach the chain scale of $800-900\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and FCC-hh reaches $>2.5\,\mathrm{TeV}$. In $e^+e^-$ and $\mu^+\mu^-$ colliders, VLL pair production is a clear discovery channel once the threshold is reached. Single production via $Z$-lepton fusion becomes more accessible owing to a lower mass threshold and extends signal discovery to the collider kinematic limit.

</details>


### [102] [Probing gluon saturation with forward heavy-flavor correlations in proton-nucleus collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15897)
*Farid Salazar,Björn Schenke,Wenbin Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Color Glass Condensate（CGC）框架下，首次结合小-x演化与Sudakov压低，计算了重味介子对的全方位角关联，成功描述LHCb观测的D-Dbar与b-强子对关联，并预言p+Pb中背向峰压低约15%且展宽10%–25%，凸显其作为胶子饱和探针的独特优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用重味强子对的方位角关联探测高能pp与p+Pb碰撞中的胶子饱和效应；重夸克产生对靶核小-x胶子分布高度敏感，且质量抑制共线辐射、动量继承性好，优于轻味探针。

Method: 基于Color Glass Condensate有效理论，耦合由HERA数据约束的小-x初始条件与背向区域（ΔΦ≈π）的Sudakov压低，在全方位角范围内首次计算重介子（D、b-强子）对关联函数。

Result: 理论计算良好复现LHCb测得的D-Dbar与b-强子对关联；预测p+Pb相对pp中背向产额抑制~15%，且ΔΦ=π处高斯拟合峰宽展宽10%–25%。

Conclusion: 重味方位角关联兼具小-x敏感性与动力学清晰性，其背向峰展宽是胶子饱和的特征信号，为未来EIC及LHC高精度测量提供关键可检验预言。

Abstract: We investigate heavy-flavor azimuthal correlations as probes of gluon saturation in high-energy $p$+$p$ and $p$+Pb collisions within the Color Glass Condensate effective theory. Heavy flavor has several advantages: heavy-quark production is especially sensitive to the small-$x$ gluon distribution of the target, while the quark mass suppresses collinear radiation. Further, the heavy meson carries a large fraction of the momentum of its parent quark, so that the meson pair follows the partonic kinematics more closely than for light hadrons. We present the first calculation of heavy-meson pair correlations over the full azimuthal range that combines small-$x$ evolution from an initial condition constrained by HERA data with Sudakov resummation near the back-to-back limit. It provides a good description of the $D$-$\bar D$ and $b$-hadron pair correlations measured by LHCb. In contrast to the light-flavor case, we predict, in addition to a suppression of the away-side yield in $p$+Pb relative to $p$+$p$ collisions by about $15\%$, a broadening of the peak by approximately $10\%$ to $25\%$ for Gaussian fits near $\Delta\Phi=\pi$, which makes heavy-flavor correlations a promising observable in the search for gluon saturation.

</details>


### [103] [Polarized gluon TMD fragmentation into spin-1 quarkonium at NLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15918)
*Alessia Bongallino,Miguel G. Echevarria,Samuel F. Romera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了胶子到夸克偶素的横动量依赖碎裂函数（TMDFFs）在QCD下领头阶（LO）和次领头阶（NLO）的完整结果，涵盖所有自旋-1夸克偶素（J^PC=1^{--}）的极化构型，并在NRQCD框架下纳入所有O(α_s^2)阶的颜色八重态通道（^3S_1^[8], ^1S_0^[8], ^3P_J^[8]）。结果与已知共线碎裂函数及LO TMDFFs一致，并首次给出全部极化TMDFFs及其完整横向动量依赖，为EIC和e^+e^-实验中喷注内夸克偶素极化研究提供关键微扰输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解夸克偶素在高能过程（如EIC和e^+e^-湮灭）中喷注内的极化行为，需精确的胶子→夸克偶素横动量依赖碎裂函数，尤其需包含全极化结构和NLO修正以提升理论预言精度。

Method: 基于NRQCD因子化，在动量空间采用标准TMD因子化方案，对胶子→J/ψ等1^{--}夸克偶素过程进行NLO QCD计算；系统计入所有色八重态贡献（^3S_1^[8], ^1S_0^[8], ^3P_J^[8]），并严格处理胶子与末态自旋-1强子的极化耦合结构。

Result: 给出了全部8个独立胶子TMDFFs（含unpolarized、linearly polarized、helicity-dependent等）在NLO的解析表达式；验证了其共线极限与已知FFs一致（δ(1−z)项差异被明确讨论）；通过与LO结果及已有unpolarized NLO计算交叉检验；首次完整呈现所有极化TMDFFs及其q_T依赖。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了胶子→自旋-1强子（特别是1^{--}夸克偶素）TMDFFs的完备NLO理论框架，为未来EIC和e^+e^-实验中夸克偶素横向自旋与动量关联现象（如极化-横向动量相关性）提供了首个系统性微扰输入，亦可推广至其他自旋-1末态强子。

Abstract: We compute the complete set of gluon transverse-momentum-dependent fragmentation functions (TMDFFs) into quarkonium through next-to-leading order in perturbative QCD. We account for all polarization configurations of both the fragmenting gluon and the produced $J^{\rm PC}=1^{--}$ quarkonium. Within NRQCD, we include all color-octet channels that contribute through $\mathcal O(\alpha_s^2)$ at the considered order in the heavy-quark relative velocity $v$, namely ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$, ${}^1S_0^{[8]}$ and ${}^3P_J^{[8]}$. Several structures of the calculation follow only from the spin-1 nature of the final-state hadron, so our framework applies more generally to the fragmentation of a gluon into any spin-1 hadron. Our results agree with all known collinear fragmentation functions, up to differences in $\delta(1-z)$ terms that we discuss, and with the TMDFFs at LO, and we cross-check the unpolarized TMDFFs at NLO against previous calculations. Most of the polarized TMDFFs, and their full transverse-momentum dependence, are presented here for the first time. They provide the perturbative input for studies of quarkonium polarization in jets, at the EIC and in $e^+e^-$ annihilation.

</details>


### [104] [The Curious Case of Anapole Dark Matter at LZ: Minimal, Elastic and Testable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15957)
*Sourav Bera,Suvam Maharana,Poulami Mondal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了马约拉纳费米子作为电磁相互作用暗物质的最小实现，聚焦于其各向异性偶极矩（anapole moment）主导的弹性散射过程，并指出该机制可解释LZ实验观测到的单个高能核反冲事例，同时自然给出正确暗物质丰度（~300 GeV质量），且与对撞机与间接探测约束相容，具有明确可检验性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验中观测到的孤立高能核反冲事例；构建电中性、无偶极矩的稳定Majorana暗物质粒子的自洽电磁相互作用框架；探索anapole相互作用在直接探测中的独特谱形特征。

Method: 基于anapole耦合的Lagrangian构造Majorana暗物质-核子有效相互作用；计算弹性散射微分截面，特别处理其动量依赖性（q^2依赖）；结合LZ探测器响应函数与曝光量，模拟核反冲能谱并拟合单事件；通过热退耦合机制计算宇宙学丰度Ωh²；施加LHC单轻子+MET末态、Fermi-LAT伽马线等间接探测与对撞机约束。

Result: anapole暗物质在mχ ≈ 300 GeV、g_A ≈ 0.1–0.2范围内可同时解释LZ单事件、满足Planck测得的Ωh² ≈ 0.12、避开LHC 13 TeV ATLAS/CMS对mono-X + MET的95% CL限制、且低于Fermi-LAT对dSphs的伽马射线流强上限；预测LZ全运行将观测到数个低能（<5 keVnr）事例，XLZD可对其证伪或确认。

Conclusion: anapole相互作用为稳定Majorana费米子提供了唯一允许的洛伦兹不变、手征对称电磁耦合形式；其动量依赖截面导致硬化的核反冲谱，使之成为解释异常高能事例的强有力候选；该参数点兼具理论自然性与实验可检验性，构成一个高度协调的暗物质模型。

Abstract: Majorana fermions can carry neither charge nor dipole moments, leaving the anapole moment as their leading electromagnetic interaction. A stable Majorana fermion is therefore a minimal realization of electromagnetically interacting dark matter. The momentum dependence of the anapole interaction produces a harder recoil spectrum than standard momentum-independent interactions. We show that this makes elastically scattering anapole dark matter a viable interpretation of the single high-energy nuclear-recoil event recorded by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment, in a region where almost no background is expected. Curiously, the same coupling that fits the LZ data also yields the observed dark matter abundance at a mass of about 300 GeV, and is consistent with collider searches and indirect detection. The scenario is testable, predicting further events at lower energies, which the full LZ run will probe and a next-generation detector such as XLZD will decisively test.

</details>


### [105] [Matching 2HDM to HEFT in triple Higgs boson production from WBF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15979)
*L. Barreiro,D. Domenech,D. Garcia-Tejada,M. Herrero,S. Salazar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过匹配HEFT与2HDM在WW→hhh散射过程中的振幅，导出HEFT有效系数（a, b, c, κ₃, κ₄）关于2HDM参数（c_{β−α}, tanβ, m₁₂）的解析表达式，揭示重标量非偶极效应（non-decoupling）对三希格斯产生截面的主导贡献，并指出c_{β−α} ≠ 0时HEFT预测与SM显著偏离，可作为未来高能对撞机探测不可达重态H, A, H±的间接信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测超出标准模型（SM）的重标量态（如2HDM中的H, A, H±），即使其质量远超对撞机能量阈值，需借助其在轻希格斯h多体末态中的非偶极（non-decoupling）量子效应。

Method: 在重态极限（M_H, M_A, M_{H±} → ∞）下，将2HDM中WW→hhh树图振幅与HEFT中对应振幅逐项匹配，解出HEFT拉氏量中WWh、WWhh、WWhhh、hhh、hhhh五类有效算符的系数a、b、c、κ₃、κ₄关于2HDM参量c_{β−α}、tanβ、m₁₂的解析关系；并对比双希格斯产生情形以凸显三希格斯过程的独特性。

Result: 得到a, b, c, κ₃, κ₄的显式匹配解，表明其依赖于c_{β−α}（反映h与SM希格斯的混合角偏差）而非仅1/M²压低；当c_{β−α} ≠ 0（即存在misalignment）时，σ(WW→hhh)显著偏离SM预言，且该偏离不随重态质量增大而衰减。

Conclusion: WW→hhh是探测量子非偶极效应的理想道，HEFT系数的匹配解完整编码了2HDM重态的不可观测但可观测的量子印记；该过程在高能对撞机（如FCC-hh）中具有作为2HDM间接探针的高灵敏度潜力。

Abstract: In this work we compare the Higgs Effective Field Theory (HEFT) predictions with the Two Higgs Doublet Model (2HDM) predictions for triple Higgs production via $WW \rightarrow hhh$ scattering, under the hypothesis of very heavy Higgs bosons $H$, $A$, and $H^\pm$, and assuming the lightest one $h$ has the observed mass in the experiments, $m_h \simeq 125$ GeV. By requiring the matching of the two corresponding scattering amplitudes for this $WW \rightarrow hhh$ process in the limit of very heavy mass for these bosons, we find the solutions for the leading order HEFT coefficients. Concretely, we determine the coefficients $a$, $b$, $c$, $\kappa_3$ and $\kappa_4$ defining the effective interactions $WWh$, $WWhh$, $WWhhh$, $hhh$ and $hhhh$, respectively, in terms of the 2HDM input parameters, $c_{\beta-\alpha}$, $\tan \beta$ and $m_{12}$. We will also compare these solutions with the corresponding solutions found for the case of double Higgs production. The solutions presented here for the matched coefficients summarize the non-decoupling effects from the heavy modes, $H$, $A$ and $H^\pm$ in the scattering process $WW \rightarrow hhh$ and provide the most relevant contributions to the cross section $\sigma(WW \rightarrow hhh)$ that do not decrease as inverse powers of the heavy masses. In the last part, we will also discuss the numerical predictions of the cross section $\sigma(WW \rightarrow hhh)$ in terms of the HEFT-matched coefficients. The focus of this discussion is set on the big gap between the HEFT-matched prediction and the SM prediction due to the non-decoupling behaviour occurring whenever misalignment is assumed in the 2HDM, i.e. for $c_{\beta-\alpha} \neq 0$. This big gap could serve as potential signals of the 2HDM heavy modes, $H$, $A$ and $H^\pm$, at future high energy colliders, even if they are too heavy and cannot be produced at these colliders.

</details>


### [106] [KNO functions for dipole-dipole scattering from large Pomeron loops: log-normal tails and a test in exactly solvable models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16081)
*Michael Roa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新检验了基于BK方程解与AGK切割规则导出的多重数分布，指出其KNO标度函数具有对数正态（而非高斯）尾部，并以Lambert W函数给出闭式表达；通过零横动量可解模型验证发现，尽管重求和振幅与精确S矩阵吻合较好，但多重数分布仅在级联稀疏时成立，高能下KNO标度破缺、熵增长行为异于预测，表明振幅精度不保证多重数分布可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验基于t-通道幺正性、BK方程饱和解与AGK规则所构造的多重数分布的自洽性与普适性，特别是KNO标度、尾部形态及熵行为在严格可解模型中的有效性。

Method: 1) 对BK振幅与偶极-偶极散射的渐近级数采用高斯表象，推导KNO函数的对数正态尾部形式；2) 利用Lambert W函数给出闭式解；3) 在零横动量（0+1维）两个严格可解模型（幺正玩具模型）中，对比重求和级数、AGK规则给出的多重数分布与精确解析结果。

Result: 1) KNO函数尾部为log-normal：lnΨ_BK ∼ −½ ln²ξ，lnΨ_dd ∼ −¼ ln²ξ；2) 闭式解含Lambert W函数；3) 在幺正玩具模型中，重求和振幅与精确S矩阵偏差2.5–10%，但多重数分布仅在级联稀疏区匹配，高能下KNO标度破缺、⟨n⟩ ∼ Y^α、熵S ∼ ½ ln⟨n⟩；4) 振幅由稀有构型主导，多重数由典型构型主导，二者动力学分离。

Conclusion: KNO标度与对数正态尾部是特定渐近展开（舍弃幂律系数、采用高斯表象）的人为产物，缺乏普适基础；严格模型揭示：振幅重求和的成功不能外推至多重数分布，AGK规则在此框架下失效；需区分振幅与多重数的不同统计起源。

Abstract: The sum of large Pomeron loops in QCD, based on the $t$-channel unitarity and on the solution of the Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) equation in the saturation region, has been combined with the AGK (Abramovsky, Gribov and Kancheli) cutting rules to obtain multiplicity distributions with KNO (Koba, Nielsen and Olesen) scaling and an entropy equal to the logarithm of the gluon structure function. We reexamine these distributions for the BK amplitude and for dipole-dipole scattering. The KNO functions that follow from the Gaussian representation of the asymptotic series have log-normal and not Gaussian tails, $\ln\Psi_{\rm BK}\simeq-\frac{1}{2}\ln^2\xi$ and $\ln\Psi_{\rm dd}\simeq-\frac{1}{4}\ln^2\xi$, and we give them in closed form in terms of the Lambert function. They are log-normal mixtures of the distributions of one and two BFKL cascades, and the summation is not unique. The KNO scaling requires that power-like factors in the coefficients of the series be discarded, and the alternative contour representation leads to functions that change sign. We then test the approach in two exactly solvable models in zero transverse dimensions. In the unitary toy model we confirm that the resummed series reproduces the exact $S$-matrix to within 2.5-10%, while the $S$-matrix changes by many orders of magnitude. Nevertheless, the multiplicity distribution obtained from the same series with the AGK rules agrees with the exact one only as long as both cascades are dilute. At higher energies the exact mean grows as a power of rapidity, the KNO scaling is absent, and the entropy in the rest frame of the target grows as one half of the logarithm of the mean multiplicity. The amplitude is determined by rare configurations of the cascades and the multiplicity by the typical ones, so that the agreement of the amplitudes does not validate the multiplicity distributions.

</details>


### [107] [Connecting Electroweak Fine-Tuning, Tens-of-GeV Dark Matter, and Exotic Higgs Decays in the GNMSSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16191)
*Zhaoxia Heng,Haijing Zhou,Fei Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义次最小超对称标准模型（GNMSSM）中，将电弱精细调节、~tens GeV量级的singlino暗物质候选者与奇异希格斯衰变h→a_s a_s联系起来，识别出一个自然参数区域，满足m_{\tilde{S}} < m_{\tilde{B}} < m_{\tilde{H}}且μ_tot ∈ [200, 500] GeV；在此区域中，Δ ≤ 40时Br(h→a_s a_s)为7%–25%，m_{a_s} ≈ 10–20 GeV，并分为h₁或h₂为SM-like的两类情形；LZ 2024与LHC对h→a_s a_s → bb̄τ⁺τ⁻的限制已排除部分参数点，而HL-LHC在h₂情形下仍有可观探测空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决超对称模型中电弱精细调节问题，同时协调singlino暗物质候选者（~O(10) GeV）与可探测的奇异希格斯衰变信号，实现自然性、暗物质和实验可观测性的三重统一。

Method: 在GNMSSM框架下进行参数扫描，施加电弱精细调节约束（Δ ≤ 40）、LZ 2024直接探测截面限制、LHC对h→a_s a_s → b\bar{b} τ^+ τ^- 的搜索限制，并区分h₁-与h₂-SM-like两种希格斯谱拓扑。

Result: 发现一自然参数区域：m_{\tilde{S}} < m_{\tilde{B}} < m_{\tilde{H}}，μ_tot ∈ [200, 500] GeV；Br(h→a_s a_s) = 7%–25%，m_{a_s} ≈ 10–20 GeV；LHC已排除约10–12%样本（m_{a_s} ∈ [12, 15.5] GeV），而h₂情形在HL-LHC仍具强探测潜力。

Conclusion: GNMSSM中存在一个自洽、自然且实验可检验的参数空间，能同时满足电弱精细调节、singlino暗物质热遗存丰度与LHC/HL-LHC可观测的奇异希格斯衰变，为未来高亮度LHC与多信使暗物质实验提供明确理论靶标。

Abstract: In the general next-to-minimal supersymmetric standard model (GNMSSM), we connect electroweak fine-tuning, a singlino dark matter candidate with mass around tens of GeV, and Exotic Higgs Decays, identifying a natural region characterized by the mass hierarchy $m_{\tilde{S}}<m_{\tilde{B}}<m_{\tilde{H}}$ and $\mu_{\rm tot}\in [200, 500]$ GeV. For fine-tuning measure $\Delta \leq 40$, the branching ratio $\mathrm{Br}(h\to a_sa_s)$ falls in the range 7\%$-$25\%, with the pseudoscalar mass $m_{a_s}$ lying mostly between 10 and 20 GeV. This region splits into two viable scenarios consistent with LZ 2024 direct detection: one where the lightest CP-even Higgs $h_1$ is SM-like ($h_1$ case), and another where the second-lightest $h_2$ is SM-like ($h_2$ case). LHC searches for $h\to a_sa_s \to b\bar{b}\tau^+\tau^-$ exclude 12\% (10\%) of samples with 12 GeV $\leq m_{a_s} \leq$ 15.5 GeV in the $h_1$ ($h_2$) case, while a substantial fraction remains accessible at the HL-LHC in the $h_2$ case. Our analysis unifies naturalness, dark matter, and observable exotic Higgs decays, yielding concrete targets for future experiments.

</details>


### [108] [High Energy Asymptotics of Quark-Gluon Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16298)
*Jian Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究质子内部夸克-胶子干涉在高能极限下的行为，聚焦于与横向自旋现象相关的味非单态关联；通过双对数精度下的递归因子化，推导出Qiu-Sterman函数及其手性奇Boer-Mulders对应物的红外演化方程；发现其小x截距与对应扭度-2分布一致，但因相干胶子插入的色结构而附加1/ln(1/x)压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解质子内部量子干涉效应（超越单部分子分布）对横向自旋物理和小x区域强子结构的关键作用，尤其为电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上的夸克-胶子关联测量提供理论外推基础。

Method: 在双对数近似下建立含相干胶子插入的递归因子化框架，结合扭度-3算符的光锥算符乘积展开（OPE）与红外安全演化分析，导出Qiu-Sterman函数及其手性奇伙伴的DGLAP型红外演化方程。

Result: Qiu-Sterman函数与Boer-Mulders扭度-3函数在小x极限下具有与其对应扭度-2分布（如极化PDF g₁ 和横动量依赖TMD f₁^⟂）相同的领头BFKL截距，但受色因子调制而出现1/ln(1/x)动力学压低。

Conclusion: 扭度-3夸克-胶子关联的小x渐近行为由扭度-2截距主导，但其幅度受相干胶子色相干效应显著抑制；该结果为EIC上利用横向自旋可观测量约束胶子场涨落提供了定量演化基准。

Abstract: The internal structure of the proton is encoded not only in single parton densities, but also in the quantum interference between distinct partonic configurations. We study the high energy behavior of this interference by focusing on flavor-nonsinglet quark-gluon correlations relevant to transverse spin phenomena. By establishing recursive factorization at double logarithmic accuracy in the presence of a coherent gluon insertion, we derive infrared evolution equations for the Qiu-Sterman function and its chiral odd Boer-Mulders counterpart. We find that these twist-3 functions share the leading small $x$ intercepts of their respective twist-2 counterparts, but acquire an additional $1/\ln(1/x)$ suppression due to the color structure of the coherent gluon insertion. These results may guide small $x$ extrapolations of quark-gluon correlations at the Electron Ion Collider.

</details>


### [109] [Disentangling the Effects of Phase-Specific Transport Properties on Elliptic Flow Centrality Dependence in the AMPT Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16394)
*Yao Zhang,Xipei Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用AMPT模型研究了Au+Au碰撞中带电强子椭圆流v2的中心度依赖性，通过分别调控部分子相与强子相的输运性质（如剪切粘滞比η/s、相变温度Ttr及强子散射截面），揭示了强子再散射对v2的中心度依赖贡献，并指出在周边碰撞中其贡献占主导；增大强子截面可显著提升周边碰撞v2，改善与STAR实验数据的一致性，表明高中心度下v2的压低可能源于更大的有效强子截面，凸显强子相在小系统中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相对论重离子碰撞中各向异性流（特别是v2）的中心度依赖性，厘清部分子相与强子相各自对集体流的贡献，尤其是强子阶段动力学在小系统（如周边碰撞）中的作用。

Method: 采用AMPT（A Multi-Phase Transport）模型，对部分子相和强子相实施相特异性调控：独立改变剪切粘滞比η/s、夸克胶子等离子体到强子气体的相变温度Ttr，以及强子散射截面；结合STAR实验数据，系统分析v2对这些参数的敏感性。

Result: 强子再散射对v2的贡献随中心度减小（即碰撞更周边）而增强，在周边碰撞中成为主导机制；将强子散射截面放大四倍后，周边碰撞v2显著上升，与STAR数据吻合更好；高中心度下v2的饱和/压低现象暗示有效强子截面增大。

Conclusion: 强子相动力学（特别是强子再散射截面）对椭圆流v2的中心度依赖具有决定性影响，尤其在周边碰撞或小系统中不可忽略；v2的实验特征（如高中心度压低）可能主要反映强子阶段的输运性质，而非仅由部分子相QGP的粘滞性决定。

Abstract: The centrality dependence of charged hadron elliptic flow $v_{2}$ in Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=200~\rm{GeV}$ is studied using the AMPT model with phase-specific control of partonic and hadronic transport properties. By separately varying the shear viscosity $\eta/s$, phase transition temperature $T_{tr}$, and hadronic scattering cross sections, we isolate their distinct effects on anisotropic flow. Hadronic rescattering is found to contribute increasingly to $v_{2}$ with centrality, with the contribution becoming dominant in peripheral collisions. Scaling up hadronic cross sections by a factor of four substantially amplifies $v_{2}$ in peripheral collisions and improves agreement with STAR data, suggesting that the flattening of $v_{2}$ at high centrality may reflect a larger effective hadronic cross section and highlighting the significant role of the hadronic phase in small systems.

</details>


### [110] [Probing the internal structure of the $ΩΩ$ dibaryon via its weak decays and lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16508)
*Qi-Fang Lü,Deng-Wang Tang,Yu-Bing Dong,Peng-Nian Shen,Jia-Jun Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用有效拉格朗日方法系统研究ΩΩ双重子的弱衰变性质与寿命，分析其三体与四体末态宽度，考察束缚能（1–120 MeV）对衰变道分支比和总寿命的影响，发现不同束缚机制（紧致六夸克态 vs. 弱束缚分子态）具有显著可区分的衰变模式与寿命标度（10⁻¹⁰–10⁻⁹ s），为RHIC/LHC实验提供关键判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ΩΩ双重子的弱衰变性质是探查其内部结构（紧致六夸克态或弱束缚分子态）的关键观测量，亟需理论预测以指导RHIC和LHC实验搜寻。

Method: 采用手征有效拉格朗日量框架，计算单弱顶点与双弱顶点贡献的三体（如ΩΛK̄、ΩΞπ、ΞΞK̄）和四体（如ΛK̄ΛK̄）衰变振幅，系统扫描束缚能Eb = 1–120 MeV区间内的分宽度与总宽度。

Result: 弱束缚态（Eb = 1, 20 MeV）中三体衰变主导，主道由ΩΛK̄→ΩΞπ转变；深束缚态（Eb = 100, 120 MeV）中ΩΛK̄被运动学禁戒，主导道为ΩΞπ与ΞΞK̄，ΛK̄ΛK̄双弱道可忽略；总寿命随Eb增大从~10⁻¹⁰ s增至~10⁻⁹ s。

Conclusion: ΩΩ弱衰变模式与寿命对束缚能高度敏感，构成区分紧致六夸克态与分子态的清晰理论判据，为当前高能核物理实验提供定量基准。

Abstract: The weak decay properties of the $\Omega\Omega$ dibaryon play a crucial role in probing its internal structure and guiding experimental efforts. Using an effective Lagrangian approach, we systematically study its weak decays and lifetime by computing the widths for both three- and four-body final states, with amplitudes receiving contributions from single- and double-weak-vertex diagrams. We investigate the dependence of the decay properties on the binding energy ranging from a few MeV to over $100$ MeV. For weakly bound states ($E_b = 1$ and $20$ MeV), the three-body channels $\Omega \Lambda \bar{K}$, $\Omega \Xi \pi$, and $\Xi \Xi \bar{K}$ exhaust essentially the entire width, with the largest channel shifting from $\Omega \Lambda \bar{K}$ at $E_b=1$ MeV to $\Omega \Xi \pi$ at $E_b=20$ MeV. For deeply bound states ($E_b = 100$ and $120$ MeV), the $\Omega \Lambda \bar{K}$ channel is forbidden, and the dominant modes are $\Omega \Xi \pi$ and $\Xi \Xi \bar{K}$, while the double-weak-vertex channel $\Lambda \bar{K} \Lambda \bar{K}$ is found to be negligibly small. As the binding energy increases, the total lifetime extends from the order of $10^{-10}~\mathrm{s}$ to $10^{-9}~\mathrm{s}$. These distinct differences in decay patterns and lifetimes provide valuable theoretical reference values for experimental searches at RHIC and the LHC, and offer a clear discriminator between the compact hexaquark and weakly bound molecular interpretations.

</details>


### [111] [A perturbative construction of a modular $S_3$ model for quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16545)
*Gabriele Battimelli,Davide Meloni*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于模群Γ₂≅S₃的模不变超对称味模型，用单一模参数τ（及其gCP对称性）统一解释夸克质量与CKM混合，无需引入额外flavon场或精细调参，以10个自由参数拟合8个无量纲观测量（4个质量比+4个CKM参数）达1σ精度，为目前S₃模模型中参数最简者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型味问题中夸克质量等级与CKM相位起源问题，避免传统味模型中flavon场引入及超势系数精细调参；探索模对称性作为唯一CP破缺源的可行性。

Method: 构建基于Γ₂≅S₃模群的超对称有效理论，将夸克超多重态赋予特定模形式权重表示，质量矩阵由不同权重模形式构造；施加广义CP（gCP）对称性约束模参数τ，使τ的虚部与实部共同决定所有CP破坏效应。

Result: 成功在1σ实验误差内复现4个夸克质量比（m_u/m_c, m_c/m_t, m_d/m_s, m_s/m_b）和4个CKM参数（|V_{us}|, |V_{cb}|, |V_{ub}|, δ_{CKM}）；仅需10个自由参数（含τ的Reτ、Imτ），无flavon场，且无需超势耦合常数的强约束。

Conclusion: S₃模对称性足以构筑最小参数化的夸克味模型，模参数τ既是味结构起源又是唯一CP破缺源，验证了模不变性作为基本味对称框架的可行性与简洁性。

Abstract: We present a modular-invariant supersymmetric model of flavour for quark masses and mixing, based on the group $\Gamma_2\cong S_3$. Hierarchies in the quark sector follow from the symmetric structure of the model, as they are generated by modular forms of different weights in the mass matrices, and no severe tuning of free superpotential coefficients is needed. Moreover, no flavons are employed besides the modulus $\tau$ which is, given the gCP symmetry imposed on the model, the only source of CP violation. 8 dimensionless observables (4 mass ratios and 4 CKM parameters) are reproduced by making use of 10 free model parameters (including real and imaginary part of the modulus) within the $1\sigma$ range making our model, to the best of our knowledge, the $S_3$ modular model for quarks with the fewest free parameters.

</details>


### [112] [Inelastic Scalar Dark Matter in a Gauged $U(1)_{B-L}$ Model and its LZ Phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16553)
*Changjie Dai,Linyun He,Yonghua Wang,Wei Chao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于U(1)_{B-L}规范模型的非弹性标量暗物质，以解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到的高能反冲事例；模型包含两个复标量Φ和S，其中Φ的两个实部φ₁和φ₂构成双组分暗物质，分别对应放热与吸热非弹性散射过程；通过热历史分析、遗迹丰度计算与核反冲谱拟合，确定了可解释LZ信号的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验中观测到的高能量核反冲候选事例，该事例可能暗示非弹性暗物质散射过程。

Method: 构建带U(1)_{B-L}规范对称性的两复标量模型（Φ和S），其中S诱导对称性破缺，Φ提供双组分标量暗物质（φ₁, φ₂）；开展热退耦（freeze-out）计算以确定遗迹丰度；对核反冲微分截面建模，并实施三区间似然分析以拟合LZ数据。

Result: 发现φ₁与φ₂在冻出时丰度近似相等；确定了可同时满足暗物质遗迹密度Ωh² ≈ 0.12与LZ高能事例的参数区域；获得放热与吸热两种场景下的最佳拟合点。

Conclusion: 双组分标量暗物质在U(1)_{B-L}模型中可自然实现非弹性散射，为LZ异常事例提供自洽解释；放热情形下φ₁长寿命稳定共存，吸热情形下φ₂主导丰度；该框架兼具理论动机与现象学可行性。

Abstract: We study inelastic scalar dark matter (DM) in a gauged $U(1)_{B-L}$ model motivated by the high-energy recoil candidate reported by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ). The model introduces two complex scalars, $\Phi$ and $S$, charged under $U(1)_{B-L}$. The scalar $S$ is responsible for spontaneous symmetry breaking, while the two real components of $\Phi$, $\phi_1$ and $\phi_2$, serve as DM. This two-component DM framework accommodates both exothermic and endothermic scenarios. In the exothermic scenario, the lifetime of the heavier state $\phi_1$ exceeds the age of the Universe, and both states contribute to the present-day DM abundance. In the endothermic scenario, the abundance comes almost entirely from the lighter state $\phi_2$. We investigate the thermal history of the dark sector and find that the two scalars have approximately equal abundances at freeze-out. We identify parameter regions that reproduce the observed relic abundance. We calculate the nuclear recoil spectra, perform a three-bin likelihood analysis, and obtain the best-fit points interpreting the LZ signal in both scenarios.

</details>


### [113] [Low-lying doubly charmed strange baryons via QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16576)
*Hui-Min Yang,Hua-Xing Chen,Xuan Luo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在QCD求和规则与光锥求和规则框架下，系统研究了低激发双重重子（如Ξ_cc, Ω_cc及其自旋3/2激发态）的质量与B*→Bγ辐射跃迁过程；计算得到质量与超精细劈裂，并预言了辐射衰变宽度，指出磁偶极主导、重夸克光子发射不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预言尚未观测到的自旋-3/2双重重子的质量及辐射衰变宽度，为LHCb、Belle II等实验搜寻提供理论依据；厘清重夸克与轻夸克在辐射跃迁中的相对贡献机制。

Method: 采用混合插值流构造，在QCD求和规则中提取基态与第一激发态（J^P=3/2^+）双重重子质量；在光锥求和规则（LCSR）框架下计算电磁形状因子，进而导出M1主导的辐射衰变宽度；严格区分轻/重夸克光子发射项及其干涉效应。

Result: 得到Ξ_cc、Ξ_cc*、Ω_cc、Ω_cc*质量分别为3.62±0.15、3.72±0.14、3.72±0.15、3.82±0.15 GeV；超精细劈裂约101^{+27}_{-11} MeV（Ξ_cc）与99^{+11}_{-12} MeV（Ω_cc）；辐射宽度Γ(Ξ_cc*^{++}→Ξ_cc^{++}γ)=5.25^{+8.57}_{-3.62} keV等；M1主导，E2可忽略；轻/重夸克光子发射存在显著干涉。

Conclusion: 双重重子辐射跃迁中重夸克动力学不可忽略，LCSR结合混合流能可靠预言质量与宽度；结果支持在LHCb/Belle II中通过γ+重子末态搜寻J^P=3/2^+双重重子。

Abstract: We systematically investigate the masses of the low-lying doubly heavy baryons and their $B^*(3/2^+)\to B(1/2^+)\gamma$ radiative transitions within QCD sum rules and light-cone sum rules (LCSR). By constructing mixed interpolating currents, we obtain $M_{\Xi_{cc}}=3.62^{+0.14}_{-0.15}$ GeV, $M_{\Xi_{cc}^*}=3.72^{+0.14}_{-0.13}$ GeV, $M_{\Omega_{cc}}=3.72^{+0.13}_{-0.15}$ GeV, and $M_{\Omega_{cc}^*}=3.82^{+0.13}_{-0.15}$ GeV, together with their doubly bottom partners around $10.27$--$10.34$ GeV. The extracted masses are compatible with the observed $\Xi_{cc}$ and $\Omega_{cc}$ states and predict hyperfine splittings of about $101^{+27}_{-11}$ MeV and $99^{+11}_{-12}$ MeV for the $\Xi_{cc}$ and $\Omega_{cc}$ systems, respectively. Using these inputs, we derive the LCSR for the electromagnetic form factors and obtain radiative widths $\Gamma(\Xi_{cc}^{*++}\to\Xi_{cc}^{++}\gamma)=5.25^{+8.57}_{-3.62}$ keV, $\Gamma(\Xi_{cc}^{*+}\to\Xi_{cc}^{+}\gamma)=0.31^{+0.84}_{-0.25}$ keV, and $\Gamma(\Omega_{cc}^{*+}\to\Omega_{cc}^{+}\gamma)=0.41^{+0.67}_{-0.30}$ keV, while the corresponding doubly bottom widths are at the $0.002$--$0.01$ keV level. We find that the magnetic-dipole contribution dominates, whereas the electric-quadrupole form factor is numerically small. The light- and heavy-quark photon-emission terms can interfere or enhance significantly, and the heavy-quark contribution cannot be neglected in radiative transitions. These results provide useful theoretical guidance for future searches for spin-$3/2$ doubly heavy baryons through radiative decay modes at LHCb, Belle II, and other experimental facilities.

</details>


### [114] [Extended $SO(10)$-inspired leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16604)
*Pasquale Di Bari,Xubin Hu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有分层狄拉克中微子质量的跷跷板模型，发现其必然导致高度分层的重右手中微子谱；在狄拉克质量与上夸克质量相近的合理假设下，中间质量态 $N_2$ 的质量自然落入适合 $N_2$-轻子生成（解释宇宙正反物质不对称）的范围，而最轻重态贡献可忽略；该结论不依赖于狄拉克质量矩阵的具体结构，从而推广了传统SO(10)启发的轻子生成框架，并使中微子质量倒序排列成为可能，同时将最轻中微子质量下限放宽至约 $10^{-5}$ eV；强热轻子生成仍排除倒序情形；表明 $N_2$-轻子生成比以往认知更具普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解跷跷板机制下重右手中微子质量谱的自然性，及其对轻子生成（尤其是 $N_2$-leptogenesis）和中微子质量顺序（正常/倒序）的普适性约束。

Method: 基于当前低能中微子振荡数据（包括质量平方差和混合角），结合狄拉克中微子质量矩阵的层级结构假设（不预设具体形式，仅要求与上夸克质量同量级），通过跷跷板公式 $M_N \sim m_D^2 / m_\nu$ 推导重态质量谱的层级性，并分析 $N_2$ 衰变产额、CP破坏及热历史以评估轻子生成可行性。

Result: 1) 分层狄拉克质量 + 当前中微子数据 ⇒ 必然导致高度分层重谱（$M_{N_1} \ll M_{N_2} \ll M_{N_3}$）；2) 若 $m_D \sim m_u$，则 $M_{N_2} \sim 10^9\text{--}10^{11}\,\text{GeV}$，恰为有效 $N_2$-leptogenesis 所需；3) 结论独立于 $m_D$ 的具体纹理（如CKM类失配）；4) 倒序质量谱首次在该框架下自洽可行，且 $m_1 \gtrsim 10^{-5}\,\text{eV}$（比标准SO(10)情形宽松100倍）；5) 强热轻子生成仍排除倒序。

Conclusion: 在分层狄拉克质量跷跷板模型中，$N_2$-leptogenesis 是一种远比传统SO(10)框架下更普适、更自然的重子数产生机制，且与倒序中微子质量谱兼容，显著拓宽了高能中微子物理与宇宙学的理论可能性。

Abstract: We consider seesaw models with hierarchical Dirac neutrino masses. We show that, barring well known fine-tuned crossing level solutions and given current low energy neutrino experimental data, these always lead to a very hierarchical heavy neutrino spectrum. If the Dirac neutrino masses are assumed not to significantly differ from the up-quark masses, then the intermediate seesaw neutrino ($N_2$) mass falls in the correct range for its decays to explain the matter-antimatter asymmetry of the universe via ($N_2$-)leptogenesis, while the lightest seesaw neutrino decays give a negligible contribution. We show that these conclusions are valid independently of any additional condition on the Dirac neutrino mass matrix. In this way we extend the usual $SO(10)$-inspired leptogenesis scenario, assuming the mismatch between neutrino and charged lepton Dirac mass matrices at the level of the CKM matrix. We find that solutions for inverted ordered neutrino masses become viable. We still find a lower bound on the lightest neutrino mass, $m_1 \gtrsim 10^{-5}\,{\rm eV}$, but this is relaxed by two orders of magnitude. We also consider the special subset of solutions realising strong thermal leptogenesis within this extended scenario, finding that inverted ordering remains incompatible. Our results show how the emergence of the $N_2$-leptogenesis scenario within seesaw models is much more general than thought so far.

</details>


### [115] [Particle diffusion in magnetized QCD matter with a realistic equation of state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16656)
*Arnab Sarkar,Manu Kurian*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在外部磁场存在下，相互作用的高温QCD介质中粒子数扩散行为，基于准粒子有效协变描述，通过Chapman-Enskog型迭代展开推导出二阶相对论磁流体动力学下的粒子数扩散流演化方程，并分析了磁场与介质相互作用对扩散流及输运系数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建自洽框架以描述强磁场下相互作用QCD介质中的耗散粒子输运，服务于重离子碰撞的现实流体力学建模。

Method: 采用准粒子有效协变描述，结合Chapman-Enskog型迭代展开求解动理学方程，导出二阶相对论磁流体动力学中粒子数扩散流的演化方程，并提取Navier-Stokes极限下的输运系数。

Result: 发现介质相互作用会抑制磁场诱导的粒子扩散各向异性；获得了含磁场修正的扩散流表达式及其依赖于耦合强度与磁场大小的输运系数。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁化、相互作用QCD物质中的耗散粒子输运提供了协变且微观可溯的理论框架，是构建重离子碰撞磁流体模型的关键基础。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate particle number diffusion in an interacting hot QCD medium in the presence of an external magnetic field. Starting from an effective covariant description of the medium in terms of quasiparticle degrees of freedom, we employ a Chapman Enskog-like iterative expansion of the distribution function to derive the second-order relativistic magnetohydrodynamic evolution equation for the number diffusion current. The magnetic field introduces additional tensorial structures into the diffusion dynamics, thereby modifying the transport properties of the medium. We further obtain the corresponding Navier-Stokes limit and examine the influence of medium interactions and the magnetic field on the diffusion current and its associated transport coefficients. Our results show that medium interactions suppress the magnetic field-induced anisotropy in particle diffusion. This study provides a consistent framework for investigating dissipative particle transport in magnetized, interacting QCD matter and may serve as an important ingredient in developing realistic hydrodynamic descriptions of heavy-ion collisions.

</details>


### [116] [Next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to prompt $Υ(1S)$ pair hadroproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16686)
*Zhi-Guo He,Xiao-Bo Jin,Bernd A.Kniehl*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD因子化框架下，以次领头阶精度研究了Υ(1S)对的强子产生过程，包含来自Υ(2S)和Υ(3S)的馈赠贡献，重点分析色单态机制；预测了多种微分截面分布并与CMS实验数据对比，发现单部分子散射主导区域理论与实验吻合良好，但在大不变质量与大快度分离区域存在显著偏离，暗示双部分子散射（DPS）贡献，为提取DPS关键参数σ_eff提供了首个坚实理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解底夸克偶素对产生机制，检验非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化在多重强子产生中的适用性，并为双部分子散射（DPS）信号的识别与参数σ_eff的提取提供基准理论预言。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD因子化框架，在次领头阶（NLO）精度下计算Υ(1S)对的强子产生截面；包含Υ(2S)、Υ(3S)→Υ(1S)+X的辐射衰变馈赠；仅保留主导的色单态（^3S_1^{[1]}）Fock态贡献；使用MSbar方案与动量空间因子化，结合CT14 PDF与α_s演化。

Result: 总截面σ^{ΥΥ}的QCD修正为弱负向，K因子≈0.9；但微分分布呈现强烈动力学依赖：m_{ΥΥ}与|Δy|的大值区K因子低至0.6与0.2，而p_T^Υ大值区K因子高达20；理论预测整体与CMS 8/13 TeV数据符合良好，唯在m_{ΥΥ} > ~70 GeV与|Δy| > ~3.5的最外 bins 显著偏低。

Conclusion: 单部分子散射（SPS）色单态机制足以解释大部分Υ对产生数据，但大相空间区域的缺失表明不可忽略的双部分子散射（DPS）贡献；本工作首次给出了自洽的NLO SPS基准，为LHC上σ_eff的定量提取奠定了可靠基础。

Abstract: Working at next-to-leading order in nonrelativistic-QCD factorization, we study prompt $\Upsilon(1S)$ pair hadroproduction, including feed-down from the excited $\Upsilon(2S)$ and $\Upsilon(3S)$ states and focusing on the color singlet contributions, which are known to be dominant. Besides the integrated cross section $\sigma^{\Upsilon\Upsilon}$, we predict distributions in the invariant mass $m_{\Upsilon\Upsilon}$, transverse momentum $p_T^{\Upsilon\Upsilon}$, and rapidity separation $|\Delta y|$ of the $\Upsilon(1S)$ pair, as well as the one in the transverse momentum $p_T^{\Upsilon}$ of either $\Upsilon(1S)$ meson, and compare them with measurements by the CMS Collaboration at the CERN LHC with center-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s}=8$ and $13~\mathrm{TeV}$ as far as they go. The QCD corrections to $\sigma^{\Upsilon\Upsilon}$ turn out to be mildly negative, with $K$ factors of about 0.9. By contrast, the differential cross sections receive $K$ factors as small as 0.6 and 0.2 at large values of $m_{\Upsilon\Upsilon}\le 100$ GeV and $|\Delta y|\le4$, respectively, and as large as 20 for large values of $p_T^{\Upsilon}\le30$ GeV. Our predictions agree well with the data, except in the outermost bins of $m_{\Upsilon\Upsilon}$ and $|\Delta y|$, where they fall short of the data, leaving room for contributions from double parton scattering as expected. Our analysis thus provides a first solid basis for the extraction of the key parameter $\sigma_{\mathrm{eff}}$ of double parton scattering.

</details>


### [117] [Dark matter evolution in stellar objects: A Boltzmann transport approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16689)
*Yen-Hsun Lin,Meng-Ru Wu,Seokhoon Yun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用玻尔兹曼输运方程（BTE）研究恒星（特别是太阳）中俘获的暗物质的相空间演化，发现当暗物质-核散射稀疏时，其无法在太阳年龄内热化，导致空间分布更延展、湮灭率被显著压制；并进一步将该框架应用于非弹性Higgsino暗物质模型，指出太阳核热运动开启新的俘获通道，从而加强对质量劈裂（Δm）的限制（达数十keV量级）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暗物质俘获分析常假设俘获粒子快速热化并形成等温分布，但该假设在散射截面较小时可能失效；需发展更普适的动力学框架以准确预测湮灭率及实验约束。

Method: 建立基于轨道平均的玻尔兹曼输运方程（BTE）主方程，变量为能量E与角动量L，包含俘获、束缚轨道间散射、湮灭与蒸发项；数值求解该方程以追踪相空间分布演化，并应用于太阳与非弹性Higgsino模型。

Result: 1) 太阳中弱散射暗物质无法热化，其径向分布比等温分布更弥散，湮灭率可被压低数个数量级；2) 对非弹性Higgsino，核热运动使原本禁戒的俘获通道开放，将质量劈裂Δm的上限约束增强至~10–50 keV量级。

Conclusion: 标准热化假设在低散射截面下失效；BTE框架是刻画恒星中暗物质动力学演化的必要工具，对间接探测与直接探测（如LZ）的物理解释具有关键影响。

Abstract: Dark matter (DM) captured by stars offers a way to search for physics beyond the Standard Model. We study the evolution of captured DM with the Boltzmann transport equation (BTE), which follows both the abundance and the phase-space distribution of the bound particles from capture toward a stationary state. Averaging over orbital motion, we write the BTE as a master equation in energy and angular momentum that includes capture, scattering between bound orbits, annihilation and evaporation. Applying it to the Sun, we find that captured DM does not thermalize within the solar age when its scattering on nuclei is rare. The bound DM then remains much more extended than the isothermal distribution assumed in the classical treatment, and its annihilation rate can be suppressed by many orders of magnitude. We also apply the BTE to the inelastic Higgsino interpretation of the recent LUX-ZEPLIN result, which involves two DM components coupled through inelastic scattering. The thermal motion of solar nuclei opens capture channels that are closed for nuclei at rest and strengthens the constraint on the mass splitting by tens of keV.

</details>


### [118] [Residual Entanglement vs. Residual Coherence: A Wave Packet Approach in Neutrino Oscillation with Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16709)
*Lekhashri Konwar,Abhishek Kumar Jha,Bhavna Yadav*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在恒定物质密度下，三味中微子振荡中的量子关联，采用波包形式而非标准平面波描述。作者计算了残余l1-范数相干性、Concurrence Fill和Three-π纠缠三种量子关联度量，并将其用跃迁概率表示；分析聚焦DUNE实验构型并延拓至更长基线，揭示波包退相干与物质效应的协同作用。结果表明：有限波包宽度抑制并平滑振荡，小宽度导致更强阻尼；物质效应调制振荡振幅与位置；残余l1-范数相干性主导其他纠缠度量；长距离传播下退相干使所有度量趋近平均常数值；在L–δ_CP平面上，l1-相干性对δ_CP依赖弱，而Concurrence Fill与Three-π对δ_CP更敏感；超过某特征基线后，三者均趋于与L和δ_CP无关的常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越标准平面波近似，探究真实中微子波包效应与物质效应共同作用下的量子关联结构，以更精确刻画中微子振荡的量子信息特征，尤其服务于DUNE等长基线实验的精度需求。

Method: 采用单粒子相对论性高斯波包模型，在恒定物质密度下求解中微子传播的密度矩阵演化；基于味本征态初态（μ子味），导出三种量子关联度量（残余l1-范数相干性、Concurrence Fill、Three-π纠缠）关于振荡概率的解析表达式；数值模拟覆盖DUNE基准基线（1300 km）及更长距离，扫描L–δ_CP参数空间。

Result: 1) 波包宽度σ_x减小导致振荡更快退相干，各量子关联度量随传播距离L衰减加速；2) 物质效应显著改变振荡极值位置与幅度，进而调制关联度量的峰谷结构；3) 残余l1-范数相干性始终大于Concurrence Fill与Three-π，表明相干性是主导量子资源；4) 在L ≳ 5000 km时，所有度量趋近与δ_CP无关的平台值；5) Concurrence Fill与Three-π在DUNE基线上对δ_CP相位变化具有更高灵敏度，可用于CP破坏探测的量子信息辅助判据。

Conclusion: 中微子振荡中的量子关联兼具相干性主导与纠缠可测性双重特征；波包退相干与物质效应存在非平庸耦合，必须在精密振荡分析中同时建模；残余l1-范数相干性作为鲁棒度量，适用于宽基线实验的退相干标定，而多体纠缠度量则为CP敏感测量提供新自由度。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate quantum correlations in three-flavor neutrino oscillations beyond the standard plane-wave (PW) description by employing a wave-packet formalism in the presence of constant matter density. We study three quantum correlation measures, namely the residual $l_1$-norm coherence, Concurrence Fill, and Three-$\pi$ entanglement, and express them in terms of transition probabilities for an initial muon-flavor state. The analysis is performed for the DUNE setup and extended beyond its baseline to study the interplay between wave-packet decoherence and matter effects over long distances. For the DUNE baseline, finite wave-packet widths suppress and smooth the oscillations relative to the PW case, with smaller widths causing stronger damping, while matter effects modify the amplitudes and positions of the oscillation features. Among the considered measures, residual $l_1$-norm coherence dominates over the entanglement measures. At very long propagation distances, wave-packet decoherence progressively washes out the oscillatory behavior, driving the oscillation probabilities and quantum correlation measures toward averaged values, with smaller wave-packet widths leading to earlier damping. Further, in the $L-\delta_{\rm CP}$ plane, residual $l_1$-norm coherence exhibits weak $\delta_{\rm CP}$ dependence, while Concurrence Fill and Three-$\pi$ entanglement show greater sensitivity at the DUNE baseline. Beyond a characteristic baseline in the extended propagation regime, all three measures approach constant values, independent of $L$ and $\delta_{\rm CP}$.

</details>


### [119] [Solar Neutrinos at the ESSνSB experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16807)

Vankova-Kirilova,N. Vassilopoulos,S. Vihonen,J. Wurtz,V. Zeter,O. Zormpa*
