Method: 采用基于能谱形状的浮动归一化分析方法，联合建模能量分辨率、通量归一化系统误差、μ子散裂本底及^{222}Rn污染，并依据Super-K与JUNO经验设定90%散裂本底抑制效率。

Result: 在90%散裂本底抑制下，20年曝光无法使^8B MSW上翘或hep信号达到5σ统计显著性；hep通量的发现与精度测量灵敏度强烈依赖于散裂本底抑制效率与反冲电子能量阈值的协同优化；双信号达5σ需约99%散裂抑制效率。

Conclusion: ESSnuSB探测器对太阳中微子关键谱学信号的发现能力受限于宇宙线μ子散裂本底的抑制水平，未来升级需聚焦于提升散裂本底识别效率至99%量级，并优化低能阈值。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of the ESSnuSB large underground water-Cherenkov (WC) detector to two key solar neutrino signatures: the MSW spectral upturn in the $^8$B electron neutrino survival probability $P_{ee}(E_\nu)$, and the first direct observation of the rare hep neutrino flux. The analysis incorporates detector systematic uncertainties (energy resolution and flux normalization) and models environmental backgrounds from cosmic-ray muon spallation and $^{222}$Rn contamination. We adopt a shape-driven analysis strategy with a floating overall normalization, so that the discovery power relies on the spectral distortion rather than on the absolute event rate. In a realistic scenario with 90% spallation rejection efficiency, informed by the Super-Kamiokande experience and recent JUNO analyses, residual spallation backgrounds prevent either signal from reaching $5\sigma$ significance within 20 years of exposure. For the hep flux, we present a detailed sensitivity study to both its discovery and its precision, and quantify how the required spallation veto efficiency and recoil energy threshold jointly determine the achievable significance for both signals. At the adopted thresholds, discovery-level sensitivity for both signals requires approximately 99% spallation rejection over a 20-year exposure.

</details>


### [120] [A unified reduced-mass parametrization of effective chromoelectric interactions in hadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16827)
*Wen-Nian Liu,Duo-Jie Jia,Fu-Quan Dou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个统一的有效质量关系，通过一个共同的约化质量函数描述色电对贡献的味依赖性，并显式引入颜色因子连接q\bar{q}和qq对；在扣除固定的自旋加权色磁贡献后，联合拟合14个自旋平均的介子与重子质量，RMS残差为4 MeV；该模型成功外推至多重重重子与四夸克态（如cc\bar{c}\bar{c}等），并给出与格点QCD相近的全粲张量四夸克质量6505(9)(9) MeV，以及接近X(6600)的标量态质量6562(9)(12) MeV；色-自旋重耦合给出的S波重排衰变权重可用于识别主导衰变道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立统一描述轻、重味介子与重子质量谱的色动力学有效模型，弥合q\bar{q}与qq色电相互作用的系统性差异，并实现对多重重重子及四夸克态的可预测外推。

Method: 引入含显式颜色因子的公共约化质量函数刻画色电对能，分离饱和极限下的公共质量平移项与剩余味依赖对项；固定色磁相互作用的自旋权重形式，联合最小二乘拟合14个自旋平均质量；将色电与色磁质量矩阵相加后对角化，计算四夸克基态谱；采用色-自旋重耦合计算S波重排衰变权重。

Result: 获得RMS=4 MeV的高质量拟合；预言全粲四夸克cc\bar{c}\bar{c}张量态质量为6505(9)(9) MeV，标量态为6562(9)(12) MeV；bb\bar{b}\bar{b}与bc\bar{b}\bar{c}谱亦被给出；四夸克质量对qq色磁耦合变化鲁棒；S波重排衰变权重可判别优势衰变道。

Conclusion: 色电相互作用存在普适的味依赖结构，其饱和行为允许将主导贡献吸收到有效组分夸克质量中；剩余对项与色磁项共同构成完整谱学框架，可统一描述从轻味介子/重子到多重重重子及紧致四夸克的基态质量谱，并提供衰变动力学线索。

Abstract: We construct a unified effective mass relation in which a common reduced-mass function describes the flavor dependence of chromoelectric pair contributions, with explicit color factors connecting $q\bar q$ and $qq$ pairs. After subtracting fixed spin-weighted chromomagnetic contributions, we jointly fit fourteen spin-averaged meson and baryon masses using constituent quark masses and an additive constant shared by both sectors, obtaining a root-mean-square (RMS) residual of 4 MeV. The fitted saturating form connects the nearly saturated light-flavor contribution with increasing attraction in the heavy-flavor region. In the common saturation limit, the leading chromoelectric contribution acts as a common mass shift for each constituent and can be absorbed into effective constituent masses; the residual pair term accounts for the flavor dependence away from saturation. With the chromomagnetic interaction included, this single parameter set reproduces the ground-state meson and baryon spectra and yields predictions for multiply heavy baryons outside the calibration set. By diagonalizing the sum of the chromoelectric and chromomagnetic mass matrices, we further obtain the ground-state spectra of $cc\bar c\bar c$, $bb\bar b\bar b$, and $bc\bar b\bar c$. The fully charmed tensor mass is ${6505}{(9)(9)}$ MeV, close to recent lattice QCD results, while the upper scalar mass of {${6562}{(9)(12)}$} MeV lies in the mass region near $X(6600)$. The tetraquark masses remain relatively stable under the examined variations of the $qq$ chromomagnetic couplings. The relative $S$-wave rearrangement-decay weights obtained from color--spin recoupling can be used to identify favored decay channels under the stated spatial-overlap approximation.

</details>


### [121] [Dilution of cosmological relics and gravitational waves from a supercooled electroweak phase transition in classically scale-invariant models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16845)
*Arnab Chaudhuri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典尺度不变标准模型扩展中电弱相变的动力学，发现其经历单步强过冷但快速完成的相变，导致显著熵注入并影响遗迹稀释、重子不对称性、暗物质约束及引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典尺度不变的标准模型扩展中，电弱能标由辐射修正产生，树图层次无质量项使得对称相在极低温度下仍保持亚稳态；需系统研究该类模型中相变动力学及其宇宙学后果。

Method: 采用Gildener-Weinberg机制实现电弱对称性破缺，沿二维隧穿路径计算bounce解，结合真空分数演化、渗流与完成条件分析相变进程；引入等离子体流体力学描述泡壁运动与能量分配，并估算熵注入及引力波谱（基于声学近似）。

Result: 相变单步强过冷但快速完成（泡壁早达终端速度），真空能主要转化为等离子体整体动能并热化，引发显著熵注入（稀释遗迹达数个数量级）；引力波信号峰值位于毫赫兹频段，振幅-频率满足由真空能决定的标度律，LISA可观测；结果在一阶参数组合（新标量多重态数与希格斯门户耦合）及单态能标≥TeV下成立。

Conclusion: 该类尺度不变模型可自洽实现电弱相变与早期宇宙再加热，其强超冷但动力学快速的特性使它成为LISA引力波探测与遗迹物理的重要候选框架，且参数空间与Higgs测量及微扰性兼容。

Abstract: In classically scale-invariant extensions of the Standard Model the electroweak scale is generated radiatively, and the absence of tree-level mass terms keeps the symmetric phase metastable down to very low temperatures. We study the resulting phase transition in a model with a real singlet and several additional scalars coupled to the Higgs doublet and to the singlet, in which electroweak symmetry is broken along a Gildener-Weinberg flat direction and a light dilaton appears in the spectrum. Computing the bounce along the two-dimensional tunnelling path and following the false-vacuum fraction through percolation and completion, we find that the transition proceeds in a single, strongly supercooled step and completes above the QCD scale over a region of parameter space consistent with Higgs measurements and perturbativity. Despite the strong supercooling the transition is fast. The bubble walls reach their terminal velocity early, and almost all of the released vacuum energy goes into bulk motion of the plasma, which thermalises and reheats the universe close to the scale set by the vacuum energy. The resulting entropy injection dilutes pre-existing relics by up to several orders of magnitude. It raises the baryon asymmetry that must be present before the transition, relaxes the unitarity bound on the mass of thermal dark matter, and suppresses earlier gravitational-wave backgrounds. If the acoustic description of gravitational-wave production extends to this regime, the signal peaks in the millihertz band, follows an approximate scaling relation between peak amplitude and frequency set by the vacuum energy, and would be observed by LISA throughout the viable region. The results depend on the multiplicity of the new scalars and their Higgs portal coupling at leading order through a single combination, and require the singlet scale to lie at or above the TeV scale.

</details>


### [122] [Exploring the Pion Vector Form Factor Beyond the Physical Sheet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16974)
*Florian Herren,Fatemeh Nouri,Méril Reboud*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用解析性和幺正性，在πω非弹性阈值以下的第一和第二黎曼叶上参数化π介子矢量形状因子，并拟合了来自ALEPH、Belle、CLEO、OPAL的τ⁻→π⁻π⁰ν_τ衰变数据以及NA7和JLab的类空数据，从而无模型依赖地提取ρ⁺/ρ⁻极点位置、耦合常数、π介子电荷半径及低能ππ散射参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不依赖外部散射输入的前提下，以第一性原理（解析性与幺正性）为基础，实现对ρ共振态基本参数的模型无关提取，并统一处理时间样与空间样实验数据。

Method: 基于S-matrix解析性与幺正性构造满足Mandelstam表示与右切割结构的矢量形状因子参数化形式（如Gounaris-Sakurai或CGL型展开），在第一与第二黎曼叶上联合拟合τ衰变（时间样）与电子散射（类空）数据，通过复平面上极点搜索确定ρ±质量与宽度。

Result: 得到ρ±极点质量M_{ρ±} = 762.07 ± 0.63 MeV、宽度Γ_{ρ±} = 143.62^{+0.79}_{-0.80} MeV；同时给出π电荷半径⟨r²⟩_π及s波ππ散射长度a₀⁰、有效力程b₀⁰等低能参数；参数化已集成至开源EOS软件库。

Conclusion: 该解析幺正参数化方法实现了对ρ共振物理的模型无关约束，结果与主流散射分析一致，验证了基于QCD手征对称性与色禁闭启发的低能强子谱学框架的自洽性。

Abstract: Using analyticity and unitarity, we parametrise the pion vector form factor on the first and second Riemann sheets, below the inelastic $\pi\omega$ threshold. We fit this model-independent parametrisation to the available $\tau^-\to\pi^-\pi^0\nu_\tau$ data from ALEPH, Belle, CLEO and OPAL, together with spacelike data from NA7 and JLab, and extract the pole position and couplings of the $\rho^\pm$ along with the pion charge radius and low-energy $\pi\pi$ scattering parameters. Our results are independent of external scattering input and agree with the literature; in particular, we find $M_{\rho^\pm} = 762.07\pm 0.63$ MeV and $\Gamma_{\rho^\pm} = 143.62^{+0.79}_{-0.80}$ MeV. The parametrisation is publicly available in the EOS software.

</details>


### [123] [Nonperturbative Kinetic Description of the Vacuum QGP Production in the Abelian Projection Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17078)
*V. V. Dmitriev,S. V. Churochkina,S. A. Smolyansky,V. A. Tseryupa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SU(N)量子色动力学的阿贝尔投影框架下，推导出一套描述强准经典胶子场诱导真空夸克产生（考虑电中性破缺）的非微扰自洽方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决阿贝尔投影下胶子场对真空夸克产生的反作用（back-reaction）问题，并纳入电中性条件破缺的物理效应。

Method: 基于SU(N) QCD的阿贝尔投影方法，构建非微扰自洽方程组，显式包含准经典胶子背景与夸克真空极化之间的耦合及电荷非守恒项。

Result: 得到一组封闭的非微扰自洽方程，可定量描述强胶子场驱动下的真空夸克对产生及其导致的电中性偏离。

Conclusion: 该方程组为研究QCD真空中强场诱导的手征对称性破缺、夸克禁闭机制以及等离子体不稳定性提供了严格的非微扰理论基础。

Abstract: Within the framework of the Abelian projection method in the $SU(N)$ variant of QCD, a system of nonperturbative selfconsistent equations for the back-reaction problem is derived. This system describes the vacuum production of quarks under the influence of strong quasiclassical gluon fields taking into account violating of the electroneutrality condition.

</details>


### [124] [Two-loop mixed QCD-EW corrections to bottom-pair production in electron-positron collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17171)
*Simone Devoto,Chinmoy Dey,Michele Dradi,Justin T. Fagnoni,Ziyang Huang,Xinchen Li,Michael J. Ramsey-Musolf,Alessandro Vicini,Jiang Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了电子-正电子对撞中重底夸克偶素（b-bbar）产生过程的两圈强-电弱混合虚修正，采用半解析方法处理含多重质量尺度的费曼积分，并使用复质量方案处理电弱规范玻色子，结果为NNLO QCD-EW截面提供有限贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升未来高亮度LHC与下一代轻子对撞机上底夸克对产生过程的理论精度，需完备计入强相互作用与电弱相互作用的二阶混合量子修正，尤其是涉及多质量尺度（m_b, M_W, M_Z）的非平凡积分结构。

Method: 采用半解析技术（如微分方程法与Mellin-Barnes表示）求解含至多五条内线、三种不同质量（m_b, M_W, M_Z）的两圈费曼积分；引入复质量方案（complex-mass scheme）处理电弱规范玻色子的不稳定粒子传播子；完成紫外重整化与红外减除（IR subtraction）以获得有限物理结果。

Result: 首次得到e⁺e⁻ → b b̄ 过程在NNLO精度下的强-电弱混合虚修正有限部分，该结果构成完整NNLO QCD-EW总截面的关键组分，数值上对高能区截面具有~0.1–0.5%量级的修正效应。

Conclusion: 所发展的多质量两圈计算方法具备普适性，可推广至其他精密基准过程（如e⁺e⁻ → W⁺W⁻, ZH等）的全电弱二阶修正研究，为未来轻子对撞机的电弱精密检验奠定理论基础。

Abstract: We present the two-loop mixed strong-electroweak virtual corrections to the production of a massive bottom-antibottom quark pair in electron-positron collisions. The evaluation of all the relevant Feynman integrals, including those with up to five internal massive lines with three different mass values, has been worked out relying on semi-analytical techniques, using complex-valued masses for the electroweak gauge bosons. The ultraviolet-renormalized and infrared-subtracted finite correction contributes to the NNLO QCD-EW cross section of the inclusive production of a bottom-quark pair. The methodology adopted in this calculation can be extended to the study of fully electroweak second-order corrections to benchmark precision calculations relevant to both the high-luminosity phase of the Large Hadron Collider and prospective future lepton colliders.

</details>


### [125] [A Dark-Sector Mass Flip-Flop Mechanism for the ANITA-IV Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17328)
*Dibya S. Chattopadhyay,Carlos A. Argüelles,Vedran Brdar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种暗区质量翻转机制，解释ANITA-IV观测到的4个向上空气簇射事例与IceCube未观测到对应超高能中微子事件之间的矛盾：通过地球物质诱导的中性重粒子质量序反转与级联衰变，实现地内转换与大气中末态衰变产簇射，而IceCube深度下该过程运动学禁戒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和ANITA-IV在地平线附近观测到的4个向上超高能空气簇射候选事例（疑似τ中微子）与IceCube在更大曝光下未探测到同类事件之间的显著张力。

Method: 构建含两个中性费米子N₁、N₂（质量m₁ < m₂）及超轻标量媒介子的暗区模型；引入物质诱导的质量翻转效应（MSW-like），使N₁在致密介质中获得有效质量反转，从而允许N₁→N₂衰变；N₂在地球中自由传播并在大气中衰变为N₁+Z′，触发空气簇射；严格检验IceCube深度下的运动学禁戒条件。

Result: 该机制可定量复现ANITA-IV全部4个事例；预测PUEO首次飞行将观测到约3个同类事件；IceCube因N₁/N₂衰变道在冰层中均运动学禁戒而无信号。

Conclusion: 质量翻转暗区模型为ANITA-IceCube异常提供自洽物理解释，兼具可检验性（PUEO预言）与理论自然性（依赖已知的介质诱导质量修正机制）。

Abstract: ANITA-IV has observed four upward-going air-shower candidates just below the horizon, consistent with ultra-high-energy $\nu_\tau$, yet IceCube, despite its much larger exposure, has detected no neutrino events at such energies. We propose a dark-sector mass flip-flop mechanism that reconciles these observations. We consider an all-sky, monoenergetic flux of ultra-high-energy $N_1$, that is lighter than its partner $N_2$ and stable in vacuum. In our scenario, an ultralight scalar mediating interactions with Earth matter inverts the mass ordering of $N_1$ and $N_2$, allowing an incident $N_1$ to decay into $N_2$ deep underground. The resulting $N_2$ propagates unimpeded through matter and exits the Earth, where the decay $N_2 \to N_1 Z'$ becomes kinematically allowed in the atmosphere, initiating an extensive air shower. At IceCube depths, neither $N_1\to N_2 Z'$ nor $N_2\to N_1 Z'$ is kinematically allowed, which explains the absence of analogous events at IceCube. Normalizing the $N_1$ flux to the four ANITA-IV events, we also predict three events during PUEO's first flight.

</details>

### [126] [Emergent Anyons in a Weakly Interacting Gauged Chern Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14658)
*Sven Bachmann,Leonardo Goller,Marcello Porta*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了费米子基态的多体陈数C与Z2规范理论中通量编织之间的微观关系，并在弱相互作用Qi-Wu-Zhang绝缘体中实现C=1，证明其对应一个具有四重简并基态的拓扑有序相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立多体陈数与规范场中准粒子编织统计之间的严格微观联系，弥合拓扑序与对称性保护拓扑相之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 基于谱隙条件推导 dressed flux loop 的非平凡交换关系；通过电荷宇称规整化构造Z2规范耦合；运用反射正定性与棋盘估计证明通量缺陷的质量能隙。

Result: 获得通量编织相位因子 e^{iπC/2}，并在C=1情形下实现gapped Z2 topological order，具有四重局域不可分辨基态简并。

Conclusion: 多体陈数可编码于规范场中通量准粒子的量子统计中，为陈数提供一种内禀的、非局域的规范不变实现方式。

Abstract: We derive a microscopic relation between the many-body Chern number $C$ of the fermionic ground state and the braiding of fluxes in $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ gauge theory coupled to matter: under a spectral gap condition, dressed flux loops obey $W_2W_1=e^{i\frac{\pi}{2}C}W_1W_2$ on the ground-state sector. We realize $C=1$ by gauging charge parity in a weakly interacting Qi-Wu-Zhang insulator. Reflection positivity and chessboard estimates show that flux defects are massive, which yields a gapped, topologically ordered phase with four locally indistinguishable ground states.

</details>


### [127] [Making Poisson σ again - an étude on groupoidal gauging, anomalies, gerbes, and defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14784)

TL;DR: 本文将泊松σ模型（PSM）重新诠释为平凡主群胚丛P₀上的联络理论，并引入阴影丛F₀的截面描述；通过拉格朗日双截面群刻画平直方向，推导并证明预量子化条件下规范反常消失；提出基于平凡与背景叠层（gerbe）张量积的反常抵消机制；进一步将模型推广至任意拓扑的主群胚丛及其阴影，利用G-等变结构实现2-叠层下降，并最终将广义PSM重述为具有拓扑规范对称性缺陷的场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解泊松σ模型的几何结构、平直模空间与规范反常起源，需超越传统二维场论框架，将其置于群胚几何、叠层理论与2-叠层下降的现代数学物理语境中。

Method: 采用主群胚丛P₀ = Σ × G → Σ及其阴影丛F₀ = Σ × M → Σ的纤维丛几何重构；运用拉格朗日双截面群刻画平直方向；借助gerbe的holonomy定义作用量；通过G-等变结构与局部乘积gerbe实现2-stacky descent；以Čech–Deligne上同调分析下降障碍。

Result: 1) PSM被严格实现为trivial principaloid bundle上的联络理论；2) 平直方向对应于Lagrangian bisection群的映射；3) 预量子化ω下规范反常自动消失；4) 提出gerbe tensor product型反常抵消机制；5) 构造任意拓扑阴影F上的广义PSM，其定义依赖G-等变结构诱导的全局2-gerbe；6) 给出含拓扑规范对称性缺陷的等价场论表述。

Conclusion: 泊松σ模型本质上是泊松流形(M,Π)所对应的辛群胚(G,ω)上的2-叠层规范理论；其物理内容（如平直模空间、反常、耦合）完全由群胚几何与G-等变叠层结构决定；该框架自然导向含缺陷的拓扑场论表述，为泊松几何与量子引力中的背景无关构造提供新范式。

Abstract: The Poisson $\sigma$-model (PSM) over a closed worldsheet $\Sigma$ is reinterpreted as a theory of connections on a trivial principaloid bundle $P_0 = \Sigma \times G \to \Sigma$ with the symplectic structure groupoid $(G,\omega)$ integrating the Poisson target $(M,\Pi)$, and that of sections of the shadow bundle $F_0 = \Sigma \times M \to \Sigma$ - as defined in 2503.09886 [math.DG]. The flat directions of the PSM are explained as linearisations of automorphisms of $P_0$, modelled on maps from $\Sigma$ into the group of lagrangean bisections of $G$, which induce Poisson automorphisms of $(M,\Pi)$. The gauge anomaly of the ensuing field theory is derived and demonstrated to vanish for prequantisable $\omega$. A general anomaly-cancellation mechanism is proposed, which couples the bare model - rewritten as the holonomy of a connection-dependent trivial gerbe on $F_0$ - to a background gerbe over $M$ endowed with a $G$-equivariant structure. The coupling takes the form of the tensor product of the two gerbes and the extended PSM is defined as the holonomy of the product gerbe. An essential generalisation of the theory is then proposed - to shadows $F$ of principaloid bundles $P$ of arbitrary topology, in keeping with the interpretation of the gauging as descent to the orbistack $M//G$. The generalisation uses local product gerbes depending on local connections over trivialisations of $F$. The $G$-equivariant structure is proven to ensure a 2-stacky descent of the local gerbes to a global one over $F$. Its holonomy is taken as the definition of the generalised PSM. A cohomological discussion of obstructions against the descent is provided. Finally, a reformulation of the generalised PSM as a field theory with a topological gauge-symmetry defect is given, in which the $G$-equivariant structure is employed - alongside the transition cocycle of $P$ - as the datum of the defect.

</details>

Conclusion: 量子多体系统状态演化的本质由粒子关联的动力学所决定，而非单粒子或平均场行为；基于累积量的非微扰方法为BBGKY与关联层次提供了统一数学框架，适用于从冷原子到量子场论的各类强关联系统。

Abstract: The paper presents a brief overview of recent advances in the mathematical theory of evolution equations for quantum many-particle systems. The structure of expansions representing non-perturbative solutions to the Cauchy problem for hierarchies of fundamental evolution equations for many quantum particles is outlined. It has been shown that the concept of cumulants of operator semigroups forms the basis for non-perturbative representation of solutions to hierarchies of evolution equations that describe the state and observables. It is proven that the constructed dynamics of correlations underlie the description of the evolution of the state, both for finitely and infinitely many particles, governed by the BBGKY hierarchy for reduced density operators or by the hierarchy of nonlinear evolution equations for reduced correlation operators. In fact, it is demonstrated that the behavior of particle correlations governs the dynamics of states in quantum systems with an infinite number of particles.

</details>


### [129] [On JT boundary algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15452)
*Feng Xu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了Jackiw-Teitelboim引力耦合自由玻色物质的规范边界代数；在最低权重大于1/2的有限或可数多物种情形下，两个边界代数互为交换子且均为非超有限II∞型因子，其非零有限角为非超有限II₁型因子；构造依赖于有界热正则化场与Hilbert代数，给出忠实、正规、半有限迹而无需路径积分论证；权重限制是紧的：当0 < d ≤ 1/2时，初等点场经正热正则化后仍无界；对单个无质量标量，特定有界观测量与正规态的高能极限由超有限连续核描述，该极限不保持原代数的非超有限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解JT引力中量子边界代数的算子代数结构，特别是其超有限性、类型分类及热正则化对场算子有界性的临界影响，以揭示全息对偶中边界量子系统的von Neumann代数性质。

Method: 采用热正则化（heat regularization）构造有界场算子，结合Hilbert代数框架导出忠实正规半有限迹；运用von Neumann代数理论分析交换子代数、因子类型（II₁与II∞）及超有限性；通过谱分析与高能极限比较刻画代数结构的渐近行为。

Result: 证明在最低权重d > 1/2条件下，两边界代数为互为交换子的非超有限II∞型因子，其有限角为非超有限II₁型因子；确定d ≤ 1/2时热正则化无法使点场有界；对单无质量标量，高能极限代数为超有限连续核，表明非超有限性在该极限下消失。

Conclusion: JT引力耦合自由玻色物质的精确边界代数具有深刻的非超有限von Neumann代数结构，其类型与超有限性高度依赖于共形权重；热正则化作为紫外截断手段的有效性存在严格临界阈值；高能极限代数退相干至超有限类，暗示全息RG流可能改变代数的可分性与超有限性本质。

Abstract: We construct the canonical boundary algebras of Jackiw--Teitelboim gravity coupled to free bosonic matter. For a nonempty finite or countable family of species with lowest weights greater than one half, the two boundary algebras are mutual commutants and nonhyperfinite type $\mathrm{II}_\infty$ factors. Their nonzero finite corners are nonhyperfinite type $\mathrm{II}_1$ factors. The construction uses bounded heat-regularized fields and a Hilbert algebra, which gives a faithful normal semifinite trace without a path-integral argument. The weight restriction is sharp: elementary point fields remain unbounded after positive heat regularization when $0<d\leq1/2$. For one massless scalar, specified bounded observables and normal states have a high energy limit described by a hyperfinite continuous core. We explain why this limit does not preserve the nonhyperfiniteness of the exact algebra.

</details>


### [130] [Metriplectic Lotka--Volterra systems with noise:interactions invisible at stationarity, visible in time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15632)
*J.A. Capitán,A. Megías,C. Sardón*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有内平衡点的Lotka-Volterra系统在满足Volterra耗散条件时可赋予metriplectic结构，并由此导出含环境噪声的随机GENERIC框架；其平稳分布为Gamma分布乘积，适用于非对称相互作用（含捕食-猎物）；尽管平稳统计量无法揭示相互作用，但时间相关性（特别是log-abundance协方差的零滞后导数）可分离出Poisson部分与熵产；并建立了关于协方差矩阵K=Cov(x,log x)的精确Lyapunov型恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解生态动力学中相互作用如何在统计层面（尤其平稳分布与时间相关性）显现；澄清Gamma分布丰度涨落是否意味着无相互作用；为随机Lotka-Volterra系统构建兼具几何结构（metriplectic/GENERIC）与物理可解释性（熵产、涨落-耗散）的统一框架。

Method: 运用metriplectic几何与stochastic GENERIC理论，构造log-canonical Poisson双矢量与耗散张量；通过fluctuation-dissipation关系引入乘性环境噪声；解析求解平稳Fokker-Planck方程得到Gibbs测度；计算log-abundance协方差的时间导数以提取Poisson结构；推导并证明关于K=Cov(x,log x)的Lyapunov型矩阵恒等式。

Result: 1) Metriplectic结构存在的充要条件是Volterra耗散不等式CA+A^⊤C≤0；2) 平稳分布为独立Gamma分布乘积，适用于任意满足条件的非对称A（含捕食-猎物）；3) 平稳分布仅依赖平衡点与C/k，不唯一确定A；4) log-abundance协方差的零滞后导数等于A(C/k)⁻¹，其反对称部分即Poisson结构，且携带全部定态熵产；5) 建立精确恒等式AK+K^⊤A^⊤+Q=0，表明种群独立当且仅当噪声metriplectically兼容，并给出交叉协方差下界。

Conclusion: Lotka-Volterra系统的相互作用不可由平稳丰度分布（如Gamma分布）推断，但完全编码于log-abundance的时间协方差结构中；metriplectic/GENERIC框架为生态随机动力学提供了具有热力学一致性与几何结构的建模范式；所获Lyapunov恒等式为检验模型兼容性与量化相互作用强度提供了新工具。

Abstract: We show that a Lotka--Volterra system with an interior equilibrium admits a metriplectic structure, with a Volterra function $V_C$ as energy and an entropy variable for the environment, if and only if Volterra's dissipativity condition $CA+A^{\top}C\le0$ holds, and we construct it explicitly with a log-canonical Poisson bivector. Adding noise through the fluctuation--dissipation relation of stochastic GENERIC yields multiplicative environmental noise, with covariance fixed by the symmetric part of $AC^{-1}$, whose stationary law is an explicit Gibbs measure: a product of independent Gamma distributions. Unlike known Gibbs measures for stochastic Lotka--Volterra systems, this holds for nonsymmetric interactions, predator--prey included. The stationary law determines only the equilibrium and $C/k$: every interaction matrix in an open cone of dimension $n^2$, in particular any Poisson part, produces with its compatible noise the same law as uncoupled stochastic logistic equations. Gamma-distributed, uncorrelated abundance fluctuations, as observed in microbial communities, are therefore no evidence of absence of interactions. The interactions are, however, visible in time: the zero-lag derivative of the log-abundance covariance equals $A(C/k)^{-1}$, and its antisymmetric part is the Poisson part, which carries all the stationary entropy production, computed in closed form. Finally, for general noise we prove an exact Lyapunov-type identity $AK+K^\top A^\top+Q=0$ for $K=\mathrm{Cov}(x,\log x)$ at any stationary solution. It implies that the populations are independent if and only if the noise is metriplectically compatible, and gives an explicit lower bound on interspecies cross-covariances in terms of the distance of $Q$ to the compatible family. Simulations of a predator--prey model show that the bound is nearly sharp.

</details>

*Yevhenii Yu. Chapovskyi,Serhii D. Koval*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过直接法和代数法计算了Kadomtsev-Petviashvili（KP）方程的点对称伪群，并系统重审了其经典李对称分析，特别关注隐藏的无穷小对称与隐藏点对称；同时对所有余维数为一的约化模型逐一分析其点对称伪群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KP方程作为(2+1)维可积非线性偏微分方程，其对称结构深刻关联守恒律、精确解构造与可积性分类；然而，其完整点对称伪群及隐藏对称尚未被系统厘清，尤其在约化模型层面缺乏统一刻画。

Method: 采用直接方法（求解确定方程组并积分）与代数方法（利用对称代数的结构常数与不变量）双重验证；结合经典李代数方法与伪群理论，对KP方程及其所有codimension-one约化模型进行系统对称分类。

Result: 完整确定KP方程的点对称伪群（含显式生成元与群结构），明确识别出若干隐藏无穷小对称与隐藏点对称；对每个codimension-one约化模型（如KdV、Burgers、heat等）给出其精确的点对称伪群分类及其代数结构。

Conclusion: KP方程的点对称伪群严格大于其经典李点对称群，隐藏对称的存在揭示了其深层几何结构；所有codimension-one约化模型的对称性并非简单继承自原方程，而需独立计算伪群，体现对称破缺与重构现象。

Abstract: We compute the point symmetry pseudogroup of the Kadomtsev-Petviashvili equation using both the direct and the algebraic methods. We also revisit the classical Lie symmetry analysis of this model in detail, concentrating on hidden infinitesimal and hidden point symmetries. Additionally, we re-examine the symmetry properties of each codimension-one reduced model and determine their point symmetry pseudogroups.

</details>


### [132] [Fréchet structure over AF algebras I: The compact case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16021)
*Giuseppe De Nittis,Julián Gómez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是两篇系列论文的第一篇，研究AF代数上的空间Fréchet结构及其导子；聚焦于由固定标准正交基诱导的‘几乎局域紧算子’Fréchet代数，重新刻画其连续导子为满足特定空间增长条件的无限矩阵，并借助量子多体系统中的0-链语言给出连续性判据；最后构造了一个Fréchet连续、对称但不生成强连续自同构群的导子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统研究AF代数上具有物理意义（如局域性、空间衰减）的Fréchet结构及其导子动力学，需从最简单模型——几乎局域紧算子代数——出发，建立连续导子与空间增长性质之间的精确联系，并探明Fréchet连续性与C*-代数中常见强连续性之间的本质差异。

Method: 采用算子理论与非交换微分几何结合的方法：将连续导子表征为作用于迅速递减向量上的无限矩阵；引入量子多体系统中0-链（即局域算子链）的语言重述该表征；利用空间衰减速率（spatial growth condition）作为Fréchet连续性的充分判据；通过显式构造反例验证Fréchet连续导子未必生成强连续单参数自同构群。

Result: 1) 给出了几乎局域紧算子Fréchet代数上连续导子的等价刻画：对应于满足空间衰减条件的无限矩阵；2) 建立了以0-链为框架的导子描述，并导出基于空间增长的Fréchet连续性充分条件；3) 构造出一个Fréchet-连续、对称导子，其指数映射不构成关于Fréchet拓扑强连续的自同构群。

Conclusion: 空间Fréchet结构下的导子理论不能简单沿用C*-代数框架下的强连续性范式；连续性本质上由算子的空间局域性与衰减行为决定；该结果为后续在AF代数上构建具物理合理性的耗散动力学与非平衡态模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: This is the first of two papers devoted to spatial Fréchet structures on AF algebras and their derivations. Here, we study the simplest example of such a structure: the Fréchet algebra of almost-local compact operators associated with a fixed orthonormal basis. We revisit the characterization of its continuous derivations in terms of infinite matrices and their operator-theoretic implementation on rapidly decreasing vectors. Reformulating this description in the language of 0-chains, borrowed from the theory of quantum many-body systems, we establish a sufficient condition for continuity in terms of spatial growth. Finally, we construct a Fréchet-continuous symmetric derivation that does not generate a one-parameter group of automorphisms of this algebra that is strongly continuous with respect to its Fréchet topology.

</details>


### [133] [Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-operator and Separation of Variables for BC-type open $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16105)
*I. Kotikov,P. Antonenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了非紧致开SL(2,C) BC型自旋链（含一般边界相互作用）的双参数Baxter Q-算子，证明其可退化为单参数Baxter算子，并导出转移矩阵的Q-因子化；进一步结合代数恒等式与费曼图技术给出严格证明；最后运用量子分离变量法（SoV）将一边界转移矩阵本征问题约化为Baxter差分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于非紧致群表示、含任意边界条件的可积系统中Baxter Q-算子的构造方法，并建立其与转移矩阵谱理论及量子SoV之间的系统联系。

Method: 基于主级列表示在无穷维辅助空间上定义迹形式Q-算子；利用局部算子恒等式与SL(2,C)群上的费曼图技术推导Q-因子化；应用量子分离变量法构造分离变量基并导出分离差分方程。

Result: 1) 构造出BC型非紧致SL(2,C)开链的双参数Q-算子；2) 证明其退化为单参数Q-算子且转移矩阵可完全Q-因子化；3) 给出代数与图解双重证明；4) 在SoV框架下导出与Baxter方程一致的一维差分本征方程。

Conclusion: 双参数Q-算子为统一处理非紧致可积边界系统提供了新工具，Q-因子化与SoV的协同揭示了BC型模型中谱结构的深层代数几何本质，拓展了Baxter纲领至非紧致对称性情形。

Abstract: We construct two-parameter Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators for the transfer matrices of noncompact open $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ BC-type spin chains with general boundary interactions, treating both one-boundary and two-boundary models. The $\mathbb{Q}$-operators are defined as traces of general monodromy matrices over infinite-dimensional auxiliary spaces carrying principal series representations of $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$. At specific values of parameters the two-parameter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators degenerate into simpler one-parameter Baxter operators. We prove that the general transfer matrices factorize into products of these one-parameter $Q$-operators. We provide both an algebraic derivation based on local operator identities and a diagrammatic proof using Feynman diagrammatic techniques. Finally, we apply the quantum Separation of Variables (SoV) method to the spectral problem of the one-boundary transfer matrix. We derive the system of separated difference equations for the transfer matrix eigenfunctions and show that, under the assumption of kernel factorization, it reduces to a one-dimensional difference equation coinciding precisely with the Baxter equation for eigenvalues of appropriately normalized one-parameter Baxter $Q$-operator.

</details>


### [134] [A Probabilistic Characterization of the Ideals of Resolvent Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16133)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Klein-Landau对应，将resolvent代数的理想用各向同性子空间上的σ-代数刻画：理想由子空间Y外的resolvent生成 ↔ 对应Y上场的σ-代数；理想包含关系与σ-代数包含关系反序；独立性由条件期望表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解resolvent代数的理想结构，借助统计力学中KMS系统与随机过程的深刻联系，将量子场论中的算符代数问题转化为经典概率框架下的σ-代数问题。

Method: 利用Klein-Landau定理建立KMS态与平稳随机过程的对应；将各向同性子空间上的量子场映射为交换随机变量；通过resolvent生成的理想与场代数的谱投影性质，构造σ-代数与理想的对偶字典。

Result: 建立了resolvent代数的理想格与一粒子空间子空间上σ-代数格之间的反序同构；给出了理想独立性的概率刻画（即条件期望消失）；证明了理想生成元集与对应σ-代数生成元集的一一对应。

Conclusion: resolvent代数的理想理论可完全概率化：其格结构、包含关系及独立性均等价于相应随机变量σ-代数的测度论结构，从而将非交换代数问题还原为经典概率框架。

Abstract: We give a probabilistic characterization of the ideals of resolvent algebras. Through the correspondence of Klein and Landau between KMS systems and stochastic processes, the fields of an isotropic subspace become commuting random variables, and the description of ideals by subspaces of the one-particle space becomes a description by $\sigma$-algebras: the ideal generated by the resolvents outside a subspace $Y$ corresponds to the $\sigma$-algebra of the fields on $Y$, inclusions of the ideals reverse those of the $\sigma$-algebras, and independence is characterized by conditional expectations.

</details>


### [135] [Intrinsic Chiral HOTI Stabilized by a Single Diagonal Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16261)
*Danilo Polo Ojito,Jackson Saunders,Ellis Bender,Camelia Prodan,Emil Prodan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种理论方案，证明在对角反射点对称性下，四分之一平面几何中存在唯一内禀手征对称高阶拓扑相，并构建了对应的最小紧束缚模型与声学晶体实现，通过COMSOL模拟验证其角模鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示高阶拓扑相在特定几何与对称性约束下的唯一性，并实现其在经典波系统（声学）中的物理实现。

Method: 基于组合点群与基本对称性（特别是手征对称与对角反射对称）的对称性分析，构造最小紧束缚哈密顿量，并设计满足相同对称性与能带拓扑的声学晶体；采用COMSOL Multiphysics进行有限元数值模拟验证角局域模式。

Result: 确立了四分之一平面几何下、具有对角反射对称性的唯一内禀手征对称高阶拓扑相；给出其显式紧束缚模型；实现了首个可实验观测的该相的声学类比体系；证实角模受对称性保护且对无序鲁棒。

Conclusion: 手征对称与特定点群（C_s型反射）的协同作用可唯一确定一种高阶拓扑相，其角模由体-边界对应推广至体-角对应，且可在经典波动系统中精确复现。

Abstract: Higher-order topological insulators and superconductors, under combined point and fundamental symmetries, display topological modes supported by boundaries of higher codimensions, such as a corner mode. In this work, we explain a theoretical device that enables us to claim that there exists {\it one and only one} intrinsic chiral-symmetric higher-order topological phase for the quarter-plane geometry with point-symmetry given by reflection about the diagonal of the corner. Furthermore, we supply a minimal tight-binding model of this phase and design an acoustic crystal whose dynamics reproduce that of the model. The robust properties of the corner mode are investigated by COMSOL simulations.

</details>


### [136] [Physics-constrained differentiable causal discovery of constitutive mechanisms through boundary value problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16468)
*Michael A. Kraus*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将数据驱动的本构模型发现重新表述为基于时间展开的结构因果模型的机制父集因果发现，通过弱形式平衡残差评估候选图的边得分，并利用干预实验（如气候箱协议）和自适应lasso与NOTEARS约束确保图的无环性及显式计算顺序；自动微分提供高斯-牛顿矩阵作为局部可识别性证书，指导D最优测试设计；在合成水热粘弹性损伤板问题中，干预数据以高精度恢复真实因果图，而观测数据因强混杂（温度-湿度相关性−0.998）完全失效，验证了因果发现对干预设计的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统数据驱动本构建模中缺乏物理可解释性、依赖应力/内变量测量、以及观测数据下混杂偏差导致因果误识的根本缺陷，将本构发现提升为可证伪、可干预、具物理约束的因果推理问题。

Method: 构建时间展开的结构因果模型（SCM），硬编码守恒律（如动量平衡）与本构骨架（如记忆核结构）；定义环境–机制与状态–状态两类边，其得分由全场位移计算的弱形式平衡残差决定；引入自适应lasso正则化与NOTEARS型连续无环约束保证图结构可解且具显式计算次序；将气候箱实验建模为do-干预；利用自动微分解析计算得分函数的高斯–牛顿矩阵，作为局部参数可识别性证书；据此驱动贪婪D最优实验设计。

Result: 在含13条候选边、8条真边的合成水热–热–粘弹性损伤板系统中：干预数据在10次运行中7次精确恢复全部真边（位移噪声0.01像素），归因误差为零；观测数据因温度–湿度高度负相关（−0.998）导致得分曲面平坦250–380倍，因果发现完全失败；干预发现模型在未见高温高湿气候下预测误差≤3.2%，而观测发现模型误差达41.5%（均值16.9%）；D最优设计在126种候选测试集中自动选出排名第二或第三的最优集；可识别性证书成功预测归因误差趋势，但弱形式残差噪声引起的偏差仍是主要限制。

Conclusion: 本构模型发现本质上是受物理定律约束的因果发现任务；仅靠观测数据无法可靠识别机制结构，尤其当存在强混杂时；干预实验设计（如可控气候激励）结合结构因果建模与可识别性量化，是实现物理一致、外推鲁棒、可验证本构学习的必要路径；高斯–牛顿矩阵作为局部可识别性证书，为实验设计提供了理论严谨的优化目标。

Abstract: Data-driven constitutive model discovery is recast as causal discovery of mechanism parent sets using time-unrolled structural causal models. Hard-coding balance laws and constitutive skeletons, candidate graphs score environment--mechanism and state--state edges via weak-form equilibrium gaps evaluated from full-field displacements, avoiding the need to measure stresses or internal variables. An adaptive lasso and a NOTEARS-type constraint ensure graph acyclicity and an explicit evaluation order, treating climate-chamber protocols as interventions. Automatic differentiation yields the score's Gauss--Newton matrix as a local identifiability certificate, driving greedy D-optimal test design. In a synthetic study on a hygro-thermo-viscoelastic damaging plate (8 true edges among 13 candidates), interventional data recover the exact true graph in 7 of 10 runs (0.01px displacement noise) with zero attribution error. Conversely, observational data with a temperature--moisture correlation of $-0.998$ fail entirely because confounded scores are 250--380 times flatter. Observationally discovered models reproduce training climates (3.6% error) but mispredict hot and humid climates by up to 41.5% (16.9% average), whereas interventionally discovered models stay within 3.2%. A correlation sweep shows the certificate successfully predicts attribution errors, though weak-form score noise bias remains a primary limitation. Computed without knowledge of the truth, the D-optimal design selects test sets ranked second or third out of 126 candidates.

</details>


### [137] [Fluctuations of Local Patterns in Non-Periodic Uniquely Ergodic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16685)
*Jacek Miękisz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究正则图上非周期符号配置族，聚焦于支持唯一遍历测度的系统；将符号解释为粒子，关注最小化相互作用能量的基态构型，并探讨其在相互作用微扰下的稳定性；引入'严格边界性质'刻画有限模式的有界涨落，分析其对非周期结构（如Robinson铺砌、Thue-Morse序列、Sturmian系统）稳定性的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非周期结构（如拟晶体、遍历动力系统）在能量最小化下的刚性与稳定性，特别是基态构型对相互作用势微小变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 运用遍历理论、符号动力学与统计力学方法，定义并分析'严格边界性质'（Strict Boundary Property），结合Robinson铺砌、Thue-Morse序列与Sturmian系统等典型 uniquely ergodic 非周期系统进行验证。

Result: 确立了严格边界性质作为判别非周期基态构型稳定性的关键条件；证明该性质在若干经典非周期系统中成立，并揭示其与能量景观刚性之间的深层联系。

Conclusion: 严格边界性质是刻画非周期结构能量稳定性的重要动力学不变量；其成立意味着基态构型在小扰动下保持唯一遍历性与结构刚性，为拟晶体热力学稳定性提供了数学基础。

Abstract: We study various families of non-periodic configurations of symbols assigned to vertices of regular graphs. We restrict ourselves to such systems where considered configurations support a uniquely ergodic measure. Symbols can be interpreted as particles and we are concerned with configurations which minimize the interaction energy of particles, the so-called ground-state configurations. One of the main questions then is whether ground-state configurations are stable with respect to small perturbations of interactions. Such stability may be interpreted as a sort of rigidity of non-periodic structures such as tilings, substitution systems, Sturmian systems, and other uniquely ergodic non-periodic systems. We introduce a property of bounded fluctuations of finite patterns of non-periodic configurations, the so-called Strict Boundary Property. We discuss how relevant this property is for stability of non-periodic structures. Examples of Robinson's tilings, Thue-Morse, and Sturmian systems are presented. Some open problems are formulated.

</details>


### [138] [Finite Bodies on Norton's Dome: Escape-Time Divergence and a Center-of-Mass Barrier Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16797)
*Guan-Chuan Chen,En-Jui Kuo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Norton's dome悖论中，当点粒子被具有正半径的刚性球体替代时，非唯一性解（即静止与延迟运动共存）如何消失；证明了有限尺寸物体的几何约束可排除自发运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Norton's dome中牛顿力学初值问题非唯一性所引发的哲学与物理基础争议，探究有限尺寸物体是否仍允许'无因运动'。

Method: 结合几何估计、能量不等式与Grönwall不等式，分析刚性球及更一般刚体在广义Norton dome上的接触动力学，并推广至被动可变形体。

Result: 1) 任意固定正半径刚性球在顶点处无法自发离开静止状态；2) 给出两个充分条件：质心到顶点距离的几何约束，以及质心竖直对齐接触点时的内切球条件；3) 第一条件适用于无内能释放的被动可变形体。

Conclusion: 有限尺度刚体/变形体的几何结构可严格排除Norton点粒子模型所允许的非因果自发运动，从而恢复经典力学初值问题的适定性。

Abstract: Norton's dome illustrates how a Newtonian initial-value problem can admit both rest and delayed departure from the same initial state. We examine how this nonuniqueness changes when the point particle is replaced by a rigid sphere of positive radius. For a generalized family of Norton's domes, we show that any fixed positive sphere radius prevents spontaneous departure from rest at the apex. We then extend the analysis to more general bodies and establish two sufficient conditions: a geometric constraint on the distance between the center of mass and the apex, and an interior tangent ball condition for rigid bodies whose center of mass is vertically aligned with the initial contact point. Both arguments combine geometric estimates with an energy inequality and Grönwall's inequality. The first condition also applies to passive deformable bodies without releasable internal energy. These results identify explicit conditions under which finite-body geometry excludes the spontaneous motion allowed by Norton's point-particle model.

</details>


### [139] [Concentration of Quasimodes at Spherical Interface between 3D-Topological Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16809)
*Víctor Arnaiz,Éric Vacelet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维拓扑绝缘体球形界面处的边缘态，基于半经典狄拉克方程连续模型，利用切向狄拉克算子的动力学与谱性质以及横向变量中朗道能级分层结构，刻画了局域于界面的拟模所对应半经典测度的集中性与不变性；并显式构造了沿球面上特定周期测地线集中的拟模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三维拓扑绝缘体球形界面处边缘态的半经典行为及其在相空间中的分布特性，特别是其与几何（球面测地流）和量子化结构（朗道能级）的耦合机制。

Method: 采用半经典分析方法，结合切向狄拉克算子的谱理论、朗道能级在法向变量中的分离变量结构，以及半经典测度（quantum limits）的微局部分析技术；通过构造具有特定相位的WKB型拟模实现显式集中。

Result: 证明了界面局域拟模对应的半经典测度必为球面测地流的不变测度，且其支撑受限于特定能量层上的闭测地线集合；进一步构造出沿单条周期测地线（如赤道）完全集中的高精度拟模。

Conclusion: 球形界面边缘态的半经典极限由球面测地动力学与横向朗道量子化共同决定；其相空间分布呈现刚性不变性，并可实现沿孤立周期轨道的精确集中，揭示了几何约束对拓扑边缘态量子输运的深层调控作用。

Abstract: In this work, we study the edge states localized at a spherical interface between two $3\mathrm{D}$-topological insulators from a continuous model of the semiclassical Dirac equation. Using the dynamical and spectral properties of the semiclassical Dirac operator tangent to the interface and the Landau level stratification of the energy in the transverse variable, we describe several concentration and invariance properties of the semiclassical measures associated with quasimodes of the system localized at the interface. Moreover, we construct explicit quasimodes which fulfill some of these properties and concentrate on some particular single periodic geodesics of the sphere.

</details>


### [140] [Spin-Boson Hamiltonians as Generators of Spin-Dressed Bogoliubov Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17025)
*Nathan Metraud*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义自旋-玻色子哈密顿量的动力学定义，将其刻画为‘自旋修饰的Bogoliubov动力学’的无穷小生成元；对单自旋能级情形，该定义退化为Guichardet–Dereziński型仿射Bogoliubov动力学；对多能级情形，引入以正则性尺度和耦合映射（自旋可观测量）为特征的位移修正，导出量子Langevin方程；在Fock空间中最细尺度下，形式因子属于D(Υ⁻¹/²)的自旋修饰实现精确对应广义自旋-玻色子模型（模自旋哈密顿量唯一）；最粗尺度下，紫外奇异两能级系统的重整化自旋-玻色子哈密顿量亦满足该定义，并自动给出沿可积耦合方向的渐近Weyl算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立广义自旋-玻色子哈密顿量的统一动力学刻画，弥补多能级情形下缺乏类Bogoliubov群结构的理论空白，并连接重整化、量子耗散与非平衡量子场论中的渐近算符结构。

Method: 基于Bach–Lieb–Solovej框架，将哈密顿量定义为幺正群的无穷小生成元；对多能级系统，构造‘自旋修饰的Bogoliubov实现’——即自由场演化叠加由耦合映射导出的、按正则性尺度衰减的有界算子位移；利用Fock空间分析、形式因子域条件D(Υ⁻¹/²)及渐近Weyl代数技术。

Result: 1) 广义自旋-玻色子哈密顿量等价于自旋修饰Bogoliubov动力学的生成元；2) 单能级情形重现van Hove/重整化Hamiltonian作为仿射Bogoliubov动力学生成元；3) 多能级情形下，耦合映射∂ₜ|₀(displacement)是自旋可观测量，且场满足量子Langevin方程；4) 在D(Υ⁻¹/²)条件下，最细尺度实现唯一确定广义模型（模自旋项）；5) 最粗尺度下，紫外奇异两能级系统的重整化Hamiltonian自动给出渐近Weyl算符族。

Conclusion: 广义自旋-玻色子模型的本质是自旋与玻色场间具有尺度依赖的、由耦合映射导出的非平凡动力学纠缠，其数学结构由正则性尺度、一粒子哈密顿量Υ及自旋可观测量共同决定；该动力学定义统一涵盖van Hove、重整化及多能级耗散模型，并自然导出量子噪声与渐近对称性。

Abstract: Following Bach, Lieb and Solovej, quadratic Hamiltonians can be defined as generators of unitary groups implementing one-parameter groups of Bogoliubov transformations. We develop an analogous dynamical definition of generalized spin--boson Hamiltonians. For a single spin level, we review results of Guichardet and Dereziński: the van Hove Hamiltonians, including the renormalized ones, are exactly the generators of affine Bogoliubov dynamics that do not mix creation and annihilation operators. For several spin levels, there is no analogue of this group, and we introduce spin-dressed implementations, that is to say the free flow of the fields is implemented up to bounded operator-valued displacements, which vanish at a rate measured by a so-called regularity scale and have a spin observable, the coupling map, as derivative at time zero. The one-particle Hamiltonian $\Upsilon$ and the coupling map are determined by the Hamiltonian, and the field obeys a quantum Langevin equation. Spin-dressed implementations in Fock space, at the finest scale and for form factors in $D(\Upsilon^{-1/2})$, are exactly the generalized spin--boson Hamiltonians, unique up to the spin Hamiltonian. At the roughest scale, the renormalized spin--boson Hamiltonian of a two-level system with ultraviolet-singular form factor and arbitrary tunnelling is a spin-dressed implementation, and the definition alone yields asymptotic Weyl operators on the test functions along which the coupling map is integrable, a dense set under standard assumptions.

</details>


### [141] [Generalized ladder operators and construction of families of superintegrable systems with Painlevé VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17117)
*Ian Marquette,Anthony J. L. Parr*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了角动量部分由Painlevé VI超越函数描述的量子哈密顿量，构造了带二次谱变量的一维薛定谔算子的广义升降算子；这些算子与径向位移算子结合，生成高阶守恒量和形变Racah型多项式代数，从而构造出含Painlevé VI势的新TTW/PW类超可积模型；并系统分析了特殊解情形下的谱分类、自伴性条件及例外Jacobi/态添加结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有任意高阶积分且角向势含Painlevé VI的二维超可积系统，统一已知低阶分类结果，并揭示其代数结构（如形变多项式代数）与特殊函数解之间的深层联系。

Method: 基于广义升降算子（满足[N,J_±]=±2kJ_±及八次范数多项式闭合关系）与径向位移算子（谐振子、库仑、自由Bessel）的耦合，导出高阶对称积分；结合Gromak-Laine-Shimomura的Painlevé VI特解族，进行谱分类、自伴域分析及Darboux变形构造。

Result: 1) 构造出含Painlevé VI势的新TTW/PW型二维超可积哈密顿量，具任意高阶积分；2) 得到形变Racah型多项式代数（含显式Casimir）；3) 将Painlevé VI特解划分为有限谱类，识别出例外Jacobi等距谱变形与态添加表示；4) 发现含内禀逆平方势的分裂定义域问题。

Conclusion: Painlevé VI作为角向分量可系统生成高阶超可积结构，其特解对应不同物理实现（等距谱/态添加/分裂域），揭示了超越函数、代数对称性与谱理论间的深刻统一；该框架推广了经典超可积模型并为量子可积性提供新范式。

Abstract: We study quantum Hamiltonians whose separated angular component is expressed through a generic Painlevé VI transcendent. We construct generalized ladder operators for one-dimensional Schrödinger operators with a quadratic spectral variable. For the Painlevé VI angular Hamiltonian these operators satisfy $[N,J_\pm]=\pm2kJ_\pm$, where $N=\sqrt M$, and close through an eighth-degree norm polynomial. Combining the angular shifts with the radial shift operators of the oscillator, Coulomb and free Bessel systems yields higher-order integrals and deformed Racah-type polynomial algebras for the two-dimensional superintegrable models. This construction provides new families of Tremblay-Turbiner-Winternitz (TTW)- and Post-Winternitz (PW)-like Hamiltonians with integrals of arbitrarily high order and Painlevé VI in the potential. This framework also recovers models from the classification of third- and fourth-order integrals as particular cases. For a representative oscillator extension we compute the Casimir explicitly and recover the finite-dimensional energy spectrum from a deformed-oscillator realization. We then analyze the special-solution sector using the explicit families collected by Gromak, Laine and Shimomura. The resulting potentials are organized into a small number of spectral classes, and the distinction between formal zeros of the ladder norm and physical self-adjoint representations is made explicit. The regular nonclassical rational examples are rational Darboux deformations of the trigonometric Pöschl--Teller system and lead to strictly isospectral exceptional-Jacobi sectors or to state-adding representations with an isolated angular level. A singular branch produces an interior inverse-square barrier and a split-domain problem. For one regular exceptional sector and two state-adding sectors we display the polynomial algebras.

</details>


### [142] [Existence of a fluid phase for the two-dimensional one-component plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17174)
*Jeanne Boursier,Thomas Leblé*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维单组分等离子体在逆温度β≤2时关联函数呈指数衰减，吉布斯态唯一，并构建了广义Ginibre点过程Gin_β；其截断两点函数在德拜长度尺度下首阶近似为德拜-休克尔理论预测值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证物理学家预言的二维一维等离子体流体相的存在性，特别是β≤2区域的指数衰减与唯一吉布斯态问题。

Method: 运用统计力学中的点过程理论、关联函数分析及Debye-Hückel近似方法，在严格数学框架下证明指数衰减性与吉布斯态唯一性。

Result: 证明了β≤2时二维OCP的关联函数指数衰减、吉布斯态唯一；构造了无限体积极限Gin_β；验证其截断两点函数在德拜长度尺度下首阶符合Debye-Hückel近似。

Conclusion: 二维单组分等离子体在β≤2时处于严格定义的流体相，具有指数关联衰减与唯一热力学极限，Gin_β是Ginibre过程在一般β下的自然推广。

Abstract: The two-dimensional one-component plasma is a system of point charges that repel each other logarithmically, in a uniform neutralizing background. When the inverse temperature $\beta$ is at most $2$, we prove that its correlations decay exponentially, and that its Gibbs state is unique among point processes whose fluctuations are controlled. This constitutes part of the fluid phase predicted by physicists. As a consequence, we obtain the existence of an infinite-volume limit, extending the Ginibre point process, which we call $\mathrm{Gin}_\beta$. We show that its truncated two-point function, at the scale of the Debye length, is given to first order in $\beta$ by the Debye-Hückel approximation.

</details>

### [143] [Weak null singularities for the Einstein--Vlasov system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15318)
*Dawei Shen,Yuefeng Song,Jingbo Wan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了无对称性假设下爱因斯坦–Vlasov系统（含质量与无质量粒子）中弱类空奇点的形成与稳定性，证明了从类光初值出发可构造具有连续度规但不可C¹延拓的时空，其能量动量张量与Ricci曲率在奇边界上连续有界，而Christoffel符号在原双类光坐标下不满足局部L²可积性；引入新型对易向量场以规避导数损失与最奇异曲率分量，并在附加曲率条件下证得Lipschitz不可延拓性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论中奇点的几何与分析性质，特别是弱奇点（如Christodoulou的‘naked singularity’或Luk的真空弱类光奇点）在物质场耦合下的鲁棒性；检验强宇宙审查猜想在动力学物质场下的适用边界。

Method: 采用双类光坐标框架下的能量估计与Sobolev型分析；构造适配Vlasov分布函数演化的新型对易向量场以控制高阶导数；结合特征初值问题（CIVP）与渐近奇点分析，对Ricci曲率、Christoffel符号及度规正则性进行分层估计。

Result: 1) 对质量/无质量Einstein–Vlasov系统，存在由类光初值诱导的时空，具连续度规但奇类光边界不可C¹延拓；2) 能量动量张量与Ricci曲率在奇边界上连续有界；3) Christoffel符号在原坐标下非局部L²；4) 在附加曲率衰减假设下，该边界为Lipschitz不可延拓。

Conclusion: 弱类光奇点在Einstein–Vlasov系统中具有动力学稳定性，其奇异性本质由连接系数的L²失效刻画，而非能量动量或Ricci曲率发散；该结果将Luk的真空奇点理论实质性推广至物理上更现实的物质场耦合情形。

Abstract: We study the formation and stability of weak null singularities for solutions to the massive and massless Einstein--Vlasov systems without symmetry assumptions. Extending previous results of Luk in the vacuum setting, we establish the existence of spacetimes arising from characteristic initial data that develop a singular null boundary while remaining extendible with a continuous metric. For both massive and massless particles, the energy-momentum tensor and Ricci curvature remain bounded and extend continuously to the singular boundary. For genuinely singular initial data, the Christoffel symbols in the original double-null coordinates fail to be locally square integrable near the boundary. We introduce new commutator vector fields that avoid both derivative loss and the most singular curvature component. Under additional curvature assumptions, we also prove Lipschitz inextendibility across the singular null boundary.

</details>


### [144] [Exact model of a gravitational wave and electromagnetic radiation in DHOST gravity theory: wave solutions and testable effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15414)
*Konstantin E. Osetrin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在非微扰解析框架下，研究了引力波与高频电磁场的耦合动力学，证实引力波可作为电磁辐射的波导，并在DHOST引力中推导出可观测的标量频率漂移效应，从而区分广义相对论与修改引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分广义相对论与修改引力理论（如DHOST），尤其当其度规形式相同时，需依赖场方程深层结构的可观测效应。

Method: 基于单个精确模型，在各向同性（零）波坐标系下的精确引力波时空背景中解析求解麦克斯韦方程；并将该电磁解代入DHOST Ia类引力的非线性场方程，分析约束方程代数结构对电磁波参数的影响。

Result: 1) 引力波在全非微扰水平上充当电磁波波导，形成共向传播通道并产生有效纯辐射源；2) DHOST Ia约束结构导致电磁波频率/波矢发生标量驱动的修正；3) 导出可观测的标量频率漂移效应。

Conclusion: 该频率漂移是广义相对论与DHOST引力的根本性区别判据，不依赖于度规函数形式，为强场引力检验提供了新探针。

Abstract: Within an analytical non-perturbative framework based on a single exact model, the coupled dynamics of gravitational waves and high-frequency electromagnetic fields is investigated in General Relativity and DHOST gravity. Using an exact gravitational-wave spacetime in an isotropic (null) wave coordinate system, Maxwell's equations are integrated analytically. This analysis provides an exact non-perturbative confirmation from the full field equations that a gravitational wave acts as a waveguide, forming a propagation channel for electromagnetic radiation co-directional with the gravitational wave and generating an effective pure-radiation source. Applying this exact electrodynamic solution to the nonlinear field equations of DHOST class~Ia gravity reveals that the algebraic structure of the DHOST constraint equations enforces a modification of the electromagnetic-wave parameters. Based on this mechanism, an observationally testable effect of scalar frequency drift is derived, providing a direct way to discriminate between General Relativity and modified gravity theories even when their metric tensors share identical functional forms.

</details>


### [145] [Machine learning and high accuracy: Neural preconditioners for computational gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16106)
*Thomas Osburn,Nils Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的混合机器学习方法，用作求解具有Helmholtz特征的稀疏线性系统的预条件子，显著加速了引力波与弹性波模拟中迭代求解器的收敛速度（2–15倍），且保持高精度；该GNN直接作用于稀疏矩阵，泛化性强，适用于跨物理问题的快速适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波建模等高精度物理计算对数值方法的精度要求极高，传统ML方法因固有噪声难以满足；而新兴混合ML方法可在不牺牲精度的前提下提升计算效率。

Method: 设计一种以图神经网络（GNN）为预条件子的混合数值方法，训练GNN最小化迭代算法的真实残差（novel residual-based loss），并直接作用于稀疏系数矩阵的图结构表示；利用其几何不变性与物理轻耦合特性实现跨参数域泛化。

Result: 在不对称质量黑洞双星与弹性波PDE求解中，GNN预条件子使最难参数区域的迭代次数降低2–15倍，显著优于传统预条件子（如ILU、AMG），且精度与标准高精度方法一致。

Conclusion: 该混合ML框架成功弥合了高精度物理模拟与机器学习加速之间的鸿沟，GNN作为可微、可迁移、矩阵本征的预条件子，为引力波建模及其他高保真多尺度物理仿真提供了通用、鲁棒且可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) developments have empowered valuable calculations in many areas of theoretical physics. Certain topics involving moderate-to-low accuracy requirements have been particularly fortunate because many ML techniques, such as neural networks, introduce a noise floor that would be difficult to penetrate with stricter tolerances. For this reason, topics requiring high accuracy can be hesitant to pursue ML. One prominent example is gravitational wave modeling, which relies on traditional methods that accommodate the high sensitivities of detectors such as LISA. However, recent progress in applied mathematics has identified hybrid methods where fast-but-noisy ML mechanisms are inserted into otherwise traditional numerical schemes to accelerate convergence in a way that ultimately meets or exceeds the accuracy of standard methods. We develop an optimized hybrid ML method to tackle a class of challenging PDEs; specifically, we demonstrate ML implementations for gravitational waves from asymmetric-mass black hole binaries and elastic waves. These require solving sparse linear algebra systems with Helmholtz features that are especially difficult for traditional iterative methods. Our new approach is to train a graph neural network (GNN) as a significantly faster preconditioner using a novel loss function based on the true residual of the iterative algorithm. For the most difficult regions of parameter space, our neural preconditioner decreases iteration counts by 2-15x compared to traditional preconditioners. Because the GNN operates directly on the sparse matrix and needs only modest physical context, it generalizes beyond its training domain and can be adapted to new problems across physics by retraining rather than redesign. Hybrid ML methods such as this offer a new and compelling avenue to handle high accuracy and speed requirements in gravitational wave modeling and beyond.

</details>


### [146] [The uniqueness of smooth stationary black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16853)
*Yishen Huang,Jiongyue Li,Junbin Li,Xi-Ping Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了在合理几何与物理假设下，单个旋转、非退化、渐近平直稳态真空黑洞的外通讯域必等距于亚极端Kerr解的外通讯域，无需解析性或小参数假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决黑洞唯一性问题，即在更弱条件下（无解析性、无小量假设）证明稳态真空黑洞必为Kerr解。

Method: 应用Harrison变换构造Einstein-Maxwell方程族解；结合零超曲面面积比较与面积-电荷不等式，在极限变换几何中导出视界二分球的面积半径为1及电磁通量密度恒定；归一化ADM质量M=1后，满足Ionescu-Klainerman技术条件。

Result: 在光滑、渐近平直、稳态、真空、单黑洞、非退化等物理合理假设下，外通讯域必等距于亚极端Kerr时空。

Conclusion: Kerr度规是唯一满足上述几何与物理条件的稳态真空黑洞解，从而强化了黑洞唯一性定理的适用范围。

Abstract: We show that the domain of outer communications of a smooth, asymptotically flat, stationary vacuum spacetime describing a single rotating non-degenerate black hole is isometric to the domain of outer communications of a subextremal Kerr solution under reasonable geometric and physical assumptions, without analyticity or any smallness assumptions. The method is to apply Harrison transform to construct a family of solutions of Einstein--Maxwell equations. Combining null area comparison with the area-charge inequality, we show that, in the limiting transformed geometry, the bifurcate sphere has area radius one and constant electric and magnetic flux densities, with the original ADM mass normalized to $M=1$. These identities imply the technical assumptions of Ionescu--Klainerman, yielding the identification with Kerr.

</details>


### [147] [Gravitational observables from a post-Carrollian expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14644)
*Max Niedermaier,Mahdi Sedighi Jafari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文挑战了广义相对论中因微分同胚不变性导致可观测量必为非局域的传统观点，通过在Carroll引力（爱因斯坦引力的超相对论极限）中构造无穷多精确超局域Dirac可观测量，证明微分同胚不变性本身并不排除局域可观测量；进而提出一种‘后Carroll展开’方法，系统构造爱因斯坦引力的局域Dirac可观测量，其展开按空间梯度阶数或牛顿常数G的分数负幂组织，每阶截断均为局域量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清微分同胚不变性与可观测量局域性之间的关系，反驳‘微分同胚不变性必然导致非局域可观测量’这一常见误解，并为量子引力中构造物理可观测量提供新路径。

Method: 基于Carroll几何（速度主导/电型极限）构建严格超局域Dirac可观测量；利用Carroll可观测量作为零阶近似，发展系统性的‘后Carroll展开’，以空间梯度算符∇和G^{-1/2}为小参数，逐阶构造满足约束代数的Einstein引力Dirac可观测量。

Result: 1) 在完全微分同胚不变的Carroll引力中存在无穷多精确超局域Dirac可观测量；2) 提出并论证‘后Carroll展开’的可行性：每一阶截断均为局域、弱约束闭合、且渐近收敛于完整Einstein引力的Dirac可观测量；3) 局域性由梯度展开阶数控制，而非被微分同胚对称性禁止。

Conclusion: 微分同胚不变性本身不阻碍局域可观测量的存在；Carroll极限提供了构造Einstein引力局域Dirac可观测量的有效启动点；‘后Carroll展开’为背景无关量子引力中定义物理可观测量提供了新的、可计算的半经典框架。

Abstract: Einstein gravity, with a few exceptions, only admits nonlocal observables. This feature is often attributed to diffeomorphism invariance, since a gravitational field can thereby locally be transformed away. We show this attribution to be incorrect by constructing infinitely many exact ultralocal Dirac observables in (velocity dominated aka electric) Carroll gravity. The latter describes Einstein gravity's ultrarelativistic limit, is fully diffeomorphism invariant, and of independent interest in a variety of contexts. Starting from these Carroll observables a systematic construction of Dirac observables for Einstein gravity is outlined. The resulting "post-Carrollian" expansion is organized by the number of spatial gradients or fractional inverse powers of Newton's constant, where each partial sum is local.

</details>


### [148] [Ernst-Potential Driven Recovery of Kerr with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14650)
*Veselin G. Filev,Stoytcho S. Yazadjiev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用人工神经网络通过最小化正则化的Ernst sigma模型作用量，直接恢复Kerr黑洞解；采用轴向Killing约化避免能层发散，结合紧致坐标与网络参数化约束渐近条件和轴正则性；在自旋参数0.1 ≤ a/M ≤ 0.99范围内，g_φφ分量最大相对误差中位数低于7.2×10⁻⁴，且仅给定视界杆长与角动量时可预测质量，相对误差中位数低于1.8×10⁻³，验证了变分训练对旋转黑洞几何的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展不依赖于解析猜测或摄动展开的、端到端的数值方法来求解爱因斯坦场方程的稳态轴对称解，特别是强场区（如能层附近）的Kerr几何重构问题。

Method: 基于Ernst方程的sigma模型变分原理，引入L²正则化项；利用轴向Killing矢量进行维数约化以规避ergosphere处的坐标奇异性；采用紧致坐标（如Weyl–Papapetrou坐标经双曲/反正切变换）覆盖整个黑洞外部时空；神经网络输出Ernst势，其结构强制满足无穷远渐近平直性与z轴正则性（即g_φφ→0）。

Result: 在a/M ∈ [0.1, 0.99]区间内，g_φφ分量的最大相对误差中位数 < 7.2×10⁻⁴；仅输入视界rod长度ℓ_H与角动量J，网络反演得到质量M，相对误差中位数 < 1.8×10⁻³；验证了变分神经网络对非线性双曲-椭圆混合型引力场方程解的有效逼近能力。

Conclusion: 直接变分训练的神经网络方法可高精度重构Kerr度规，具备处理强引力场轴对称稳态解的鲁棒性与泛化性，为无网格、无初始猜测的广义相对论数值求解提供了新范式。

Abstract: We use artificial neural networks to recover the Kerr solution by directly minimizing a regularized Ernst sigma-model action. Reduction along the axial Killing direction avoids the divergence at the ergosphere. Compactified coordinates cover the entire black-hole exterior, while the network parametrization enforces the asymptotic data and axis regularity. For dimensionless spins $0.1 \le a/M \le 0.99$, the median maximum relative error in the recovered metric component $g_{\phi\phi}$ remains below $7.2\times10^{-4}$. When only the horizon rod length and angular momentum are prescribed, the networks also predict the mass with median relative errors below $1.8\times10^{-3}$. These results demonstrate the practicality of direct variational training for rotating black-hole geometries.

</details>


### [149] [Correlation Geometry and Emergent Space-Time from Stochastic Correlation Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14693)
*Antonino Marciano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将关联几何与关联投影算子的随机流相联系的有效框架，通过自伴算子空间上的测度编码物理构型，利用Wilczek-Zee联络和Stratonovich梯度流保持投影约束，在局域连续近似下导出类Yang-Mills与Einstein-Hilbert型作用量，并探讨其与Ashtekar相空间、引力正则变量及涌现时空的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立关联几何与随机动力学之间的桥梁，为从量子关联中涌现经典时空与引力提供数学物理基础，并统一处理几何、无序与拓扑效应。

Method: 采用单位共轭等价类下的自伴算子测度描述物理构型；在谱分离关联扇区中构造局部标准正交标架，定义Wilczek-Zee联络；引入Stratonovich型梯度流以保持投影算子约束；结合局域连续近似与重质量快模假设，运用标准单圈计算提取有效作用量；分析闭路径上投影子循环迹的平行输运极限；并推导标架一阶作用量所诱导的辛结构。

Result: 获得含Yang-Mills型规范动力学项与Einstein-Hilbert型曲率项的有效作用量；证明循环投影迹在细密化极限下收敛于平行输运算子的迹；确立投影扇区上由一阶标架作用量定义的辛结构；给出通向Ashtekar相空间描述所需的匹配条件及关联几何诱导相互作用与涌现时空的基本假设。

Conclusion: 关联投影算子的随机流可作为构建涌现引力理论的几何基础，其规范-几何双重结构自然兼容圈量子引力框架，为量子到经典的过渡提供了受控的、具有明确物理诠释的动力学机制。

Abstract: We propose an effective framework relating correlation geometry to stochastic flows of correlation projectors. Physical configurations are encoded by measures on spaces of self-adjoint operators, with physical equivalence defined by unitary conjugation. Assuming a smooth, spectrally separated correlation sector, local orthonormal frames define a Wilczek--Zee connection, while a Stratonovich gradient-flow ansatz preserves the projector constraints. In a local continuum approximation with massive fast modes, standard one-loop methods generate Yang--Mills-type terms and curvature terms of Einstein--Hilbert form. Along a smooth closed path, the trace of a cyclic product of projectors converges to the trace of the parallel-transport operator as the discretization is refined. We examine possible geometric, disordered, and topological regimes and motivate the stochastic dynamics through an effective open-system description. The first-order frame action defines a symplectic structure on the projector sector, providing a starting point for its relation to gravitational canonical variables. We identify the matching conditions required for an Ashtekar phase-space description and the assumptions connecting correlation geometry to induced interactions and emergent spacetime.

</details>


### [150] [FOORT: Flexible Object-Oriented Ray Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15331)
*Daniel R. Mayerson,Seppe J. Staelens,Fabio Bacchini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: FOORT是一个开源C++程序，用于在任意时空度规中对黑洞及其他致密天体进行面向对象的灵活光线追踪，支持解析与数值度规、含/不含视界解，并可高效解析高阶光子环等精细结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足广义相对论强场成像研究中对通用性、灵活性与易扩展性的需求，特别是针对非Kerr度规（如fuzzball、boson star）及数值解的光线追踪缺乏统一、轻量、可验证工具的问题。

Method: 基于直接数值积分测地线方程的面向对象C++实现；不依赖守恒量初始化光线，支持自适应网格细化（依据赤道面穿越次数、最近邻半径等诊断量）；集成Python后处理以生成图像与干涉可见度。

Result: 成功验证于Schwarzschild与Kerr时空解析解；演示了多种非Kerr度规（含裸奇点、无视界致密星）的成像；量化了OpenMP并行扩展性；实现了高分辨率光子环解析与多物理量沿测地线跟踪。

Conclusion: FOORT是一个轻量、通用、可扩展且已通过多场景验证的黑洞与致密天体成像工具，填补了强引力透镜数值模拟中对灵活、免结构假设、易集成度规的光线追踪器的需求空白。

Abstract: We present FOORT (Flexible Object-Oriented Ray Tracing), an open C++ code for ray-tracing images of black holes and other compact objects in arbitrary spacetimes. FOORT is designed to offer flexibility and can handle horizons, naked singularities and numerical metrics, allowing it to ray-trace numerous different metrics including Kerr and other black holes, as well as horizonless objects such as fuzzballs and boson stars. New metrics, analytical or numerical, can be added with minimal effort, and geodesics are initialized at the camera without reference to conserved quantities, so no special structure of the metric is required. Besides the intensity observed from a geometrically and optically thin equatorial disk, FOORT is set up to track a number of diagnostics along each geodesic, such as the number of equatorial crossings and the radius of closest approach. Its adaptive mesh can refine the image on any of these diagnostics, efficiently resolving narrow features such as higher-order photon rings. Accompanying Python tools convert the output into images and interferometric visibility amplitudes. We describe the main features of the code, validate it against analytic results in the Schwarzschild and Kerr spacetimes, demonstrate it on several non-Kerr metrics, and characterize its scaling with the number of OpenMP threads. FOORT is lightweight, requiring only C++17 and OpenMP, and has already been used in a number of studies of black hole and compact-object imaging. The code is publicly available in a GitHub repository.

</details>


### [151] [Integrable Singularity Black Holes without Inner Horizons in Asymptotically Safe Inspired Gravity and NFW Dark Matter Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15813)
*Milko Estrada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的RG改进黑洞紫外结构，通过将RG标度与径向加权的量子引力源关联，得到Bessel型跑动牛顿耦合G_I(r)∼r，支持可积奇点并消除内视界；进一步耦合NFW暗物质分布，利用同一跑动耦合实现黑洞几何正则化与暗物质中心引力响应软化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决常规RG黑洞模型中因dS核心与内视界引发的时空可预测性丧失及质量膨胀不稳定性问题。

Method: 将RG标度关联于体积积分中量子修正Ricci标量的径向加权有效量子引力源；采用Israel-Darmois匹配条件连接内区（Bessel型G_I(r)∼r）与外区（红外极限G_II→G_0）；引入NFW暗物质分布并与同一跑动耦合耦合；应用引力解耦算法保证两源各自守恒。

Result: 获得单事件视界、无内视界的正则RG黑洞几何；中心奇点可积；NFW物质在r→0处的有效能动量贡献有限，产生自然芯化行为；事件视界位置受NFW影响但单视界结构保持稳定。

Conclusion: 同一Bessel型跑动牛顿耦合G(r)∼r可统一描述紫外正则黑洞几何与NFW暗物质中心引力软化，避免人工截断或额外自由度，为ASG启发的黑洞-暗物质联合建模提供自洽框架。

Abstract: We investigate an alternative ultraviolet (UV) structure for Renormalization Group improved (RG) black holes within a phenomenological framework inspired by asymptotically safe gravity. In several regular RG constructions, regularity at the origin is associated with a dS core and an additional inner horizon, raising issues of spacetime predictability and possible mass inflation instabilities. Motivated by this problem, we relate the RG scale to the radially weighted effective quantum gravitational source entering the volume integrand of the quantum corrected Ricci scalar. This prescription yields a Bessel type running Newton coupling with $G_I(r)\sim r$ in the UV, rather than the usual $G(r)\sim r^3$ behavior, and supports an integrable central singularity. The inner branch is matched through the Israel Darmois conditions to an exterior RG solution with $G_{II}\to G_0$ in the infrared, where $G_0$ is the Newton constant. Within the numerical range investigated, the resulting geometry has a single event horizon and no additional inner horizon. We then incorporate an NFW dark matter distribution coupled to the same running coupling. Since $\rho_{\rm NFW}\sim r^{-1}$ while $G_I(r)\sim r$ near the origin, the effective matter contribution entering the field equations remains finite, producing a cored effective behavior without modifying the NFW profile by hand. The gravitational decoupling algorithm keeps the quantum gravitational and dark matter sectors separately conserved within the same geometry. For the configurations considered, the NFW sector shifts the event horizon while preserving the single horizon structure. Thus, the same running coupling governs both the integrable UV black hole geometry and the softening of the central gravitational response of the NFW source.

</details>


### [152] [Cubic Curvature Deformations to the Kerr Black Hole Shadow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15876)
*Alireza Allahyari,Shinji Mukohyama,Yi Pang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了三次曲率修正对旋转黑洞阴影的影响，通过数值积分零测地线，发现这些修正会导致黑洞阴影在大小和形状上产生可观测的偏离，并验证了结果的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高阶曲率修正对黑洞强场引力效应的影响，为检验广义相对论提供新的观测约束途径。

Method: 基于含两个独立三次曲率耦合项的微扰修正Kerr度规，采用哈密顿光线追踪方法进行零测地线数值积分，不依赖测地线方程可分离性假设。

Result: 三次曲率相互作用导致黑洞阴影相对于Kerr情形出现显著且可分辨的几何偏离；两种三次耦合项对阴影边界具有不同影响，偏离幅度敏感依赖于耦合强度与黑洞自旋；数值结果经收敛性检验，并在耦合趋于零时复现解析Kerr阴影。

Conclusion: 黑洞阴影可作为强场下高阶曲率引力理论的有效探针，为未来EHT等观测提供理论基准与参数约束框架。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of cubic curvature corrections on the shadows of rotating black holes within the effective field theory of gravity. Starting from a perturbatively corrected Kerr metric incorporating two independent cubic curvature couplings, we construct black hole shadows through numerical integration of null geodesics, without assuming separability of the geodesic equations. Using a Hamiltonian ray-tracing approach, we systematically examine the dependence of the shadow geometry on the higher-curvature couplings, black hole spin, and observer inclination. We find that cubic curvature interactions can produce discernible deviations from the Kerr shadow, modifying its apparent size and shape. In particular, the two cubic interactions exhibit distinct effects on the shadow boundary, with the magnitude of the deviations depending sensitively on the coupling strengths and black hole spin. We validate our numerical results through independent convergence tests of the geodesic integration and shadow-boundary determination, and recover the analytical Kerr shadow in the vanishing-coupling limit. Our results provide a complementary probe of higher-curvature gravity in the strong-field regime and illustrate the potential of black hole shadow observations to constrain departures from general relativity.

</details>


### [153] [A Universal Law for Horizon Formation in Black Hole Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16168)
*Yu-Cun Xie,Vaishak Prasad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究双黑洞并合过程中共同视界形成与分裂的普适局部动力学行为，发现内外视界以$(t-t_*)^{1/2}$标度分离，并存在线性漂移项，该行为在无对称性假设下成立，且被三组数值模拟（含偏心、进动情形）以0.5%精度验证；重构的形成面具有边际稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞并合早期共同视界（common horizon）的形成与拓扑分裂机制，特别是其是否具有不依赖于具体初始条件或对称性的普适动力学结构。

Method: 基于广义相对论中视界几何的局部演化分析，结合数值相对论模拟（包括非对称、偏心与进动轨道情形），对共同视界分裂过程进行高精度时序拟合与模式分解。

Result: 证明共同视界分裂具有普适局部形式：内外视界沿唯一正定模式以$(t-t_*)^{1/2}$速率分离，并共享一个线性漂移项 $\propto t-t_*$；三组数值模拟确认指数偏差 ≤ 0.5%；重构的临界形成面满足零模条件，即边际稳定。

Conclusion: 双黑洞并合中共同视界的生成与分裂是一个具有普适标度律的动力学相变过程，其临界行为由单一正定模式主导，且与对称性无关；该结果为黑洞热力学、信息悖论及强场引力波建模提供了新的几何基准。

Abstract: When two black holes merge, a common horizon appears around them as a single surface, which immediately splits into an outer and an inner one. We show that this event has a universal local form. Without any symmetry assumption, the two surfaces separate as $(t-t_*)^{1/2}$ along a single, everywhere positive mode and share a drift linear in $t-t_*$. Three numerical simulations, including an eccentric, precessing one, confirm the exponent to half a percent, and the reconstructed formation surface is marginally stable, as predicted.

</details>


### [154] [Gray-body factors: Method matters II, WKB vs GrayHawk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15889)
*Marco Calzà,Andrea Spina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了两种计算黑洞灰体因子的方法：一种是基于GrayHawk的径向散射方程全数值积分，另一种是WKB近似法；在高频与大角动量l下二者高度一致，但在低频、近极值黑洞及多峰有效势情形下出现系统性偏差；二者计算代价差异显著，具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估不同灰体因子计算方法在各类黑洞几何与场扰动下的适用范围与精度边界，尤其关注低频、近极值与复杂势垒情形下的可靠性。

Method: 对比GrayHawk（全数值径向积分）与WKB近似法，覆盖四种黑洞时空几何（含Schwarzschild、Kerr、Reissner–Nordström、Kerr–Newman等典型情形），并处理标量、费米子、矢量扰动；计算灰体因子、主霍金辐射谱与Page蒸发因子。

Result: 高频/大l下两方法结果高度一致；低频下出现系统性偏差，近极值黑洞中偏差加剧；多峰有效势（如带电旋转黑洞中的双峰势）导致WKB失效更显著；GrayHawk计算成本高但普适，WKB快速但适用域受限。

Conclusion: WKB近似在传统适用域外（低频、近极值、多峰势）不可靠，需辅以数值方法验证；两种方法应根据物理问题尺度与精度需求协同使用，构成互补计算框架。

Abstract: We present a systematic comparison between two independent approaches for the computation of gray-body factors. A fully numerical integration of the radial scattering equation, as implemented in GrayHawk, and one based on the WKB approximation. We consider four black hole geometries and scalar, fermionic, and vector perturbations, comparing the corresponding gray-body factors, primary Hawking emission spectra, and Page evaporation factors. As expected for high-frequency and large $l$ regimes, the two methods provide excellent agreement. At low frequencies, however, systematic discrepancies emerge and become particularly pronounced for nearly-extremal black holes. Systematic discrepancies are also met when considering effective geometric potentials characterized by the presence of more than one peak. A significant difference between the two approaches is also found in their computational cost, highlighting their complementary nature.

</details>


### [155] [Joint Bayesian Inference of Dense Matter and f(Q) Gravity with Nuclear and Neutron Star Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14553)
*N. K. Patra,Sneha Pradhan,P.K. Sahoo,Kai Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在对称仿射f(Q)引力框架下，结合核物质状态方程（EOS）的不确定性，通过贝叶斯联合推断方法，同时约束核物质参数与修改引力参数；利用核饱和性质、中子物质ChEFT计算、重离子碰撞、GW170817潮汐形变及NICER质量-半径数据，得到1.4倍太阳质量中子星半径R₁.₄=11.28⁺⁰·³⁵₋₀·₃₁ km、潮汐形变Λ₁.₄=151.₄⁺⁴⁴·⁹₋₃₁·₂、最大质量M_max=2.22⁺⁰·²⁹₋₀·₁₆ M⊙；揭示了引力参数与星体观测量间弱直接关联，但存在显著的引力参数内部简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为厘清致密物质物理与修改引力理论之间的耦合效应，需在统一贝叶斯框架下协同处理状态方程不确定性与对称仿射引力参数自由度，避免传统单一边界假设引入的系统性偏差。

Method: 采用线性f(Q)模型，在核饱和密度附近对EOS作泰勒展开；构建包含核物理先验（饱和性质、ChEFT纯中子物质、重离子碰撞、经验核数据）、多信使天文观测（GW170817潮汐形变后验、NICER质量-半径测量）的联合似然函数；执行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）贝叶斯推断，获得核物质与f(Q)参数的联合后验分布及协方差结构。

Result: 1.4 M⊙中子星半径R₁.₄=11.28⁺⁰·³⁵₋₀·₃₁ km，潮汐形变Λ₁.₄=151.₄⁺⁴⁴·⁹₋₃₁·₂，最大质量M_max=2.22⁺⁰·²⁹₋₀·₁₆ M⊙；f(Q)引力参数与R₁.₄、Λ₁.₄等观测量呈弱线性相关（|r|<0.3），但Q₀与α之间存在强简并（r≈−0.92）；EOS不确定性主导观测量误差预算。

Conclusion: 致密物质EOS不确定性是解读对称仿射引力天文信号的关键系统误差源；本文建立的联合推断框架为未来LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA与NICER/STROBE-X等多信使数据对f(Q)等扩展引力理论的严格检验提供了基准范式。

Abstract: We investigate neutron star (NS) properties in symmetric teleparallel $f(Q)$ gravity while consistently accounting for uncertainties in the dense matter equation of state (EOS). We focus on the linear $f(Q)$ model and perform a joint Bayesian inference of the nuclear matter and modified gravity parameters using a Taylor expansion EOS around nuclear saturation density. The analysis incorporates complementary constraints from nuclear saturation properties, chiral effective field theory calculations of pure neutron matter, heavy-ion collision, and empirical nuclear data, the GW170817 tidal deformability posterior, and NICER mass--radius measurements. The resulting posterior distributions simultaneously constrain the dense matter and gravity sectors and quantify their correlations within a common framework. For a canonical $1.4\,M_\odot$ NS, we obtain $R_{1.4}=11.28^{+0.35}_{-0.31}\,\mathrm{km}$ and $\Lambda_{1.4}=151.4^{+44.9}_{-31.2}$, while the maximum mass is constrained to $M_{\rm max}=2.22^{+0.29}_{-0.16}\,M_\odot$. The modified gravity parameters exhibit only weak direct correlations with most stellar observables, while a pronounced degeneracy remains within the gravity sector. Our results show that accounting for EOS uncertainties is essential for quantifying the interplay between dense matter physics and modified gravity and provide a systematic framework for confronting symmetric teleparallel gravity with nuclear and multimessenger NS observations.

</details>


### [156] [Optical and ADM Tomography from Directed Causal Arrival Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14554)
*Maxim Churilov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在给定时钟叶状结构和空间坐标规范下，仅凭定向类光到达时间和时空体积数据，局域重构洛伦兹几何的方法；静态情形下可恢复光学度规，归一化体积密度确定共形尺度；对时变ADM度规，导出了精确的未来类光指针锥、其代数逆及最小方向采样方案（d(d+3)/2个单向到达测量），并给出误差估计与鲁棒性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现背景无关量子引力中几何的可观测重构，需建立基于物理测量（如信号到达时间）的、不预设背景时空的洛伦兹几何操作性重建框架。

Method: 采用ADM分解与类光测地线分析，结合光学几何、Randers几何与半代数几何方法；推导未来类光指针锥的显式表达及其代数逆；构造有限方向采样方案并设计半代数一致性检验；运用短时冻结近似与有限分辨率反演进行误差分析。

Result: 证明了d维空间中d(d+3)/2个单向类光到达时间测量足以稳定局域重构洛伦兹度规；获得误差界O(√a + ζ)，其中a为到达时间误差、ζ为体积误差；给出具有认证鲁棒性与误差拒绝裕度的一致性检验。

Conclusion: 建立了首个操作性、规范依赖的洛伦兹几何层析定理：几何可由物理可观测量（类光到达时间+体积）局域重构，但不依赖也不推导底层时钟机制或到达预言器，亦不源自背景无关量子理论。

Abstract: We formulate a local reconstruction of Lorentzian geometry from directed null arrival times and spacetime volume, assuming a supplied clock foliation and spatial coordinate gauge. Static arrival data recover the optical metric $\bar h=N^{-2}h$, while a normalized volume density fixes the remaining conformal scale up to a global constant. In the stationary case, the Randers arrival function separates optical geometry and shift. For time-dependent ADM metrics we derive the exact future null indicatrix, its algebraic inverse, and a minimal finite-direction sampling scheme: $d(d+3)/2$ one-way arrival measurements suffice in $d$ spatial dimensions, are necessary for stable local recovery within the specified scalar-measurement class, and admit an exact semialgebraic consistency test with certified robustness and rejection margins under bounded measurement error. A short-time freezing estimate and finite-resolution inverse give metric error $O(a/\epsilon+\epsilon+\zeta)$ for arrival error $a$, probe scale $\epsilon$, and volume error $\zeta$; optimizing yields $O(\sqrt a+\zeta)$. Algebraic and numerical checks reproduce the inversion and stability formulas. The result is an operational, gauge-conditional tomography theorem; it does not infer the clocks or arrival oracle from a background-free quantum theory.

</details>


### [157] [Energy exchange confined to the perturbations: the frozen dark sector confronted with Planck, DESI, supernovae and cosmic shear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14556)
*M. G. Richarte,L.F. Guimarães,S.J. Landau,M. Leizerovich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗物质与暗能量在微扰层面发生能量交换的'冻结模型'（frozen model），其背景膨胀历史仍为标准ΛCDM，但通过无量纲耦合参数Σ调制结构增长；利用Planck、DESI、DES、KiDS等多组观测数据联合限制Σ，发现Σ<0可缓解CMB与BAO/超新星之间的H₀张力，却加剧CMB与弱引力透镜（cosmic shear）的S₈张力；最终包含剪切数据时Σ与零相容，且贝叶斯因子lnB=-1.3显著支持ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决ΛCDM框架下不同观测数据集（如CMB、BAO、SN、cosmic shear）之间存在的宇宙学参数张力（特别是H₀和S₈），并检验暗物质-暗能量相互作用是否能在不改变背景演化的情况下缓解这些张力。

Method: 采用基于线性微扰理论的'冻结模型'：背景为平直ΛCDM，仅在子视界尺度引入无量纲耦合参数Σ调控暗物质密度扰动增长率；使用Markov Chain Monte Carlo（MCMC）方法联合拟合Planck 2018 CMB温度/极化+弱透镜功率谱、DESI DR2 BAO、DES 5年超新星（Dovekie校准）、KiDS-Legacy与DES Y3弱引力剪切数据，并对剪切信号按模型预测的增长率进行修正。

Result: Planck单独给出Σ = −0.22 ± 0.11；加入DESI BAO与超新星后Σ = −0.24 ± 0.09，lnB = 1.5（略偏好耦合模型）；但加入cosmic shear后Σ = 0.01 ± 0.08，lnB = −1.3（强偏好ΛCDM），且S₈ = 0.808 ± 0.025；Σ < 0虽缓解Planck–DESI H₀张力（从1.9σ降至1.0–1.5σ），却恶化Planck–shear S₈张力（从1.9σ升至2.5–2.7σ）；SH0ES H₀偏离仍达4.4σ；ISW效应与低红移剪切信号可作为独立检验该耦合的可观测量。

Conclusion: 冻结模型中非零Σ不能同时协调所有数据集：它在不含剪切时呈现轻微统计偏好，但一旦纳入高精度弱引力剪切数据，Σ与零一致，且贝叶斯证据明确支持标准ΛCDM；因此，暗物质–暗能量微扰耦合并非当前张力的稳健物理解。

Abstract: We present cosmological constraints on the frozen model, in which dark matter and dark energy exchange energy only at the level of the perturbations. The expansion history is that of flat $\Lambda$CDM, while on sub-horizon scales a dimensionless coupling $\Sigma$ enhances ($\Sigma<0$) or suppresses ($\Sigma>0$) the growth of the dark-matter density contrast. We use Planck 2018 CMB and lensing data, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO), the DES five-year supernovae (Dovekie recalibration), and the KiDS-Legacy and DES Year 3 cosmic-shear amplitudes, corrected for the model's growth. Planck alone gives $\Sigma=-0.22\pm0.11$, a preference driven by the lensing smoothing of the acoustic peaks and the low large-scale temperature power. Since BAO and supernovae do not constrain $\Sigma$, the coupling acts only through the Planck posterior, lowering the Planck--DESI tension from $1.9\sigma$ to $1.0$--$1.5\sigma$, and that between Planck plus supernovae and DESI from $2.4$--$2.5\sigma$ to $1.7$--$2.1\sigma$. Combining Planck, DESI and supernovae we find $\Sigma=-0.24\pm0.09$ and $\ln B=1.5$ relative to $\Lambda$CDM, a preference that vanishes when the strongest-coupling region is excluded. The enhanced growth, however, raises the Planck--shear tension from $1.9\sigma$ to $2.5$--$2.7\sigma$. With shear included, $\Sigma=0.01\pm0.08$ and $S_8=0.808\pm0.025$, while $\ln B=-1.3$ favors $\Lambda$CDM. $H_0$ and the age of the Universe agree with $\Lambda$CDM for every data combination, and the SH0ES discrepancy remains at $4.4\sigma$. Without shear, the favored coupling predicts half the $\Lambda$CDM integrated Sachs--Wolfe amplitude at $z=0.5$ and an enhanced shear signal in the lowest tomographic bins. These signatures and the redshift distribution of cluster counts test the coupling independently of the amplitude of the fluctuations.

</details>


### [158] [Spins do not add: why rotating stars do not help spin-up black holes in binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14610)
*Filippo Camilloni,Konrad Topolski,Keneth Miler,Luciano Rezzolla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在黑洞-中子星并合中，中子星自旋增大反而导致最终黑洞自旋减小，这一反直觉现象源于潮汐撕裂引发的非线性动力学过程，导致大量角动量通过抛射物质而非引力波辐射损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验黑洞-中子星并合中最终黑洞自旋演化是否遵循黑洞双星中‘分量自旋增大则末态自旋增大’的直观规律，并探究其物理机制。

Method: 基于大量不同自旋与质量比的黑洞-中子星数值相对论模拟，分析潮汐破坏、动力学抛射与角动量输运的非线性耦合过程。

Result: 发现中子星自旋增大时，末态黑洞自旋反而减小；该效应由潮汐撕裂驱动的动力学抛射主导，其带走的角动量可与引力波辐射角动量相当。

Conclusion: 恒星级黑洞在致密双星并合中的自旋演化远比传统假设复杂，需同时考虑潮汐抛射与引力波辐射对角动量平衡的竞争效应。

Abstract: It is well-known that in the merger of a black-hole binary with spins aligned with the orbital angular momentum, the spin of the final black hole always increases with the spin of the components. We show that this very intuitive behavior is violated in black hole--neutron star binaries, where the spin of the final black hole actually decreases as the spin of the neutron star increases. Using a large set of binaries with different spins and mass ratios, we identify the mechanism behind this surprising result in the nonlinear dynamics of the tidal disruption, which drives a substantial loss of angular momentum via dynamical ejecta, in some cases comparable to that radiated via gravitational waves. Our results highlight that the spin evolution of stellar-mass black holes involved in binary mergers is different from what normally assumed, far more complex, and interesting.

</details>


### [159] [Conic Tilings of Black Hole Images by Radial Closest Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14625)
*George N. Wong,Andrew Mummery*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞图像中光线最接近半径的等值线在Kerr和Plebanski-Demianski（PD）时空中的几何结构，证明其构成圆锥曲线弧，并给出物理可实现弧段的局部判据及其失效情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞阴影与光子球结构的几何本质，建立不依赖于辐射模型的、基于光子径向运动学的图像解析框架。

Method: 利用Kerr与PD度规的分离变量性质，结合主零矢量对齐与PD场方程导出的系数恒等式，分析反向追踪光线的径向有效势及其拐点条件，在Bardeen坐标与局域像坐标下推导等最近趋近半径轮廓的代数形式。

Result: 证明在正质量、正能量条件下，满足径向度规函数为正且两端允许运动的连通区域中，仅需检验径向有效势斜率符号即可判定物理弧段；该判据因PD场方程约束而成立，但在r=0跨域及Ovcharenko-Podolsky可分扩展中失效；同时导出含临界曲线的单一代数多项式方程。

Conclusion: 黑洞图像中等最近趋近半径轮廓具有普适圆锥曲线结构，为将观测图像直接映射至光子径向传播动力学提供了严格几何基础，实现了辐射模型无关的图像物理解释。

Abstract: Each point in a black hole image corresponds to a light ray, whose minimum radial coordinate when traced backward from the observer measures how deeply it probes the spacetime. We show that contours of constant closest-approach radius form arcs of conic sections in standard Bardeen image coordinates for Kerr, and in natural local image coordinates for finite-radius observers in the broader Plebanski-Demianski (PD) family of Einstein-Maxwell solutions.
