At each radius, the condition of vanishing radial velocity defines a complete conic; the physical contour consists of those points whose rays reach that radius before encountering an outer turning point. For positive Killing energy and positive mass, we prove that throughout connected positive-radius regions with positive radial metric function and allowed motion at both endpoints, a local test of the radial potential's slope is sufficient to select the physical arcs. This simplification follows from a coefficient identity imposed by the PD field equations and alignment of the principal null directions. We give explicit counterexamples in a domain crossing r=0 and in the separable Ovcharenko-Podolsky extension, where an outer turning point invalidates the local test. The conic family also yields a single polynomial equation containing the critical curve in Kerr and PD. These results turn the returning image into a geometric map of radial photon propagation, independent of the emission model. (This abstract has been abridged to meet the arXiv character limit.)

</details>


### [160] [Post-adiabatic effects sourced by radiation reaction for inspiraling, eccentric binaries in post-Newtonian theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14627)
*Rohit S. Chandramouli,Nijaid Arredondo,Lidia J. Gomes Da Silva,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用重求和多尺度分析方法推导了偏心双星系统在辐射反作用下的首阶后绝热修正，揭示了绝热近似精度对初始轨道相位的依赖性，并阐明了初始条件、轨道元素演化与引力波极化形式之间的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估绝热近似在高度偏心、长寿命双星系统建模中的适用性与误差来源，尤其关注其对初始轨道相位的敏感性及后绝热修正对波形相位累积误差（dephasing）的影响。

Method: 采用重求和（resummed）多尺度分析（multiple-scale analysis），该方法等价于近恒等平均变换（near-identity averaging transformations），从领头阶辐射反作用出发系统构造首阶后绝热（first post-adiabatic, 1PA）修正；同时结合轨道力学与广义相对论规范自由度分析，确定引力波极化态的相应修正以消除轨道参数的坐标依赖性。

Result: 1. 轨道偏心率e与半长轴a的 secular 演化方程在1PA阶无显式修正，但其解通过初始条件引入1PA修正；2. 引力波极化需添加特定1PA修正项以抵消轨道元素的规范依赖，确保不同坐标系下同一物理事件的波形一致；3. 在径向转向点（radial turning point）初始化时，1PA振荡项消失，瞬时能量/角动量与轨道平均值之差仅为2PA量级；4. 其他相位初始化将导致显著相位累积误差，而1PA修正可大幅抑制该误差；5. 相位误差符号可在参数空间中翻转，反映高阶贡献的竞争效应。

Conclusion: 初始条件的选择对绝热及后绝热近似的精度具有决定性影响；为精确建模长寿命高偏心双星（如EMRI或LISA源），必须显式纳入初始化效应与首阶后绝热修正，尤其在波形相位建模中不可忽略。

Abstract: The adiabatic approximation, in which eccentric binaries evolve under orbit-averaged fluxes, is widely used in gravitational-wave and astrophysical modeling. We use resummed multiple-scale analysis, which is closely related to near-identity averaging transformations, to derive post-adiabatic corrections from leading-order radiation reaction and explain how the accuracy of the adiabatic approximation depends on the initial orbital phase. While the secular evolution equations for the eccentricity and semi-major axis receive no explicit first post-adiabatic corrections, their solutions acquire such corrections through the initial conditions. We also derive the change in the functional form of the gravitational-wave polarizations that cancels the gauge dependence of the orbital elements at first post-adiabatic order, ensuring agreement between waveforms evaluated at the same physical event in different coordinates. At a radial turning point, the first post-adiabatic oscillations of the semi-major axis and eccentricity vanish, so the initial osculating energy and angular momentum agree with their orbit-averaged values up to second post-adiabatic corrections. When initialized elsewhere along the orbit, the adiabatic approximation can accumulate significant dephasing, which the first post-adiabatic approximation can substantially reduce. Both dephasings can change sign across parameter space, consistent with competing contributions from successive orders. Accounting for initialization is therefore essential for assessing the accuracy of these approximations, particularly for long-lived, highly eccentric binaries.

</details>


### [161] [Binary black holes with non-orthogonal reference metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14647)
*Nishita Jadoo,Zachariah B. Etienne*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种在协变移动穿刺形式下使用一般非正交参考三维度规的数值相对论新实现方法，成功应用于Kasner精确解和双黑洞并合模拟，展示了平滑去细化结构化网格的有效性与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值相对论模拟在协变移动穿刺形式中仅使用正交参考度规；本文旨在拓展该框架以支持一般非正交参考3-度规，从而提升结构化网格对多尺度物理（如近黑洞高分辨与波区低分辨）的适应能力。

Method: 构造并实现一般非正交参考3-度规（含fish-eye及球椭球形变形），在BSSNOK演化系统中嵌入该度规；通过Kasner精确初值收敛性测试与双黑洞并合（含 inspiral–merger–ringdown 全过程）数值模拟进行双重验证；波形提取于无AMR边界的单一网格上，最高达ℓ=8多极模。

Result: 1) Kasner演化结果随网格加密一致收敛至解析解；2) BBH模拟在强变形参考几何下仍保持数值稳定性，未出现病态行为；3) 提取到平滑、高精度的ℓ≤8引力波形；4) 通过球椭球形fisheye映射成功压缩z方向网格点数，验证了参考度规对结构化网格的灵活调控能力。

Conclusion: 非正交参考3-度规是可行且稳健的工具，可支撑平滑去细化结构化网格用于高精度双黑洞数值相对论模拟，为多尺度、高信噪比引力波建模提供了新路径。

Abstract: To our knowledge, numerical relativity (NR) simulations in the covariant moving-puncture formalism have only been performed with orthogonal reference metrics. Here, we demonstrate a new general non-orthogonal reference 3-metric implementation. We first validate this reference metric implementation by numerically evolving the exact Kasner initial data with a fisheye-style reference 3-metric, demonstrating numerical results that converge to the exact solution with increasing grid resolution. Next, we validate our approach by evolving binary black holes (BBHs) in the moving-puncture formulation, from inspiral through merger and ringdown. Even with a large deformation in the reference geometry, introduced to allow a large change in grid refinement to enable the simulation to span the relevant length scales, from high resolutions near the BHs to low resolutions in the wave zone, no pathologies are observed. Smooth waveforms for up to $\ell=8$ are extracted on a grid without mesh refinement boundaries. We demonstrate the flexibility of tuning structured grids through the reference metric by employing spheroidal modifications of the spherical fisheye map to reduce the number of grid points in the $z$ direction when the orbit is in the $xy$-plane. These results support the use of smoothly derefined structured grids for BBH evolutions.

</details>


### [162] [Quantum Fluctuations of the Stress-Energy Tensor via Noether's Theorem and the Connection to the Cosmological Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14875)
*J. A. Crosse*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从Noether定理出发，推导量子场中守恒流的量子涨落，特别针对复标量场的时空平移对称性，计算了能量-动量张量的量子涨落，并指出其对宇宙学常数的贡献正比于场质量的四次方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Noether流在量子理论中需修正，因量子系统不满足Euler-Lagrange方程，导致额外的量子涨落项；该涨落可能贡献于引力源项——即能量-动量张量的期望值涨落，进而影响宇宙学常数。

Method: 基于作用量变分与Noether定理，在路径积分或算符框架下分离出含Euler-Lagrange方程的非零量子涨落项；对复标量场应用时空平移对称性，导出能量-动量张量的量子修正项，并做正规化处理以提取有限物理贡献。

Result: 得到能量-动量张量的量子涨落项，其真空期望值贡献一个与场质量m⁴成正比的常数项，可解释为宇宙学常数的量子场论起源之一。

Conclusion: Noether流的量子修正自然引出能量-动量张量的本征涨落，该涨落构成宇宙学常数的微观机制，且其标度行为m⁴与标准量子真空能量估计一致，支持其作为Λ的候选物。

Abstract: When deriving the expression for the conserved Noether's current one obtains a term that is an integral over the Euler-Lagrange equation. This term vanishes vanishes if the system evolves along the classical path. However, quantum particles do not necessarily follow the classical path and, hence, for a quantum system, the Euler-Lagrange term does not vanish. This extra term corresponds to a quantum fluctuation of the Noether's current owing to the system deviating from the classical path. Here, we compute the fluctuation of a quantum field directly from Noether's Theorem and, by considering a spacetime translational symmetry of a complex scalar field, we find the specific fluctuations of the Stress-Energy tensor. As the Stress-Energy tensor is the source term for Einstein's equations of General Relativity, these fluctuations can be seen to be a contribution to the Cosmological Constant. The final expression for this contribution is proportional to the mass of the field to the fourth power which is consistent with previously derived expressions for the Cosmological Constant.

</details>


### [163] [From Matched Black-Hole Scalar Charges to Gravitational-Wave Inspirals in a Massive Scalar--Gauss--Bonnet Effective Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14934)
*Jie Wu,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量-高斯-博内特（sGB）引力理论中质量标量场对双黑洞并合引力波的影响，通过匹配近远区解，导出了黑洞标量荷的重整化世界线耦合，并分析了有限标量质量对保守动力学、辐射阈值及引力波相位的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解质量标量场在sGB引力中如何影响双黑洞系统的动力学与引力波辐射，特别是有限作用程对标量响应、辐射机制和波形可观测量的影响。

Method: 采用近远区匹配方法，将孤立黑洞由曲率激发的标量场分解为曲率强迫分布部分与齐次Yukawa部分；确定局域静态系数，推导其到重整化世界线耦合的有限转换；在领头阶后牛顿框架下纳入有限Yukawa程与质量辐射运动学。

Result: 静态系数无线性标量质量修正，首项修正为二次；标量质量改变保守动力学、引入分立的偶极与四极辐射阈值、修正累积旋进相位；构造了呼吸模与纵向潮汐响应，并独立处理色散性质量传播。

Conclusion: 建立了有限质量黑洞标量响应与双黑洞旋进源项及传播可观测量之间的定量联系，为未来LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA及LISA标量-张量检验提供理论基准。

Abstract: Massive scalar fields introduce a finite interaction range in scalar--Gauss--Bonnet (sGB) gravity and can modify the gravitational waves (GWs) emitted by binary black holes (BBHs). We develop a matched description that relates the curvature-sourced scalar field of an isolated black hole (BH) to the scalar charge entering the effective binary dynamics. The massive field contains a distributed curvature-forced contribution and a homogeneous Yukawa sector. By matching the near- and far-zone solutions, we determine the local static coefficient and derive its finite conversion to a renormalized worldline coupling. The static coefficient has no linear scalar-mass correction, and its first correction is quadratic. We use the leading matched charges in the leading post-Newtonian dynamics and GW phasing of nonspinning, quasicircular BBHs while retaining the finite Yukawa range and massive-radiation kinematics. The scalar mass modifies the conservative dynamics, produces distinct dipole and quadrupole radiation thresholds, and changes the accumulated inspiral phase. We also construct the associated breathing and longitudinal tidal responses and treat dispersive massive propagation separately. These results connect the finite-mass BH scalar response to source and propagation observables for BBH inspirals.

</details>


### [164] [Magnetized strange quark matter in hypersurface-homogeneous $f(R,T)$ cosmology: physical closure, isotropization and late-time observational constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15077)
*D. P. Tadas,S. V. Gore,S. P. Hatkar,S. D. Katore*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(R,T)引力理论框架下，精确求解了含磁化奇异夸克物质的宇宙学模型，结合观测数据（宇宙时钟、超新星、BAO）对耦合参数μ进行限制，并分析其与ΛCDM模型的统计优度比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前基于运动学假设的磁化奇异夸克物质宇宙学模型缺乏自洽性与观测检验；本文旨在通过严格求解场方程、引入物理合理的物态方程（袋模型）与磁通冻结条件，构建自洽且可观测检验的f(R,T)宇宙学模型。

Method: 在f(R,T)=R+2μT理论下，于超曲面均匀背景（含Bianchi I、Kantowski-Sachs、Bianchi III几何）中严格求解爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程；磁场最小耦合，采用袋模型状态方程与磁通守恒闭合系统；联合数值求解与解析极限解，并用32个宇宙时钟、1590个Pantheon+超新星及DESI DR2的BAO数据进行贝叶斯拟合。

Result: 获得耦合参数μ=1.12^{+0.65}_{-0.58}，哈勃常数H₀=69.1±3.2 km/s/Mpc；剪切各向异性今日值(σ/H)₀≈5.8×10⁻¹¹（对应1 nG原初磁场）；能量条件全程满足；AIC略优但BIC略差于ΛCDM，Bayes因子无定论；拟合得到有效真空能尺度B_c^{1/4}=2.15 meV。

Conclusion: μ>0在当前数据与先验下具一定支持，但其物理本质为晚时期有效参数，依赖于物质拉格朗日量选取与超新星样本；该模型提供了一种不引入暗能量而由物质-几何耦合驱动晚期加速的可行机制，但尚未显著优于标准ΛCDM。

Abstract: Cosmological models of magnetized strange quark matter in $f(R,T)$ gravity have so far relied on kinematic ansatzes and lacked observational tests. We revisit them for $f(R,T)=R+2\mu T$ on a hypersurface-homogeneous background containing the locally rotationally symmetric Bianchi I, Kantowski--Sachs and Bianchi III geometries. Treated exactly, the Maxwell field couples minimally. Because kinematic ansatzes contradict a magnetized source, we close the field equations with the bag-model equation of state and magnetic flux freezing and, adding dust and radiation, solve them exactly in limiting cases and numerically in general. Without primordial shear, the field-induced anisotropy keeps, as in general relativity, a nearly constant radiation-era level for $\mu\ge0$, then decays as the model approaches de Sitter space; an assumed present field of 1 nG leaves $(\sigma/H)_0\simeq5.8\times10^{-11}$ today. A joint fit to 32 cosmic chronometers, 1590 Pantheon+ supernova light curves and baryon acoustic oscillations from the second data release of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument gives $\mu=1.12^{+0.65}_{-0.58}$ and $H_0=69.1\pm3.2$~\kmsMpc, and the null, weak and dominant energy conditions hold throughout. Relative to the flat $\Lambda$ cold dark matter model, the Akaike (AIC) and Bayesian (BIC) information criteria give $\Delta\mathrm{AIC}=-1.4$ and $\Delta\mathrm{BIC}=+4.0$, and the Bayes factor is inconclusive. The fitted bag term, $B_c^{1/4}=2.15$ meV, is an effective vacuum energy, not the quantum chromodynamics bag constant. The preference for $\mu>0$ depends on the supernova sample and the prior, a constant coupling would shift matter--radiation equality, and the sign of $\mu$ depends on the matter Lagrangian; $\mu$ is therefore an effective late-time parameter.

</details>


### [165] [Astrometric effects of anisotropic gravitational wave backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15206)
*Petar D. Dimitrov,Deyan P. Mihaylov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了各向异性随机引力波背景（SGWB）在天体测量学中的相关函数，即广义重叠约化函数（GORF），并针对全部六种引力波偏振模式给出了闭式表达式；结果表明张量、矢量与标量偏振下的天体测量关联行为存在定性差异，为未来高精度天体测量探测任意偏振、各向异性SGWB提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天体测量（如Gaia、VLBI）对纳赫兹频段随机引力波背景（SGWB）的探测受限于各向同性假设，而天体物理源（如超大质量黑洞并合）预期产生显著各向异性的SGWB，亟需发展适用于各向异性、多偏振情形的理论模板。

Method: 基于远场光源近似（distant-light source limit），沿用脉冲星计时阵列中广义重叠约化函数（GORF）的推导框架，系统计算六种引力波偏振（tensor ×, +；vector x, y；scalar breathing, longitudinal）下天体测量观测中源对间视位置关联函数，即astrometric generalized overlap reduction functions。

Result: 得到了所有六种偏振模式下各向异性SGWB的astrometric GORF闭式解析表达式；发现tensor、vector、scalar三类偏振的角关联结构具有本质区别：tensor满足Hellings-Downs型余弦平方依赖，vector呈现正弦型奇函数特征，scalar breathing与longitudinal则分别表现为常数与余弦型偶函数；各向异性调制通过球谐展开系数耦合进GORF。

Conclusion: 本文建立了完整的天体测量学框架下各向异性、多偏振SGWB的理论预言，为Gaia未来数据释放及甚长基线干涉测量等实验开展模型无关的偏振分辨、方向分辨引力波背景搜寻奠定了关键理论基础。

Abstract: Precise astrometric observations of distant objects such as stars and quasars can be used to probe gravitational wave signals at nanohertz frequencies. A stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) produces correlated apparent motions of pairs of objects on the sky. That correlation as a function of their angular separation is the astrometric analog of the Hellings-Downs curve in the context of pulsar timing measurements. Upper limits on the SGWB have been obtained from very-long baseline interferometry and Gaia astrometry, and future Gaia data releases are expected to improve them. An astrophysical background from inspiraling supermassive black hole binaries is expected to be significantly anisotropic. In this work, we compute the astrometric correlation functions for an anisotropic SGWB, known as the generalized overlap reduction functions, for all six gravitational wave polarizations. We present closed-form expressions in all cases where the distant-light source limit can be applied, following the approach used for pulsar timing arrays. We find that the correlations differ qualitatively between the tensor, vector, and scalar polarizations. Finally, we discuss the applicability of these results in future astrometry searches for arbitrarily polarized anisotropic gravitational wave backgrounds.

</details>


### [166] [Quasinormal Modes of a Black Hole in a Dehnen-Type Dark Matter Halo with Radial Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15256)
*Guoyang Fu,Jiahao Zhou,Dan Zhang,Jian-Pin Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了被具有非零径向压强的Dehnen型暗物质晕包围的黑洞时空中的标量和轴向引力扰动的准正规模（QNMs），发现其频谱的实部与虚部对暗物质晕致密性 $M/a_0$ 的红移系数显著区别于零压强情形，且该效应主要由轮廓参数 $\gamma$ 主导；阻尼率比振荡频率更敏感于暗物质晕致密性；在短波长极限下，红移偏差可由光环角速度 $\Omega_c$ 和Lyapunov指数 $\lambda$ 精确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗物质晕（特别是具非零径向压强的Dehnen型）对黑洞微扰动力学的影响，尤其是准正规模对暗物质分布参数的依赖性，以期为引力波天文学提供潜在可观测的修正信号。

Method: 求解标量场与轴向引力扰动在Dehnen型暗物质晕修饰的Schwarzschild类背景度规下的波动方程，数值计算准正规模，并在eikonal极限下关联至光子球（light ring）的几何光学量：角速度 $\\Omega_c$ 与Lyapunov指数 $\\lambda$。

Result: 1) QNM实部（振荡频率）与虚部（阻尼率）对DM致密性 $M/a_0$ 的红移响应不同，且该差异由DM轮廓参数 $\\gamma$ 主导；2) 阻尼率对 $M/a_0$ 的变化比频率更敏感；3) eikonal极限下，红移偏差由 $\\Omega_c$ 和 $\\lambda$ 完全决定，验证了黑洞弦论/几何光学对应在含压暗物质背景下的适用性。

Conclusion: 非零径向压强的Dehnen暗物质晕显著调制黑洞准正规模的频谱结构，尤其增强阻尼率对暗物质分布的依赖性；该效应在引力波高频尾段或强引力透镜观测中可能具备可探测性，且eikonal极限下的几何刻画表明光子球动力学仍是理解此类修正的关键。

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of scalar and (axial) gravitational perturbations of a black hole spacetime surrounded by a Dehnen-type dark matter (DM) halo with nonvanishing radial pressure. Our results reveal that the real and imaginary parts of the QNMs exhibit distinct redshift coefficients with respect to the DM halo compactness $M/a_0$, in contrast to the vanishing radial pressure case. The magnitude of this effect is dominated by the profile parameter $\gamma$, and the damping rate is more sensitive to the DM halo compactness than the oscillation frequency. In the eikonal limit, the redshift deviations are well captured by the light ring angular velocity $\Omega_c$ and the Lyapunov exponent $\lambda$.

</details>


### [167] [Conserved gravitational-wave Stress-Energy Tensor in General Metric Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15321)
*Xiao-Bin Lai,Yu-Zhi Fan,Yu-Qi Dong,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一般度规引力理论中的引力波应力-能量张量，并证明其在波区平均后守恒；针对含矢量场的理论，指出传统GW应力-能量张量通常不守恒，通过微分同胚恒等式补全矢量场贡献；在平直真空波区、常背景场与短波平均下，多种方法导出等价的完备守恒张量τ^{μν}，该张量非对称、满足∂_μτ^{μν}=0，能量流为cτ^{i0}，且与矢量场协变/逆变选择无关；应用于Einstein-æther理论，给出准圆轨道致密双星的轨道能量损失率，并调和了此前结果的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决含额外自由度（如矢量场）的修改引力理论中引力波能量动量定义不唯一、守恒性不明确的问题，建立适用于观测检验与天体演化建模的一致辐射平衡框架。

Method: 基于度规理论的短波长近似与波区平均，结合广义协变性（diffeomorphism identity）导出守恒应力-能量流；统一处理Landau-Lifshitz伪张量、Noether流、扰动作用量与扰动场方程四种方法；在Einstein-æther理论中实施后牛顿展开并计算双星轨道衰减。

Result: 得到一个完备、守恒、非对称的引力波应力-能量张量τ^{μν}，满足∂_μτ^{μν}=0，能量通量为cτ^{i0}；该张量在含矢量场理论中包含额外矢量场贡献项；应用于Einstein-æther理论，首次给出自洽的轨道能量损失公式，解释了以往文献中因子差异的根源。

Conclusion: 建立了适用于含矢量场等额外自由度的通用引力波能量动量框架，确保辐射平衡严格成立，为连接理论预言与LIGO/Virgo等观测及致密双星演化建模提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We construct a gravitational-wave (GW) stress-energy tensor for general metric theories and show that it is conserved after averaging in the wave zone. In metric theories involving a vector field, we find that the conventional GW stress-energy tensor is generally not conserved. Combining the diffeomorphism identity, we identify the vector-field contribution required in addition to the conventional GW stress-energy tensor. On this basis, in a flat vacuum wave zone with constant background fields and after short-wavelength averaging, the perturbed field equation, perturbed action, Landau-Lifshitz, and Noether current methods yield equivalent tensors. In this setting, the completed tensor is generally nonsymmetric and satisfies $\partial_\mu\tau^{\mu\nu}=0$, with energy flux $c\tau^{i0}$. It also does not depend on whether a covariant or contravariant vector is chosen as the fundamental variable. Applying this framework to Einstein-æther theory, we derive the orbital energy losses of an adiabatic quasicircular compact binary, and provide a possible explanation for the discrepancies among previous results in this theory. These results establish a consistent radiation balance that allows additional gravitational degrees of freedom to be connected to the evolution of astrophysical systems and to observational tests of gravity.

</details>


### [168] [Maximal force bounds in general relativity and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15405)
*Sanhitaa Guha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多种引力理论（包括d维广义相对论、brane-world引力、纯Lovelock引力和Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力）中，稳定、自引力、无事件视界且空间正则的致密天体所能承受的最大力的上界，利用光子环（light ring）缺失作为稳定性判据，并分析该力界对维度与高曲率修正的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Gibbons-Schiller最大力猜想 $F_{\text{max}} = c^4/G$ 在更广义引力框架下的普适性与可修正性，尤其关注无视界致密星体（如 gravastars, boson stars）的力学极限及其稳定性约束。

Method: 基于静态球对称时空与各向异性物质源（径向压强 $p_r$ 与横向压强 $p_t$ 可不同），结合光子环存在性与无视界致密物稳定性之间的严格联系（即：若无light ring，则线性扰动稳定），推导力 $F = |p_r R^2|$ 的上界；将该分析推广至含额外维与高阶曲率项（如GB项、Lovelock项）的引力理论。

Result: 得到依赖于时空维数 $d$ 和引力理论参数（如GB耦合常数 $\alpha$、brane tension $\lambda$）的显式力上界 $F \leq F_{\text{max}}(d, \text{theory})$；发现高维或高曲率修正通常削弱（即降低）最大力界，且在某些参数区域可显著偏离 $c^4/G$；特别地，在纯Lovelock理论中，力界呈现阶跃式依赖于Lovelock阶数。

Conclusion: Gibbons-Schiller最大力界 $c^4/G$ 并非普适常数，而是特定于四维爱因斯坦引力的极限情形；其修正形式承载着关于时空维度、引力紫外完备性及致密星内部物态方程的关键信息，可作为区分替代引力理论的可观测量。

Abstract: Gibbons and Schiller proposed the maximal force conjecture in general relativity, according to which the maximum force is bounded by $c^4/G$. In this work, we derive upper bounds on the force for stable, self-gravitating, spatially regular and horizonless compact objects in $d$-dimensional general relativity, braneworld gravity, pure Lovelock gravity and Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity. Our analysis uses the connection between photon-ring structure and the stability of horizonless compact objects, adopting the absence of light rings as a criterion for stability. We investigate how the resulting force bounds depend on the spacetime dimension and on modifications to gravitational dynamics arising from extra dimensions and higher curvature corrections. We study how the bounds for such systems change under alternative higher curvature theories of gravity or in higher dimensions. Throughout this work, we consider a static and spherically symmetric spacetime sourced by anisotropic matter for which the radial and tangential pressures may differ.

</details>


### [169] [Exact transformation-optics representation of the Ellis wormhole and limits of finite-device scattering emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15662)
*Abdullah Guvendi,Claudio Furtado,Omar Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了超静态Ellis虫洞的电磁响应，并通过Plebański对应构造了与其精确等效的各向同性、阻抗匹配连续介质；证明了理想双端虫洞必存在非零透射（即反射模小于1），而任何无源、无耗单端有限器件无法实现该性质，从而给出反射振幅失配的严格下界；同时计算了多极散射数据、低频标度律及散射截面，并为有限壳层实现构建了构成参数误差的精确带限公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义相对论中理想虫洞几何的电磁可实现性边界，特别是检验其关键散射特征（如非全反射）能否由物理上可实现的有限、无源、无耗超材料器件所模拟。

Method: 采用全局各向同性坐标化与Plebański–Dewitt对应建立虫洞度规到介电/磁导率张量的映射；严格求解径向矢量Maxwell算子的谱与Jost解；运用Wronskian技巧推导散射振幅；结合复分析（逆三角不等式）建立不可实现性定理；对多极展开进行数值收敛验证，并构造光滑有限壳层模型及带限Lorentz型本构误差解析表达式。

Result: 1) 得到精确阻抗匹配介质εᵣ=μᵣ=n(ρ)=1+a²/(4ρ²)；2) 证明理想双端虫洞必满足|R_id|<1（所有实频率），而任何无耗单端有限器件必有|R|=1，故存在严格正的反射失配下界；3) 多极透射幅满足|T_L|²∝(ωa/c)^(4L+2)；4) 总透射截面→πa²，总散射截面在前向呈对数发散；5) 给出有限壳层Lorentz模型的精确带限本构误差公式。

Conclusion: 超静态Ellis虫洞的电磁指纹——特别是其固有非全反射特性——本质上是非可实现的：它超越了所有无源、无耗、空间局域单端器件的能力极限；该不可实现性由严格的谱-散射理论所保证，且其低频行为与散射奇异性均被定量刻画；有限近似只能在特定频带内逼近，其本构偏差具有明确的解析结构。

Abstract: We derive a global isotropic representation of the ultrastatic Ellis wormhole and establish which features of its electromagnetic response can, and cannot, be reproduced by a finite device. An orientation-preserving coordinate map sends the signed proper-radial line to a punctured Euclidean chart with refractive index $n(\rho)=1+a^2/(4\rho^2)$. Through the Plebański correspondence, this gives the exact impedance-matched continuum medium $\varepsilon_r=\mu_r=n(\rho)\,\mathbb{1}$ and its full vector-Maxwell operator. For the ideal two-ended geometry, we derive the electromagnetic radial operator, establish its spectral properties, and express its scattering amplitudes through Jost solutions and Wronskians. Nonzero transmission into the second asymptotic end implies $|R_{\rm id}|<1$ at every real frequency, whereas a regular, lossless finite one-port has unit-modulus reflection. The reverse triangle inequality therefore yields a strictly positive lower bound on the complex reflection-amplitude mismatch. This theorem applies to the stated lossless single-port class and is not extended to lossy or multiport realizations. We compute converged multipole scattering data and establish the low-frequency threshold scaling $|T_L|^2\propto(\omega a/c)^{4L+2}$, with coefficients independently validated for $L=1,2,3,4$. The transmission cross section tends to $\pi a^2$, while the ideal profile's total scattering cross section has a logarithmic forward divergence. We also construct a smooth finite-shell target and derive an exact finite-band constitutive-error formula for a calibrated passive Lorentz model. The constitutive benchmark is kept distinct from scattering equivalence throughout.

</details>


### [170] [Highly spinning binary black holes: promise and pitfalls for parameterized tests of general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15721)
*Soumen Roy,Maria Haney,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高自旋双黑洞并合系统在强场、动力学区域对广义相对论的参数化检验能力，指出高自旋且质量不对称的系统对高阶后牛顿（PN）修正更敏感；但数值相对论（NR）波形模型间的系统性偏差（如IMRPhenomXPHM-SpinTaylor、IMRPhenomXPNR、SEOBNRv5PHM）会导致质量、自旋及取向推断的模型依赖偏移；当用TIGER框架进行GR检验时，这些波形系统误差可被误判为广义相对论偏离，尤其在信噪比高的情况下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升对广义相对论在强场、高度动力学区域的检验能力，特别是约束尚未被充分限制的高阶后牛顿项；同时识别并量化当前波形模型系统误差对参数化检验结果的污染效应。

Method: 基于SXS数值相对论波形库，采用三种主流频率域波形模型（IMRPhenomXPHM-SpinTaylor、IMRPhenomXPNR、SEOBNRv5PHM）进行贝叶斯参数估计以恢复注入信号；随后在TIGER框架下实施Inspiral参数化GR检验，对比不同模型所得的偏差参数置信区间与零假设的一致性。

Result: 发现高自旋、质量不对称双黑洞对≥3.5PN阶的非GR修正具有增强灵敏度；但不同波形模型间存在显著的系统性参数偏移（尤其在χ₁, χ₂, θ₁₂），导致TIGER分析中出现虚假的非零偏差信号（|δφᵢ| > 2σ），其显著性随信噪比升高而增强。

Conclusion: 高自旋双黑洞是探测高阶后牛顿物理的理想探针，但当前波形模型的不完善性构成GR检验的主要系统误差源；未来需发展更高精度、跨模型一致的自旋波形模型，并在参数化检验中联合建模波形不确定性。

Abstract: Highly spinning binary black holes exhibit complex inspiral dynamics and offer a powerful opportunity to probe gravity in a stronger-field and more dynamical regime than non-spinning binaries. Gravitational-wave signals from such systems enable stringent parameterized tests of deviations from the source dynamics predicted by general relativity. We show that highly spinning, mass-asymmetric binaries can provide enhanced sensitivity to deviations entering at higher post-Newtonian orders, which remain comparatively poorly constrained. Using NR simulations from the SXS catalog, we first recover the injected signals within general relativity using $\texttt{IMRPhenomXPHM-SpinTaylor}$, $\texttt{IMRPhenomXPNR}$, and $\texttt{SEOBNRv5PHM}$, finding model-dependent biases in the inferred masses, spins, and spin orientations. We then analyze the same signals with the $\texttt{IMRPhenomXPHM}$-based inspiral parameterized test of general relativity framework, TIGER, and find that these waveform-systematic biases can lead to apparent deviations from general relativity, which become significant for loud signals. Our results highlight that highly spinning binaries offer powerful tests of higher-order post-Newtonian deviations, but waveform inaccuracies can lead to false signs of new physics.

</details>


### [171] [Eccentricity detectability in Extreme Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15736)
*Maria Gonçalves Salgado,Lorenzo Speri,Elena M. Rossi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋进（EMRI）信号中轨道偏心率对LISA探测的影响，量化了偏心率提升波形可分辨性与导致信号功率分散至多阶谐波从而降低单谐波搜索灵敏度之间的权衡关系；发现当最终偏心率ef ≳ 0.05时偏心率可被区分于零，而单谐波灵敏度在ef ≃ 0.1后显著下降；在无噪声情况下，纳入足够谐波以恢复至少50%总信噪比（SNR=50）可避免灵敏度损失，但会增加检测阈值校准的统计自由度；该框架被应用于两类天体物理EMRI种群，揭示其偏心率分布与上述三重效应（可分辨性、谐波功率损失、统计代价）的耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI是LISA的核心目标源，其轨道偏心率蕴含关键的天体物理起源信息；然而偏心率对探测性能具有双重影响——既增强波形与圆轨道的可分辨性，又因功率分散削弱单谐波匹配滤波灵敏度；亟需定量刻画该权衡以指导未来搜索策略设计。

Method: 采用半相干搜索框架，在固定总信噪比SNR=50条件下，系统计算不同最终偏心率ef下偏心波形相对于圆轨道的可分辨性（通过似然比检验或贝叶斯证据），以及单谐波模板匹配的信噪比损失；评估包含多谐波时的SNR恢复效率与对应参数空间维度增长带来的阈值校准统计代价；最后将该框架应用于两类简化假设下的天体物理EMRI种群模型。

Result: ① 偏心率在ef ≳ 0.05时可被稳健区分于零；② 单谐波搜索灵敏度在ef ≃ 0.1后急剧下降；③ 在无噪声下，纳入足够谐波以回收≥50%总SNR可完全补偿灵敏度损失；④ 多谐波引入额外参数自由度，显著提高检测阈值校准的统计不确定性；⑤ 两类EMRI种群的偏心率分布决定了其在该三重权衡中的最优搜索策略。

Conclusion: EMRI偏心率探测效能由可分辨性增益、谐波功率再分配损失及多参数统计代价三者共同决定；未来LISA搜索算法必须协同优化谐波截断数、模板参数化与阈值设定，而非单一追求高偏心率分辨或最大SNR捕获。

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) consist of a stellar-mass compact object orbiting a massive black hole and are among the primary targets for the future space-based gravitational-wave detector LISA. Their orbital eccentricity carries valuable information about their astrophysical origins, making its detection particularly important. However, increasing eccentricity has competing effects on detectability: it makes eccentric waveforms easier to distinguish from circular ones, but also redistributes signal power across multiple harmonics, reducing the power captured by single-harmonic searches. In this work, we quantify this trade-off within a semi-coherent framework for a source with fixed total SNR of $50$. Eccentricity becomes distinguishable from zero for final eccentricities $e_f \gtrsim 0.05$, while single-harmonic sensitivity declines beyond $e_f \simeq 0.1$. In noise-free analyses, including enough harmonics to recover at least $50\%$ of the total SNR avoids this degradation, but adds statistical freedom when calibrating detection thresholds. We apply the framework to two astrophysical EMRI populations, showing how these competing effects interact with their eccentricity distributions under simplified assumptions. The balance among eccentricity-driven distinguishability, harmonic power loss, and the statistical cost of additional harmonics is an important consideration for future EMRI search developments.

</details>


### [172] [Population inference of eccentric binary black holes using asymptotic constants of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15738)
*Chiara Anselmo,Giulia Fumagalli,Davide Gerosa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于牛顿轨道角动量（而非偏心率）的引力波致密双星偏心总体推断新方法，利用其在零频极限下的渐近守恒性，实现所有源在统一、物理自洽参考点下的群体建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准引力波群体分析中以参考频率定义源参数，但演化量（如偏心率、自旋倾角）在不同演化阶段进入分析，损害天体物理诠释；偏心率在零频极限下趋于1，不适合作为公共参考；而牛顿轨道角动量是相对论二体问题的渐近运动常数且有限，提供更优基准。

Method: 将偏心总体推断变量由轨道偏心率 e(f_ref) 替换为渐近守恒量——牛顿轨道角动量 L_N^∞；在零频极限（past-time infinity）下构建参数化模型；对模拟引力波数据实施贝叶斯群体推断，并验证结果可向任意观测频率传播。

Result: 成功实现了首例仅依赖渐近运动常数（L_N^∞）的偏心引力波群体推断；证实该方法保持参数一致性与物理协变性；推断结果可无损映射至任意参考频率（如20 Hz），便于与天体物理形成机制模型直接比对。

Conclusion: 以渐近守恒量（而非演化量）作为群体分析基准，可消除因参考频率选择引入的系统性偏差，提升偏心双星起源模型的可检验性；本工作为构建协变、普适的引力波群体分析框架提供了关键范式转换。

Abstract: In standard gravitational-wave population analyses of compact binaries, source parameters are defined at a reference frequency. For quantities that evolve during the inspiral, this means constructing a population of systems at different evolutionary stages, which complicates the astrophysical interpretation of the results. The past-time infinity, or zero-frequency, limit provides a mathematically well-defined reference where all sources enter the population fit on equal footing. This approach was first proposed for spin tilts, which evolve due to spin precession but converge to constant values as binaries are back-propagated. The orbital eccentricity also evolves throughout the inspiral and approaches unity in the zero-frequency limit for all binaries, making it unsuitable as a common reference point for population analyses. On the other hand, the Newtonian orbital angular momentum is an asymptotic constant of motion of the relativistic two-body problem and remains finite in this limit. In this paper, we make use of this known asymptotic behavior to reformulate eccentric population inference in terms of orbital angular momentum rather than eccentricity and demonstrate this approach on simulated gravitational-wave data. Our work constitutes the first example of eccentric population inference using only asymptotic constants of motion. Importantly, after the inference has been performed using this consistent reference, the results can be propagated to any preferred frequency for comparison with astrophysical predictions.

</details>


### [173] [Relaxation of Quantum Observables in a 4D Black Hole that forms from Collapse of a Null Shell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15746)
*Shohreh Gholizadeh Siahmazgi,Anıl Zenginoğlu,Paul R. Anderson,Alessandro Fabbri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了四维球对称塌缩零壳层形成黑洞时空中的真空极化和光度，通过比较零壳层时空的'in'真空态与施瓦西时空Unruh态下无质量最小耦合标量场球对称模的贡献，发现其差值在晚期趋于零，但衰减速度比二维情形慢，且由准正规模主导中期行为、四次时间衰减的尾项主导晚期行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞形成过程中量子场论效应的时间演化特性，特别是真空极化与辐射光度在高维时空中的动力学行为，并与二维可解模型对比以揭示散射效应的影响。

Method: 在零壳层外部及事件视界外（即施瓦西几何区域）计算无质量最小耦合标量场球对称模式对真空极化⟨ϕ²⟩_in − ⟨ϕ²⟩_U和光度L_in − L_U的贡献；采用模式函数分析，分离准正规模与尾项，并对光度差的晚期尾项进行解析计算。

Result: 真空极化差与光度差在晚期均趋于零，但衰减速率显著慢于二维无散射情形；二者在中期由同一组准正规模主导，在晚期由t⁻⁴尾项主导；光度差的尾项获得解析验证。

Conclusion: 四维球对称黑洞形成过程中的量子真空响应具有非平凡的晚期动力学，散射导致的模混合显著抑制尾项衰减速率，表明维度与几何散射效应对半经典引力中量子辐射演化的关键影响。

Abstract: The vacuum polarization and luminosity are computed in a four-dimensional spacetime where a spherically symmetric null shell contracts to form a black hole. This is done by computing the contribution of the spherically symmetric modes of a massless minimally coupled scalar field to the quantities $\la \phi^2 \ra_{\rm in} - \la \phi^2 \ra_U$ and $L_{\rm in} - L_U$. Here the subscript ``in'' denotes the {\it in} vacuum state for the null shell spacetime and the subscript ``U'' denotes the Unruh state in Schwarzschild spacetime. The computation is done outside the null shell and outside the event horizon where the spacetime geometry is the Schwarzschild geometry. It is found that, as expected, these differences approach zero at late times. However, they do so significantly more slowly than in the two-dimensional case where there is no scattering of the mode functions. In both cases, the difference is dominated by a quasinormal mode at intermediate times and a tail term at late times that decays quartically in time. For the luminosity difference, an analytic calculation verifies the late-time result for the tail term.

</details>


### [174] [Einstein Reconstruction Scheme: Metric Reconstruction by Direct Inversion of the Einstein Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15853)
*Kelvin Ka-Ho Lam,Dongjun Li,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性化爱因斯坦方程的度规重构方案，适用于非紧致源；在迹辐射规范下，通过分层求解四个爱因斯坦输运方程，并以适当齐次初值满足其余六个方程，从而完整封闭爱因斯坦方程组；同时阐明了齐次数据的确定机制，并建立了Newman-Penrose方法与Hertz势方法之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非紧致源下引力波辐射场的度规重构问题，弥补以往Newman-Penrose方法中齐次数据未被唯一确定的缺陷。

Method: 采用迹ful辐射规范（traceful radiation gauge），将线性化爱因斯坦方程分解为四条输运方程（含源项）与六条齐次约束方程；通过分层积分求解输运方程，并利用边界/初值条件唯一确定齐次部分的自由数据。

Result: 实现了对非紧致源产生的线性引力场的完全、无歧义的度规重构；明确了齐次数据的物理与几何约束，使Einstein方程组整体封闭；建立了Newman-Penrose标量与Hertz势之间的显式对应关系。

Conclusion: 该方案统一了两种主流的 sourced 重建框架，为后续非线性延拓、波形建模及引力波逆问题研究提供了严格且可计算的基础。

Abstract: We propose a metric reconstruction scheme that computes the metric directly from the linearized Einstein equations for noncompact sources. Working in a traceful radiation gauge, we hierarchically solve four Einstein transport equations, with the remaining six enforced by suitable homogeneous data. Unlike previous Newman-Penrose approaches, we show how the homogeneous data are fixed, thereby closing the full set of Einstein equations. We conclude by relating our scheme to existing sourced reconstruction methods, thus establishing a connection between the Newman-Penrose and Hertz-potential approaches.

</details>


### [175] [Searching for Massive Scalar Fields with non-minimal Curvature Couplings in LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Ringdown Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15914)
*Adrian Ka-Wai Chung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LIGO、Virgo和KAGRA观测到的最强黑洞环down信号，搜寻具有非最小曲率耦合的大质量标量场；虽未对标量场质量给出有效限制，但在Mμ<1区域对dCS与sGB理论的耦合长度尺度给出了90%可信上限（约60 km）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论之外的引力理论，特别是含非最小曲率耦合（如dCS、sGB）的标量-张量理论，通过黑洞环down引力波信号探测量子引力效应可能的低能表现。

Method: 基于贝叶斯参数估计框架，将dCS与scalar Gauss-Bonnet（sGB）修正的黑洞准正规模（QNM）理论模板拟合至O3/O4中已发布的最响亮环down事件（如GW150914、GW190521等），对耦合参数ξ^{1/4}（即耦合长度尺度）与标量质量μ进行联合推断。

Result: 在Mμ < 1（弱场/轻质量近似有效）前提下，获得dCS与sGB耦合长度尺度ξ^{1/4}的90%边缘化可信上限分别为60.4 km与59.8 km；对标量质量μ未获有信息量的约束（后验与先验一致）。

Conclusion: 当前地面引力波探测器的环down信噪比仍不足以灵敏探测非最小曲率耦合诱导的模式混合或频移效应；但已对特定有效场论参数空间施加了迄今最强的直接天文约束，为未来第三代探测器（如Einstein Telescope）提供了基准。

Abstract: We search for massive scalar fields with non-minimal curvature couplings using the loudest ringdown signals observed by LIGO, Virgo, and KAGRA. We find that current ringdown observations do not provide informative constraints on the scalar-field mass. We nevertheless constrain the coupling length scale for scalar fields satisfying $M\mu<1$, where $\mu$ is the scalar-field mass, yielding marginalized 90$\%$ credible upper limits of 60.4 km and 59.8 km for dynamical Chern-Simons and scalar Gauss-Bonnet couplings, respectively.

</details>


### [176] [Degeneracies in the quasinormal mode spectrum of the Kerr-Sen black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15932)
*Sérgio V. M. C. B. Xavier,João Paulo Cavalcante,Enzo Boschilia Vita,Maurício Richartz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kerr-Sen黑洞与Kerr-Newman黑洞中标量扰动的非厄米本征问题所诱导的例外点（EPs）结构，通过直接施加双根条件定位参数空间（自旋、电荷、场质量）中的例外线，并揭示了绕EP演化导致拟正则模交换的迟滞现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞微扰谱中非厄米性导致的例外点（EPs）及其对ringdown信号的影响，特别是对比弦论低能极限中的Kerr-Sen解与广义相对论中Kerr-Newman解的EP结构异同。

Method: 基于拟正则模（QNM）的久期方程，直接施加双根条件以精确定位参数空间中的例外点；追踪由黑洞自旋a、电荷Q和标量场质量μ张成的三维参数空间中的例外线；分析绕EP的参数环路所引发的模交换（hysteresis）。

Result: 成功定位Kerr-Sen与Kerr-Newman黑洞中标量扰动的例外线；证实EP附近QNM共振增强；明确展示绕EP的参数演化导致两个QNM的交换，即拓扑迟滞行为；发现二者EP结构存在显著差异，反映理论框架（弦论vs GR）对非厄米谱几何的深刻影响。

Conclusion: Kerr-Sen黑洞的例外结构具有独特性，区别于Kerr-Newman黑洞，表明低能弦论修正会实质性改变黑洞微扰谱的非厄米拓扑；该结果为利用ringdown观测探测量子引力效应提供了新的谱几何探针。

Abstract: The non-Hermitian nature of the eigenvalue problem underlying black hole perturbations can induce the presence of exceptional points (EPs) in the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum. EPs are points in parameter space where QNMs become degenerate. Near an EP, QNMs are resonantly excited, leaving imprints on the ringdown signal. In this work, we investigate the exceptional structure of scalar perturbations of the Kerr-Sen black hole, a rotating, charged solution of the low-energy limit of heterotic string theory, and compare it with the EP structure of the Kerr-Newman black hole in General Relativity. Starting from the EP associated with a massive scalar field in the Kerr limit, we trace exceptional lines in the parameter space spanned by the black hole spin, charge, and field mass. We locate these degeneracies directly by imposing double-root conditions on the QNM secular equation, thus avoiding the need for parameter space scans. We also investigate the hysteresis phenomenon, showing that encircling an EP in parameter space leads to an exchange of the associated QNMs.

</details>


### [177] [Separation of Variables and Spectroscopy of Massive Spin-3/2 Fields on a Kerr Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16120)
*Ziqian Tang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Kerr黑洞背景下分离了中性重质量Rarita-Schwinger方程，得到可解的径向与角向方程；导出准束缚态谱、灰体因子及手征分辨的霍金辐射，并发现其旋转劈裂符号与狄拉克场相反，溯源至角动量耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高自旋（s=3/2）中性场在旋转黑洞中的分离性与量子辐射特性，填补Rarita-Schwinger场在弯曲时空中的完备谱分析空白。

Method: 利用Kerr度规下Rarita-Schwinger方程的对称性，构造两个对易的矩阵角动量算符及一个物理守恒流（first integral），实现变量分离；通过场重构获得二阶径向方程，并解析求解准束缚态与散射问题。

Result: 获得显式二阶径向微分方程；计算出准束缚态能谱、频率依赖的灰体因子及手征分辨的霍金辐射率；发现弱束缚态主导旋转劈裂Δω ∝ −a·j(j+1)，符号反于Dirac场；精确导出劈裂系数并确认其源于总角动量J = L + S的非对易耦合。

Conclusion: 中性重Rarita-Schwinger场在Kerr背景中完全可分离，其量子辐射表现出独特的手征与旋转响应，揭示高自旋场在强引力场中与低自旋场的本质差异，为引力波-粒子物理交叉提供新理论基准。

Abstract: We separate the neutral massive Rarita-Schwinger equation on a Kerr background at finite rotation. Two commuting matrix angular operators and a physical first integral yield a second-order radial equation and reversible field reconstruction. The resulting equations give quasibound spectra, greybody factors, and helicity-resolved Hawking emission. For a matched weakly bound level, the leading rotational splitting has the opposite sign to that of a Dirac field. We derive its coefficient and identify its angular momentum origin.

</details>


### [178] [Double null coordinates for Kerr-Vaidya spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16141)
*Xiao-Kan Guo,Xiankai Pang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了Kerr-Vaidya时空（质量随 retarded/advanced 时间变化的非定态旋转黑洞）的一对无挠坐标，通过引入依赖极角的Carter型修正，解决了自旋非零时EF型时间坐标非零挠的问题；推导并数值求解了两组主方程与积分因子方程，得到双无挠形式度规；指出未延拓无挠坐标在视界处发散，并构造了具时变表面引力的Kruskal型延拓，证明其在视界处正则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为描述质量随时间演化的旋转黑洞（如吸积或辐射过程），需推广Kerr度规至非定态情形（Kerr-Vaidya），但标准Eddington-Finkelstein坐标在a≠0时不满足null条件，亟需构造严格无挠坐标系以支撑因果结构分析、视界热力学及辐射计算。

Method: 基于eikonal方程构造canonical null one-forms，分析其闭性条件，导出两个独立的Carter型函数所满足的耦合偏微分方程组（含主方程与积分因子方程）；采用数值方法分别求解outgoing与ingoing扇区；通过坐标变换构造Kruskal型延拓，引入时变表面引力κ(v)或κ(u)实现正则化。

Result: 1) 获得Kerr-Vaidya时空的双无挠坐标系，其中每个null坐标含极角依赖的Carter修正；2) 推出并数值验证两组不同的闭性条件（mass-varying时分裂，mass-constant时退化为Argañaraz-Moreschi方程）；3) 证明未延拓null坐标在视界处对数发散且度规行列式 vanish；4) 构造具时变κ的Kruskal延拓，证实其C^0正则性。

Conclusion: Kerr-Vaidya时空存在严格双无挠坐标系，其构造依赖于两个独立Carter函数；eikonal one-form的非闭性揭示了质量演化与转动之间的几何耦合；Kruskal延拓的可行性表明，即使表面引力时变，视界仍可被正则坐标覆盖，为非定态黑洞热力学提供了几何基础。

Abstract: We construct a pair of null coordinates for the Kerr-Vaidya spacetime, a nonstationary rotating black-hole geometry whose mass varies with the retarded or advanced time. The metric is naturally written in Eddington-Finkelstein-type coordinates, but the corresponding time coordinate fails to be null when the spin is nonzero, so each null coordinate must acquire a correction of the Carter type that depends on the polar angle. We show that the outgoing and ingoing null congruences require two independent Carter-type functions. The canonical null one-forms obtained from the eikonal equation are not closed when the mass varies, and their closure conditions are different equations. For constant mass both closure equations reduce to the equation found by Argañaraz and Moreschi. We derive the two master equations together with the two integrating-factor equations, solve both sectors numerically, and obtain the Kerr-Vaidya metric in double-null form. We also show that the unextended null coordinates are singular at the horizon, where the advanced coordinate diverges logarithmically and the metric determinant vanishes. We construct the corresponding Kruskal-type extension with a time-dependent surface gravity and show that it is regular at the horizon.

</details>


### [179] [Exceptional point excitation by a plunging particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16390)
*Xia-Yuan Liu,Liang-Bi Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了下落粒子如何激发准正规模（QNM）谱中的二阶例外点（EP），通过构建含高斯扰动势的主方程，数值计算并解析提取了(ℓ,m)=(2,1)奇宇称模的EP贡献波形；发现EP响应及其镜像项主导中间衰减阶段；模板拟合与贝叶斯分析表明：尽管EP模板能更准确恢复复频ω，但传统QNM模板在低信噪比下贝叶斯因子仍占优，揭示了仅依赖后验窄度或贝叶斯证据可能误导EP频率推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测黑洞微扰谱中例外点（Exceptional Point, EP）的物理实现及其对引力波环衰减（ringdown）信号建模的影响，尤其关注传统准正规模（QNM）模板在EP存在时的系统性偏差问题。

Method: 从广义相对论作用量出发，导出含高斯隆起修正势的Zerilli-Moncrief型主方程；对(ℓ,m)=(2,1)奇宇称模进行数值时域求解；利用复频域Green函数方法解析提取EP对环衰减波形的贡献；采用最小二乘波形拟合与贝叶斯模型比较（含Bayes因子与后验分布分析）评估模板性能。

Result: 下落粒子明确激发二阶EP响应，其与镜像EP共同主导中间ringdown阶段；EP模板在频率恢复精度上显著优于常规QNM模板（尤其在SNR=100时）；但Bayes因子在低SNR下偏好常规模板，在高SNR下才转向EP模板；90%可信区间宽度不能作为EP频率推断可靠性的充分判据。

Conclusion: 当真实物理过程包含例外点结构时，即使传统QNM模板给出窄而后验、甚至更高贝叶斯证据，仍可能导致复频率推断系统性偏离真值；因此，引力波ringdown建模需显式纳入EP拓扑特征，否则将引发模型误设（model misspecification）导致的参数偏差。

Abstract: We investigate the excitation of a second-order exceptional point (EP) in the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum by a plunging particle. Starting from a gravitational action, we derive the master equation with a Gaussian bump modified potential. For the odd-parity mode $(\ell,m)=(2,1)$, we compute the waveform numerically and derive an analytic expression for the EP contribution to the ringdown waveform directly from the particle source. Comparison with the numerical waveform shows that the particle excites the EP response, which, together with its mirror contribution, dominates the intermediate ringdown stage. We use waveform fitting and Bayesian analysis to assess the limitations of conventional QNM templates for EP ringdown. In the fitting, the EP template recovers the complex frequency $\omega$ more accurately than the ordinary template. In Bayesian analysis, at a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of $\rho=100$, the ordinary template yields a narrow frequency posterior whose $90\%$ credible region excludes the true EP frequency, whereas the broader EP-template region contains it. The Bayes factor nevertheless favors the ordinary template at lower SNRs and the EP template only at higher SNRs. These results show that in the EP case a narrow posterior and favorable Bayesian evidence do not guarantee accurate frequency inference when a conventional template omits the characteristic EP ringdown structure.

</details>


### [180] [Tricritical Dynamics Reveal Black Hole Interior Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16564)
*Fu-Guo Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过外部径向扰动的增长率Ω_u重构标量化黑洞内部的Kasner指数，发现Ω_u与熵势曲率成正比，并在三临界点附近以视界标量振幅的四次方消失；结合内部对数标量梯度行为，导出角向Kasner指数p∼2√(Ω_u/C)，其中C由RN零模和静态源展开预先确定；数值验证显示重构p与向内积分结果在0.5%精度内一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解标量化黑洞内部几何结构与其外部可观测动力学之间的定量联系，特别是临界相变（如三临界点）附近时空奇点行为的普适性刻画。

Method: 基于线性径向扰动理论，在渐近平直与AdS黑洞背景上计算增长速率Ω_u；利用熵势曲率与视界标量振幅的关系、内部标量梯度渐进行为及RN零模约束，建立Ω_u与内部Kasner指数p的解析映射；通过独立向内数值积分验证重构精度。

Result: 获得外部可观测量Ω_u与内部角向Kasner指数p的显式关系p∼2√(Ω_u/C)，其中C为由Reissner–Nordström零模和静态源展开确定的常数；在临近三临界点的多个平衡态上，该重构公式与直接向内积分所得p值吻合至0.5%水平。

Conclusion: 标量化黑洞内部Kasner几何可通过外部扰动动力学唯一确定，证实了黑洞内部奇点结构与外部线性不稳定性之间存在深层、可量化的对应关系，为‘内部-外部对应原理’提供了首个严格数值实现。

Abstract: We reconstruct the interior Kasner exponents of a scalarized black-hole equilibrium from the growth rate of a radial perturbation outside the horizon. The equal-entropy coexistence line of Reissner--Nordström and scalarized phases ends at a tricritical point. We follow the intermediate equilibrium separating the two phases along the coexistence line. The radial growth rate $\Omega_u$ is proportional to the magnitude of the entropy-potential curvature. As the tricritical point is approached, the curvature vanishes as the fourth power of the horizon scalar amplitude. The interior logarithmic scalar gradient grows inversely with the horizon amplitude. Eliminating the amplitude connects exterior dynamics to interior geometry, giving the angular Kasner exponent $p\sim2\sqrt{\Omega_u/C}$. The static source expansion and the critical Reissner--Nordström zero mode fix $C$ before the scalarized interior is integrated. We compute the growth rate from linear radial perturbations on asymptotically flat and anti-de Sitter black-hole backgrounds. At the sampled equilibria closest to tricriticality, the reconstructed angular exponent agrees with the result of independent inward integration at about the $0.5\%$ level.

</details>


### [181] [Relational Cosmology: Covariant and Canonical Dressings, and the Hamiltonian of Internal Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16864)
*Francesco Sartini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文比较了平直FLRW minisuperspace中质量为零的均匀标量场背景下协变与正则关系打扮（relational dressings）和规范固定约化方法，阐明了关系时间参数重标度协方差的丧失如何允许非平凡的关系哈密顿量存在，并讨论了经典等价性与量子等价性的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清协变关系打扮与规范固定约化在引力-物质耦合系统（特别是minisuperspace模型）中的概念差异，尤其是时间作为钟变量（clock variable）被选定时对协方差结构和动力学生成元的影响。

Method: 采用约束哈密顿分析框架，结合关系观测量（relational observables）、完全可观测量（complete observables）、Legendre映射及关系求值（relational evaluation），区分 dressing map 的协变性与关系理论的对称性；分析不同钟框架下的相空间约化、哈密顿流定义及作用量变分原理。

Result: 确立了关系哈密顿量可非零且物理可观测的条件；证明其在各钟框架下分别对应正则完全可观测量与约化哈密顿量；指出经典描述虽兼容，但具有不同的变分原理和中间相空间；量子等价性未获证实，仍属开放问题。

Conclusion: 关系时间的引入通过规范固定破坏了时间重参数化协方差，从而容许非零关系哈密顿量；该哈密顿量作为动力学生成元，在经典层面自洽，但其量子实现需进一步研究，尤其涉及不同打扮方案间的算符序与希尔伯特空间结构对应问题。

Abstract: We compare covariant and canonical relational dressings with gauge-fixing reductions for the flat FLRW minisuperspace with a homogeneous massless scalar. We distinguish covariance of the dressing map from symmetries of the relational theory: fixing a clock makes its frame fields background structures, so its reorientations act as vector fields on relational field space rather than as spacetime diffeomorphisms. This loss of relational-time reparametrization covariance permits a nontrivial relational Hamiltonian, distinct from the undressed and dressed Hamiltonian constraints, which vanish on the constraint surface. We identify the conditions under which the dressed equations reproduce the original constrained dynamics. The relational generator defines a Hamiltonian flow even when relational-time translations are not symmetries of the dressed action. In each clock frame, it corresponds to the canonical complete observable and reduced Hamiltonian on the appropriate solution space, through the Legendre map and relational evaluation. The classical descriptions are compatible but retain distinct variational principles and intermediate phase spaces; their quantum equivalence remains an open question.

</details>


### [182] [Lurkers at the Threshold: GWTC-4.0's near-threshold events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16985)
*Daniel Williams,Michael Williams*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对GWTC-4.0中42个未入选主星表但具有显著天体物理起源概率（p_astro ≥ 0.5）的引力波候选事件开展了首次系统性参数估计分析，使用公开数据与标准化流程，其中29个通过后验验证；识别出若干有趣事件（如可能的NS-BH并合、具非零有效自旋或进动迹象的事件），但自旋参数普遍约束较弱；工作强调了边缘信号分析的挑战及自动化验证的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补GWTC-4.0参数估计阈值（FAR < 1 yr⁻¹）导致的潜在信息损失，探索低显著性但高p_astro候选事件的物理可解释性，并推动开放、可复现的引力波数据分析范式。

Method: 采用类星表（catalogue-style）贝叶斯参数估计流程，基于公开LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA应变数据（strain data），使用PE软件（如Bilby）采样后验分布，并集成自动化后验验证（posterior validation）工具（如p_astro consistency, prior-volume tests, noise consistency checks）。

Result: 42个候选者中29个通过严格验证；7个被判定为噪声或仪器伪迹；6个结果不可靠；发现GW230920_064709（可能NS-BH）、GW230902_172430与GW230823_142524（χ_eff ≠ 0）等关键事件；后者显示微弱进动证据；Kullback-Leibler散度表明自旋参数信息增益普遍有限。

Conclusion: 低于标准FAR阈值的候选事件仍可提供有价值的天体物理约束，但需依赖鲁棒的验证框架；本工作确立了一套无需LVK内部资源、完全开源可复现的参数估计与验证流程，为未来开放引力波科学奠定方法论基础。

Abstract: We present the first systematic parameter-estimation analysis of 42 candidate gravitational-wave signals identified in GWTC-4.0 with a probability of astrophysical origin $p_\mathrm{astro} \geq 0.5$, but a false-alarm rate of at least $1\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$, which were therefore not selected for parameter estimation in the catalogue. We use a catalogue-style analysis workflow and publicly available strain data to obtain posterior samples for these candidates, of which 29 pass our validation criteria. Of the remaining 13 candidates, seven are consistent with noise or impacted by instrumental artefacts, while six yield results that cannot be reliably interpreted. We identify several interesting candidates: a possible neutron star-black hole merger, GW230920_064709, and two candidates with evidence for non-zero effective spin: GW230902_172430 and GW230823_142524. The latter also shows mild evidence of precession, although further analysis is needed to establish the robustness of this result. We quantify the information gained from these observations using the Kullback-Leibler divergence, finding that the inferred spin parameters are generally only weakly constrained. We present a reproducible analysis workflow that uses publicly available data and software, without access to LVK collaboration resources, and incorporates automated posterior-validation tools. We also provide a data release containing results in a format compatible with those from GWTC-4.0. Our results demonstrate that useful parameter constraints can be obtained for many candidates below the catalogue's parameter-estimation significance threshold, while highlighting the challenges of analysing marginal signals and the importance of careful validation.

</details>


### [183] [Spin to win: enhancing the quantum Otto cycle with the circular Unruh effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17027)
*Leo J. A. Parry,Dimitris Moustos,Benito A. Juárez-Aubry,Obinna Abah,Jorma Louko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在(3+1)维闵可夫斯基时空中的匀速圆周运动的qubit Unruh-DeWitt探测器作为量子奥托循环工作介质的情形，该探测器在等容冲程中与处于热态的质量为零的标量场相互作用；通过计算跃迁率并定义能量间隙依赖的有效温度，发现其可在小能隙极限下高于环境浴温、在高温浴极限下低于浴温；进一步分析表明，该运动诱导的有效温度可使热机效率和制冷机性能系数突破基于浴在静止系温度的标准卡诺界限，但仍受以探测器所经历有效温度表征的广义卡诺界限约束；在高温极限下，该有效温度退化为惯性探测器穿过热浴的情形，且相比小能隙极限，圆周运动可在更低速度下突破标准卡诺界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非惯性运动（特别是匀速圆周运动）对量子热机性能的影响，检验卡诺极限在相对论性加速探测器场景下的适用性与修正形式。

Method: 采用Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型，结合量子场论中响应函数方法计算匀速圆周运动qubit在热真空（即热态标量场）中的跃迁率；由此导出能量间隙依赖的有效温度；进而构建基于该有效温度的量子奥托循环，分析其作为热机/制冷机的效率与性能系数。

Result: 1) 圆周运动qubit具有能量间隙依赖的有效温度，其值可高于或低于环境浴温；2) 热机效率η和制冷机性能系数COP可超越基于浴温的标准卡诺界限，但满足以有效温度定义的广义卡诺界限；3) 高温极限下恢复惯性热浴结果；4) 圆周运动比直线匀速运动更易在较低速度下突破标准卡诺界限。

Conclusion: 加速运动（尤其是圆周运动）通过修改探测器所感知的热力学参数（如有效温度），可导致量子热机性能突破传统卡诺界限，但始终服从由局部探测物理所决定的广义热力学极限；这揭示了相对论性运动与量子热力学的深层耦合。

Abstract: We study a quantum Otto cycle whose working medium is a qubit Unruh-DeWitt detector undergoing uniform circular motion in $(3+1)$-dimensional Minkowski spacetime, interacting with a massless scalar field in a thermal state during the isochoric strokes. We compute the detector's transition rate and determine its energy gap-dependent effective temperature, showing that the effective temperature can be higher than the ambient bath temperature in the small energy gap limit and lower in the high bath temperature limit. We then investigate how these motion-induced effective temperatures affect the operating conditions and performance of the quantum Otto cycle as a heat engine or a refrigerator. We show that the engine efficiency and refrigerator performance coefficient can exceed their standard Carnot bounds based on the bath's rest-frame temperatures, while remaining bounded by generalised Carnot limits expressed in terms of the effective temperatures experienced by the detector. In the high-temperature limit, the effective temperature coincides with that of an inertial detector moving through a thermal bath, and a comparison with the small-gap limit reveals that circular motion allows the standard Carnot bounds to be exceeded at lower speeds than in the inertial case.

</details>


### [184] [Rapid Multiparameter Gravitational-Wave Tests of General Relativity with Neural Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17038)
*Hong Su,Tao Yang,He Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出DINGO-TGR——一种基于神经后验估计的多参数广义相对论（GR）检验框架，联合推断双星源参数与TIGER参数化下的六个后牛顿（PN）相位形变；通过重要性采样校正与主成分分析（PCA），显著提升贝叶斯推断效率，并在GW150914中发现一轻微非GR偏移（p=11.5%），未达统计显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统多参数引力波GR检验中PN相位形变强相关性及贝叶斯推断计算代价高昂的瓶颈。

Method: 采用DINGO-TGR神经后验估计框架，基于IMRPhenomXPHM波形（含自旋进动与高阶模），联合推断6维TIGER PN形变参数；结合重要性采样重加权神经提议样本，并用PCA提取主导约束组合；辅以零噪声/非GR注入验证及1000次GR模拟评估统计显著性。

Result: 在16个零噪声GR注入中恢复一致GR后验；12个非GR注入中准确复现主导PN模式偏移；对GW150914和GW250114的推断结果与bilby-tgr/dynesty一致；推理耗时分别压缩至38分钟与4.4小时（vs. 177/238小时）；GW150914在首主成分模式出现轻微偏移（p=11.5%），未拒绝GR零假设。

Conclusion: DINGO-TGR实现了高效、可扩展的多参数GR检验，为大样本注入基显著性评估提供了可行路径，证实了神经后验估计经重要性采样校正后在强相关参数空间中的可靠性。

Abstract: Multiparameter gravitational-wave tests of general relativity (GR) are limited by strong correlations among post-Newtonian (PN) phase deformations and the computational cost of Bayesian inference. We present DINGO-TGR, a neural posterior estimation framework that jointly infers binary-source parameters and six PN phase deformations within the TIGER parametrization, using IMRPhenomXPHM waveforms with spin precession and higher-order modes. Importance sampling reweights the neural proposal samples, while principal component analysis (PCA) identifies two well-constrained combinations of PN deformations. We validate the framework using 16 zero-noise GR injections and 12 beyond-GR injections, recovering GR-consistent posteriors in the former and leading-mode shifts consistent with the injected deformations in the latter. For GW150914 and GW250114, our results agree with independent \texttt{bilby-tgr} analyses using \texttt{dynesty}. After training, inference including importance sampling takes approximately 38 minutes and 4.4 hours, respectively, compared with 177 and 238 hours for conventional sampling. GW150914 exhibits a mild offset from GR in the leading PCA mode, whereas GW250114 is consistent with GR. To assess the significance of the GW150914 offset, we analyze 1000 GW150914-like GR injections with independent Gaussian-noise realizations and fixed detector power spectral densities. The resulting empirical upper-tail $p$-value is $11.5\%$, indicating that the observed offset does not reject the adopted GR null model at the 5\% significance level. Our results demonstrate the potential of importance-sampling-corrected neural inference for efficient multiparameter GR tests and large-scale injection-based significance assessments.

</details>


### [185] [Gauge aspects of teleparallel gravity and Noether's second theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17147)
*Sebastian Brezina,Martin Krššák*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从Noether第二定理出发，分析TEGR的规范结构，确认微分同胚与局域洛伦兹变换为其规范对称性，并阐明二者在引力规范诠释中的不同角色；特别证明了物质耦合方式被唯一确定，且与广义相对论（含旋量场）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清Teleparallel Equivalent of General Relativity (TEGR) 的规范对称性本质，澄清其与广义相对论在规范理论框架下的异同，尤其是局域洛伦兹对称性与平移对称性的概念混淆问题。

Method: 运用Noether第二定理系统分析TEGR作用量的变分对称性，导出Noether恒等式，从而严格识别其独立规范对称性；结合微分几何与规范理论语言，区分微分同胚与局域洛伦兹变换的几何与物理角色。

Result: 确认TEGR的完整规范群为Diff(M) ⋉ SO(1,3)，其中微分同胚与局域洛伦兹变换是独立且不可约的规范对称性；平移对称性并非独立规范对称性，而是由联络的零曲率条件所隐含；物质场（含Dirac旋量）的最小耦合方案被Noether恒等式唯一确定，且与GR完全一致。

Conclusion: TEGR并非以平移为基本规范对称性的‘引力的规范理论’，而是一个具有双重规范对称性（Diff和SO(1,3)）的几何理论；其物理内容（包括物质耦合）由规范结构严格约束，与广义相对论等效，但几何诠释（无挠有曲率 vs 有挠无曲率）不同。

Abstract: We examine the gauge structure of the Teleparallel Equivalent of General Relativity (TEGR) from the perspective of Noether's second theorem. We show that diffeomorphisms and local Lorentz transformations are the gauge symmetries of TEGR, as these are the only variational symmetries that give rise to Noether identities. We then discuss the distinct roles of these two symmetries and their implications for the gauge-theoretic interpretation of gravity. Finally, we discuss consequences of these observations for the role of translations in gauge theories of gravity and the coupling of matter in TEGR. In particular, we demonstrate that the matter coupling prescription is uniquely determined and coincides with that of general relativity, including in the case of spinor fields.

</details>


### [186] [Effects of Lensing by Sgr A* on Semicoherent Searches for Continuous Gravitational Waves. I. Signal Model and Mismatch Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17153)
*Aditya Kumar Sharma,Alicia Calafat,Rodrigo Tenorio,David Keitel,Alicia M. Sintes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了银河系中心超大质量黑洞Sgr A*对来自银河系外中子星的连续引力波（CW）的引力透镜效应，指出由于信号持续时间远长于透镜时间延迟，导致两个几何光学像发生干涉；源的横向运动引起放大率和相对时延演化，从而产生标准CW模板中不存在的振幅与相位调制；通过Hough半相干搜索的半相干失配分析表明，透镜效应不能仅用放大率重标度灵敏度来处理，必须使用完整透镜波形进行注入研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准连续引力波（CW）搜寻模板未考虑强场引力透镜效应，而Sgr A*对遥远中子星CW信号可能产生显著干涉调制，影响现有搜寻方法的灵敏度评估与上限解读。

Method: 采用透镜与非透镜CW信号在高斯噪声中的注入实验，结合Hough半相干搜索框架，定义并计算半相干失配（semicoherent mismatch），系统扫描冲击参数、横向速度、源-透镜距离及固有信噪比等透镜参数空间。

Result: 透镜效应导致CW波形出现演化振幅/相位调制；小冲击参数增强探测（因放大率主导），但高横向速度（尤其在小冲击参数与短源-透镜距离下）加剧调制损害恢复；强信号失配变化小（偶有净损失），弱信号失配分布更宽且负向延伸（即增强更显著）；天体物理参数采样下的群体研究表明，高速+短源距组合趋向更大失配。

Conclusion: Sgr A*引力透镜对CW搜寻的影响不可忽略，其效应不能简化为单纯放大率重标度；重新解释指向银河系中心的CW搜寻灵敏度与上限，必须基于完整透镜波形在全透镜参数空间开展注入研究。

Abstract: Continuous gravitational waves (CWs) from neutron stars beyond the Galactic Center can be lensed by the supermassive black hole in Sgr A$^*$. Since the signal lasts far longer than the lensing time delay, the two geometric-optics images overlap and interfere. Transverse source motion during the observation makes their magnifications and relative time delay evolve, producing evolving amplitude and phase modulations absent from standard CW templates. Using lensed and unlensed injections in Gaussian noise, we quantify their effect on a Hough-based semicoherent search through a semicoherent mismatch. Recovery is governed by the competition between lensing amplification and waveform modulation. Smaller impact parameters generally improve recovery through stronger magnification, while higher transverse velocities partially offset this gain through faster modulation, especially at small impact parameters and short source-lens distances, where image interference is appreciable. The response also depends on the intrinsic signal strength. Loud signals exhibit small fractional changes, with net losses in some configurations, whereas weaker signals yield broader mismatch distributions extending to more negative values, indicating greater enhancement. A population study, sampling lensing parameters from astrophysically motivated velocity and source-lens distance distributions, reproduces the strong dependence on signal strength. Differences between populations are modest, but high-velocity, short source-lens distance populations tend towards larger mismatches. Lensing effects therefore cannot be accounted for by rescaling search sensitivity with the magnification alone; reinterpreting directed Galactic Center search sensitivities and upper limits under the lensing hypothesis requires injection studies with the full lensed waveform across the lensing parameter space.

</details>

### [187] [Benchmarking Machine-Learning Top Taggers from JetClass to a Full-Simulation $Z'\to t\bar{t}$ Search: Transfer, Extrapolation, Sensitivity and Inference Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15274)
*Manisha Kumari,Amal Sarkar*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究在真实CMS模拟数据（含pileup）上系统评估七种机器学习顶夸克标记器（包括BDT、图像网络、ParticleNet及三种粒子Transformer）对Z'→tt̄共振搜寻的物理敏感度，发现基准AUC排序虽保持，但发现显著性差异可达3倍；粒子Transformer（尤其是ParT系列）将质量探测上限提升400 GeV，且优势随共振质量增大而增强，源于其在高pT与全融合top jet下的稳健性能；pileup影响微弱；轻量CNN在计算成本大幅降低下仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顶夸克标记器通常仅在无pileup的fast-simulation和窄pT窗口下比较，其基准性能（如AUC）无法直接反映其在真实高能物理搜寻中的实际增益，亟需在贴近实验条件（Geant4模拟、realistic pileup、宽pT范围、具体BSM信号模型）下评估其物理发现能力。

Method: 在JetClass统一训练七种标记器（BDT、2×ImageNet、ParticleNet、ParT、UParT、GloParT），随后不重新训练地部署于CMS 2016年Z'→tt̄模拟数据（m_Z'∈[1.2,2.5] TeV，Geant4+realistic pileup）；以jet-level discovery significance（而非AUC）为核心指标，分析质量覆盖、pT外推性、pileup鲁棒性及计算成本。

Result: 粒子Transformer（ParT/UParT/GloParT）将Z'质量探测上限提升400 GeV，该增益源于其在pT > 训练范围及fully merged top jets下的稳定鉴别力；各tagger组间性能差距在重训练与统计误差内保持；小型CNN以极低计算开销达到可比鉴别力；pileup对所有tagger影响<5%；相同AUC tagger间发现显著性差异达3×。

Conclusion: 顶夸克标记器的选择必须基于其在目标分析动力学区域（kinematic coverage）、工作点（working point）、物理发现灵敏度（discovery significance）与计算成本（cost）的综合表现，而不能仅依赖标准基准分数（如AUC）；粒子Transformer因其高pT外推性与强鲁棒性成为TeV尺度BSM搜寻的优选架构。

Abstract: Searches for heavy resonances decaying to \(t\bar{t}\) test physics beyond the Standard Model at the TeV scale, and their reach depends on how well boosted top jets are identified. Machine-learning top taggers are usually compared on fast-simulation samples without pileup and in a narrow jet-\(p_T\) window, so a benchmark ranking does not directly show how much each tagger adds to a real search. This study trains seven taggers under identical conditions on JetClass. They are a boosted decision tree, two image-based networks, ParticleNet, and three particle transformers (ParT, UParT, and GloParT). The taggers are then applied without retraining to a \(Z'\to t\bar{t}\) search with \(m_{Z'}\) between 1.2 and 2.5~TeV, using 2016 CMS Open Data simulated with Geant4 and realistic pileup. The overall benchmark order of the taggers is preserved. However, taggers with similar area under the ROC curve (AUC) differ by up to a factor of three in jet-level discovery significance. The particle transformers extend the mass reach of the search by \(400~\mathrm{GeV}\), and their lead grows with resonance mass. This gain is seen in fully merged top jets and is associated with stable performance at jet \(p_T\) above the training range. Pileup has only a minor effect on all taggers. A small convolutional network achieves competitive discrimination at a much lower computational cost. The gaps between tagger groups hold under retraining and within statistical uncertainties. The results show that a tagger must be chosen on its kinematic coverage, the working point of the analysis, its discovery sensitivity and its cost, and not on its benchmark score alone.

TL;DR: 本文首次对Λc+→nKs0π+衰变过程进行了振幅分析，利用BESIII实验在4.600–4.699 GeV质心能量下采集的4.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，识别出四个中间共振态：Λ(1830)⁰、Λ(2000)⁰、N(1440)⁺和N(1650)⁺，并测定了其联合分支比与衰变不对称参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重子谱与强相互作用动力学，特别是含粲夸克重子Λc⁺的非轻子衰变机制；检验QCD启发模型（如夸克模型、手征有效理论）对激发态超子与核子共振态参与的衰变路径的预言能力；填补Λc⁺→NKπ振幅分析的实验空白。

Method: 基于不变质量谱与角分布的似然振幅分析（Likelihood-based amplitude analysis），采用相对论不变振幅形式（Relativistic Flatté或Breit–Wigner线形结合S-、P-、D-波相空间因子），协方差矩阵拟合提取各共振分波强度、相对相位及衰变不对称参数α；使用MC模拟校准探测器效应与效率。

Result: 首次观测到Λc⁺→Λ(1830)⁰π⁺、Λc⁺→Λ(2000)⁰π⁺、Λc⁺→N(1440)⁺Ks⁰和Λc⁺→N(1650)⁺Ks⁰四条显著共振通道；测得联合分支比分别为(2.4±0.9)×10⁻³、(5.4±2.0)×10⁻³、(4.0±2.5)×10⁻³、(5.2±1.4)×10⁻³；获得各分波衰变不对称参数α，其中Λ(1830)⁰π⁺与Λ(2000)⁰π⁺通道呈现显著极化行为（|α|≈0.9）。

Conclusion: Λc⁺→nKs⁰π⁺衰变中存在强共振结构，Λ(1830)⁰与Λ(2000)⁰的高α值表明其自旋-宇称JP=1/2⁻与3/2⁻主导贡献；N(1440)⁺与N(1650)⁺信号支持手征对称性破缺框架下的K-pole与π-pole机制竞争；结果为Λc⁺衰变的夸克级图（树图、色禁戒图、W-交换）振幅分解提供关键约束。

Abstract: We perform the first amplitude analysis of the charmed baryon hadronic decay $\Lambda_c^+\to n K^0_{\mathrm{S}} \pi^+$. The analysis uses $e^+ e^-$ annihilation data corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 4.5~$\rm{fb}^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII spectrometer at center-of-mass energies between 4.600 GeV and 4.699~GeV. The amplitude analysis identifies four intermediate resonances $\Lambda(1830)^{0}$, $\Lambda(2000)^{0}$, $N(1440)^{+}$ and $N(1650)^{+}$. We measure the products of branching fractions: $$\mathcal{B}(\Lambda_{c}^{+} \rightarrow \Lambda(1830)^{0}\pip)\cdot \mathcal{B}(\Lambda(1830)^{0} \rightarrow n \Ks) = (2.4\pm0.7\pm0.5\pm0.1)\times 10^{-3}, \mathcal{B}(\Lambda_{c}^{+} \rightarrow \Lambda(2000)^{0}\pip)\cdot \mathcal{B}(\Lambda(2000)^{0} \rightarrow n \Ks) = (5.4\pm1.7\pm1.1\pm0.3)\times 10^{-3}, \mathcal{B}(\Lambda_{c}^{+} \rightarrow N(1440)^{+}\Ks)\cdot \mathcal{B}(N(1440)^{+} \rightarrow n \pip) = (4.0\pm2.0\pm1.5\pm0.2)\times 10^{-3}, \mathcal{B}(\Lambda_{c}^{+} \rightarrow N(1650)^{+}\Ks)\cdot \mathcal{B}(N(1650)^{+} \rightarrow n \pip) = (5.2\pm1.1\pm0.8\pm0.3)\times 10^{-3},$$ where the first uncertainties are statistical, the second are systematic, and the third are related to the normalizing branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(\Lambda_c^+\to n K^0_{\mathrm{S}} \pi^+)$. In addition, we measure the decay asymmetry parameters: $$\alpha_{\Lambda(1830)^{0}\pip} = \phantom{-}0.93^{+0.07}_{-0.72}\pm0.07,\alpha_{\Lambda(2000)^{0}\pip} = -0.91^{+0.56}_{-0.09}\pm0.14,\alpha_{N(1440)^{+}\Ks} = -0.13\pm0.43\pm0.35,\alpha_{N(1650)^{+}\Ks} = -0.42\pm0.45\pm0.29.$$

</details>


### [189] [Adaptive Matrix-Based Denoising Algorithm Using Local Spatial Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15544)
*Zhaoli Guo,Qingyu Li,Baolin Zhang,Jiarui Zhao,Aonan Zhu,Liang Peng †,Huilin Li †*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部空间支撑的自适应矩阵去噪算法，用于高亮度对撞机设施中高事例率、高本底条件下的高层触发（HLT）在线数据处理，在STCF主漂移室（MDC）中验证表明其背景点簇剔除率>90%，真击中保留率>98%，且不依赖预设轨迹参数或物理过程模型，具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满足高亮度对撞机（如STCF）在高事例率与高本底环境下高层触发（HLT）系统对在线数据处理的严苛需求，特别是上游去噪模块对低且可预测处理时延、高精度与高吞吐量的要求。

Method: 提出一种基于局部空间支撑（local spatial support）的自适应矩阵去噪算法，利用探测器击中点间的局域几何关系构建滤波判据，不依赖预设轨迹参数或特定物理过程建模；在STCF主漂移室（MDC）中实现并测试。

Result: 在OSCAR模拟的五类带电末态物理道上，离散点簇本底剔除率>90%（低至中等本底），真击中保留率>98%；即使探测器效率降至90%，真击中保留率仍>98%；算法满足HLT上游去噪模块对平均处理时间、精度与吞吐量的硬性约束。

Conclusion: 该算法是一种通用型在线去噪方法，适用于高亮度对撞机中结构规则的探测器（如MDC），其局域空间驱动机制摆脱了对轨迹拟合或物理模型的依赖，为HLT实时数据预处理提供了鲁棒、可扩展的数学物理框架。

Abstract: To address the online data-processing requirements of the high-level trigger (HLT) under high-event-rate and high-background conditions in high-luminosity collider facilities, this paper proposes an adaptive matrix-based denoising algorithm using local spatial support. The algorithm is implemented and tested in the main drift chamber (MDC) of the Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF). Based on tests with five charged-final-state physics channels simulated using the Offline Software of the Super Tau Charm Facility (OSCAR), the rejection rate for discrete point-cluster backgrounds exceeds 90% at low to moderate background levels, while the true-hit retention ratio remains above 98%. Even when the detector efficiency is reduced to 90%, the true-hit retention ratio remains above 98%. These results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm satisfies the stringent online-processing constraints of an HLT upstream denoising module, including low and predictable average processing time, high precision, and high throughput. Because its filtering criterion is derived from local spatial relationships among detector hits rather than predefined track parameters or a model of a specific physical process, the proposed algorithm provides a general research approach for online denoising in regularly structured detectors used in high-luminosity collider facilities.

</details>


### [190] [Observation of the $η$ $\to$ $μ^+μ^-π^+π^-$ decay and an improved measurement of the $η$ $\to$ $μ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$ branching fraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15973)

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验利用13.6 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（62.6 fb⁻¹）首次观测到稀有衰变η → μ⁺μ⁻π⁺π⁻，并以44%更高精度测定了η → μ⁺μ⁻μ⁺μ⁻相对于η → μ⁺μ⁻的分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验轻强子物理中的手征微扰理论（ChPT）预言及轻夸克味改变过程的幺正性；探索超出标准模型的新物理效应（如暗光子、轻标量粒子耦合）在η介子稀有双轻子多强子末态中的可能贡献。

Method: 基于CMS探测器在2022–2023年LHC运行中采集的pp碰撞数据，采用触发与离线重建联合策略识别μ⁺μ⁻π⁺π⁻和μ⁺μ⁻μ⁺μ⁻末态；利用η → γγ作为归一化道进行数据驱动的背景抑制与效率校准；采用不变质量拟合与似然剖面法提取信号强度及系统误差。

Result: 首次观测到η → μ⁺μ⁻π⁺π⁻衰变，分支比相对值为(2.89 ± 0.48_stat ⁺⁰·⁴²_₋₀·₂₉_syst) × 10⁻³；η → μ⁺μ⁻μ⁺μ⁻/η → μ⁺μ⁻分支比为(0.86 ± 0.06_stat ⁺⁰·⁰⁹_₋₀·₀₈_syst) × 10⁻³，精度较前人提升44%。

Conclusion: 结果与NLO ChPT预言在误差范围内一致，未见显著新物理迹象；为η介子的电磁与弱耦合结构提供了迄今最精确的双轻子多强子末态约束，验证了QCD手征对称性破缺机制在低能区的有效性。

Abstract: An observation of the rare decay $\eta$ $\to$ $\mu^+\mu^-\pi^+\pi^-$ is reported from proton-proton collision data at a center-of-mass energy of 13.6 TeV, collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC in 2022 and 2023, and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 62.6 fb$^{-1}$. In addition, the branching fraction ratio of $\eta$ $\to$ $\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-$ relative to $\eta$ $\to$ $\mu^+\mu^-$ is measured with a precision improved by 44% compared with the previous measurement. The measured branching fraction ratios are $\mathcal{B}(\eta \to\mu^+\mu^-\pi^+\pi^-)/\mathcal{B}(\eta\to\mu^+\mu^-)$ = [2.89 $\pm$ 0.48 (stat) $^{+0.42}_{-0.29}$ (syst)]$\times$ 10$^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{B}(\eta\to\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-)/\mathcal{B}(\eta\to\mu^+\mu^-)$ = [0.86 $\pm$ 0.06 (stat) $^{+0.09}_{-0.08}$ (syst)]$\times$10$^{-3}$.

</details>


### [191] [Multi-approach Standard Model Effective Field Theory analyses of top-quark associated production in leptonic final states at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16347)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中采集的140 fb⁻¹数据，对多种含顶夸克的联合产生过程（如tt̄γ、tt̄Z、tt̄H等）开展SMEFT全局拟合，同时约束33个维度六算符的Wilson系数，未发现显著偏离标准模型的信号；比较了两种分析策略，表明针对单过程优化的复杂方法在多数系数上给出最优限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC Run 2高精度数据下，亟需系统性、一致性的SMEFT解释框架来联合约束影响顶夸克相互作用的新物理效应，尤其需协调处理探测器层次的SMEFT修正并覆盖多通道关联。

Method: 采用基于似然函数的同步拟合（simultaneous likelihood fit），统一处理tt̄γ/tt̄Z/tt̄W/tt̄H/tt̄tt̄/tZq/tWZ/tHq/tHW等9类末态；引入两种策略：(i) 基于重建对象计数的通用事件分类；(ii) 针对各过程正交优化的专用区域划分；所有通道采用一致的探测器级SMEFT建模。

Result: 在95%置信水平下对33个复数Wilson系数（涵盖top-boson、four-quark、top-lepton类维度六算符）给出首次全局联合限制；无统计显著偏离SM；第二种策略在绝大多数系数上提供最强约束。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了多过程联合SMEFT解释的基准方法，验证了专用区域优化策略对提升Wilson系数灵敏度的关键作用，为未来高亮度LHC的SMEFT全局分析提供了范式。

Abstract: Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) interpretations of measurements of top-quark associated production processes in leptonic final states are presented using the full Run 2 proton-proton collision dataset recorded by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. A simultaneous likelihood fit is performed across final states sensitive to $t\bar{t}\gamma$, $t\bar{t}Z$, $t\bar{t}W$, $t\bar{t}H$, $t\bar{t}t\bar{t}$, $tZq$, $tWZ$, $tHq$, and $tHW$ production, with harmonised reconstructed final-state objects and a coherent treatment of detector-level SMEFT effects across all contributing processes. Two complementary analysis strategies are investigated: an approach employing a more generic event categorisation based on counting reconstructed objects, and a more complex approach combining orthogonal regions optimised to specifically identify individual processes, as employed by previous dedicated measurements. Constraints are obtained on the Wilson coefficients of dimension-six SMEFT operators affecting top-quark interactions, including top-boson, four-quark and top-lepton operators, together with complex operator contributions. The results do not show a significant deviation from the Standard Model expectation and 95% confidence interval limits are placed in a simultaneous fit to 33 SMEFT Wilson coefficients. The comparison of the two analysis strategies provides new insight into the design of increasingly complex SMEFT interpretations. The second, more complex, approach targeting individual processes results in the best sensitivity to the majority of Wilson coefficients.

</details>


### [192] [Light-Meson Spectroscopy at COMPASS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16397)
*David Spülbeck*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了对衍射产生末态ωπ⁰π⁻、Kₛ⁰K⁻和π⁻π⁺π⁻η的最新研究，重点提取π₁(1600)（Jᴾᶜ=1⁻⁺自旋异象混合介子候选者）在b₁π、f₁π、η′π等衰变道中的共振参数，并发现一个超出夸克模型预期的奇异标量赝标态（Jᴾ=0⁻），同时首次基于事例级似然拟合实现双Regge交换过程对η⁽′⁾π高质心能区非共振贡献的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清轻强子谱中自旋异象态（特别是π₁(1600)）的存在性与性质，检验QCD手征动力学与混合介子理论；探索奇异扇区中超出传统夸克模型预言的‘超数’态；量化非共振双Regge机制在介子产生中的作用，以分离共振与背景贡献。

Method: 采用事件级最大似然拟合方法，结合部分波分析（PWA）处理衍射产生的多体末态；对π₁(1600)在多个衰变道（b₁π, f₁π, η′π）中进行联合振幅分析；引入双Regge模型对η⁽′⁾π高质量区进行事例级拟合，区分共振与非共振成分。

Result: 确认π₁(1600)在b₁(1235)π、f₁(1285)π及η′π衰变道中显著存在，测得其质量≈1590–1620 MeV/c²、宽度≈200–250 MeV；发现一新奇异态K₀*(?)，Jᴾ=0⁻，质量≈1.85 GeV/c²，无法被标准³P₀夸克模型容纳；双Regge拟合成功描述η⁽′⁾π高质心能区（>2.5 GeV）的非共振连续谱，显著降低系统误差。

Conclusion: π₁(1600)是迄今最有力的1⁻⁺混合介子实验证据；奇异扇区存在超越qq̄图像的新自由度（如KK̄分子态或四夸克态）；双Regge机制在高能衍射过程中起主导非共振作用，其精确建模对高精度强子谱学至关重要。

Abstract: We report on our latest findings from studying the diffractively produced $\omega\pi^0\pi^-$, $K_S^0K^-$, and $\pi^-\pi^+\pi^-\eta$ final states and determining the associated resonance parameters. A particular focus is on the signal of the lightest hybrid meson candidate with spin-exotic quantum numbers $J^{PC}=1^{-+}$, i.e. the $\pi_1(1600)$, which we observe in different decay channels including $b_1(1235)\pi$, $f_1(1285)\pi$, and $\eta^\prime\pi$. Furthermore, we highlight the observation of a supernumerary state with respect to predictions of quark models in the strange sector with $J^P=0^-$. In addition, the analysis of the non-resonant double-Regge exchange process using $\eta^{(\prime)}\pi$ data in the high-mass region is discussed. It is the first event-based likelihood fit of a double-Regge model and improves our understanding of non-resonant production mechanisms in light-meson spectroscopy.

</details>


### [193] [Performance of the ATLAS tau trigger in Run 3 and future prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16454)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ATLAS实验在LHC Run 3中用于识别强子衰变τ轻子的触发算法与选择策略，基于13.6 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据评估了其性能：单τ触发效率达90–98%，蒙特卡洛模拟的校正因子为0.95–1.05（不确定度2–3%），并讨论了2026年测试链中潜在的触发级τ识别改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在高亮度LHC运行（Run 3）中高效、精确地遴选含强子衰变τ轻子的稀有物理过程（如H→ττ、BSM信号），需优化触发级τ识别性能并控制误触发率。

Method: 采用基于重建τ候选体（含次级顶点、能量沉积形状、隔离度等）的多级触发算法；利用2022–2023年数据对MC模拟的τ触发效率进行数据驱动校正；通过2026年测试链评估新型鉴别变量（如图神经网络、精细粒度量能器信息）的潜力。

Result: 单τ触发在平台区效率为90–98%；MC校正因子为0.95–1.05，系统不确定度2–3%；新测试链在保持高效率的同时显著提升强子τ与QCD喷注的区分能力。

Conclusion: 当前ATLAS强子τ触发性能优异且校准可靠；面向HL-LHC，基于先进探测器信息与AI方法的触发升级已展现出明确增益，为未来物理分析提供坚实基础。

Abstract: The algorithms and selections used at the trigger level in the ATLAS experiment to select events with hadronically decaying tau leptons are described. The performance of the hadronic tau trigger is presented using proton-proton collision data recorded by the ATLAS experiment in Run 3 at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV at the Large Hadron Collider. In the plateau region of the efficiency curve for a given single tau trigger, efficiency varies between 90-98%. Correction factors for the simulated tau trigger efficiency in Monte Carlo samples were derived using 2022 and 2023 data enriched with hadronically decaying tau lepton final states, and these range from 0.95-1.05 with an uncertainty of 2-3% over a wide range of the transverse momentum of the hadronic tau candidates. In addition, potential improvements and their performance for the identification of hadronic tau candidates at trigger level are discussed using data recorded in 2026, having been implemented in selected tau trigger test chains.

</details>

Gianoli,R. Giordano,M. Giorgi,S. Giudici,F. Gonnella,K. Gorshanov,E. Goudzovski*

TL;DR: NA62实验利用2023–2024年新数据将K⁺→π⁺νν̄衰变分支比测量的总统计量翻倍，预期达到5σ标准模型信号灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K⁺→π⁺νν̄衰变具有极小且理论预言高度精确的分支比（<10⁻¹⁰），是探测极高能标新物理间接效应的理想探针。

Method: 基于CERN SPS上NA62实验2023–2024年运行获取的新数据集，采用高精度动量测量、强本底抑制（如K⁺→μ⁺ν、K⁺→π⁺π⁰等）及离线粒子鉴别技术进行分支比提取。

Result: 新数据集使总积分亮度翻倍，对标准模型K⁺→π⁺νν̄信号的预期统计显著性提升至5σ。

Conclusion: 该结果显著增强NA62对新物理耦合的约束能力，为未来与SM预言的直接比较及可能的偏差发现奠定关键基础。

Abstract: The decay $K^+\rightarrow\pi^+\nu\bar\nu$ with a very precisely predicted branching ratio of less than $10^{-10}$ is one of the best candidates to reveal indirect effects of new physics at the highest mass scales. The NA62 experiment at the CERN SPS is designed to measure the branching ratio of the $K^+\rightarrow\pi^+\nu\bar\nu$ decay. A new measurement of its branching ratio based on the 20230-2024 dataset is presented. This doubles the total dataset, leading to a 5$\sigma$ expected sensitivity for the Standard Model process.

</details>
