### [195] [Monopole crystal and effective theory for a monopole-enhanced 3D U(1) gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15922)
*Alessandro Mariani*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了带单极子增强项的U(1)格点规范理论，发现强单极子增强下系统形成反铁磁Néel序基态，并推导出有效正弦-戈登模型；解析预测了随单极子化学势变化的一级相变（对应主导单极子电荷改变）及随规范耦合变化的Ising普适类相变，并通过数值模拟验证部分预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解带单极子增强项的U(1)格点规范理论的非微扰相结构与激发谱，特别是单极子密度显著升高后的集体行为及其对禁闭机制的影响。

Method: 结合解析方法（Polyakov型禁闭论证、有效场论构造、临界行为标度分析）与数值方法（蒙特卡洛模拟、相图扫描、关联函数与序参量计算）。

Result: 1) 大单极子增强下出现反铁磁Néel有序单极子晶格；2) 在该稠密基态上构建有效正弦-戈登模型，保留标准U(1)理论的定性特征；3) 解析预言单极子化学势驱动的一级相变与规范耦合驱动的Ising类连续相变；4) 数值结果支持Néel序与部分相变特征。

Conclusion: 单极子增强可诱导U(1)格点规范理论进入新量子相，其基态具有长程磁序，但低能激发仍由拓扑缺陷主导，禁闭机制可通过推广的Polyakov机制理解；相图包含一级与二级相变线，体现单极子电荷选择与规范涨落竞争的丰富物理。

Abstract: Recently, a version of U(1) lattice gauge theory on the cubic lattice with a monopole enhancement term was considered in the literature. In this work, we further our understanding of this theory with a mixture of analytical and numerical methods. We show that for sufficiently large monopole enhancement, the monopoles are no longer dilute and they arrange themselves into an antiferromagnetic, Néel-ordered ground state. Nonetheless, a variant of Polyakov's argument for the dilute excitations over the dense ground state leads to an effective sine-Gordon model which is qualitatively similar to that of the standard theory. We also predict analytically the presence of first-order transitions as the monopole chemical potential is varied at fixed gauge coupling, corresponding to a change in the dominant monopole charge. Similarly, we predict a transition in the Ising universality class as the gauge coupling is varied. We then use numerical methods to study the phase diagram of the theory and check some of our predictions.

</details>


### [196] [Critical behavior of the bosonized Schwinger model with uniform matrix product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16092)
*Takuya Okuda*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过玻色化方法和哈密顿量格点离散化，结合VUMPS算法与Symanzik有效理论约束的外推，精确计算了θ=π下二维QED（Schwinger模型）的临界质量，结果为(m/g)_c = 0.333555(5)，与最新交错费米子结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定Schwinger模型在θ=π拓扑角下的临界质量，以检验对偶性、手征反常及拓扑相变等基本量子场论机制，并为格点规范理论提供高精度基准。

Method: 采用玻色化后的非紧致标量场哈密顿量形式，构建保持电荷共轭对称性的格点哈密顿量；使用VUMPS算法在无限链上变分求解基态；局域希尔伯特空间采用自由极限下单点约化密度矩阵的谐振子本征基截断；格点修正由Symanzik有效理论指导的拟合函数约束。

Result: (m/g)_c = 0.333 555(5)，不确定度源于 continuum extrapolation 的统计与系统误差联合估计，且与交错费米子方法在同等精度下一致。

Conclusion: 玻色化+VUMPS+Symanzik外推方案可实现Schwinger模型临界参数的亚千分之一精度计算，验证了该方法在含拓扑项低维规范理论中的可靠性与普适性。

Abstract: Quantum electrodynamics in two spacetime dimensions, also known as the Schwinger model, admits a dual formulation in terms of a non-compact boson field. Using a Hamiltonian lattice discretization of the bosonized model, we compute the critical mass of the continuum Schwinger model at $\theta=\pi$. We numerically obtain the ground state on an infinite chain by the variational uniform matrix product state (VUMPS) algorithm, preserving charge conjugation exactly. As a truncated basis for the local Hilbert space, we employ the oscillator basis that diagonalizes the one-site reduced density matrix of the free limit. We constrain the fitting functions for the continuum extrapolation using the Symanzik effective theory for lattice corrections. Our result for the critical mass, $(m/g)_c = 0.333\,555(5)$, agrees with recent staggered-fermion determinations at comparable precision.

</details>


### [197] [Numerical tests of chiral fermion weights with $η$-invariant phases in the 3450 model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16876)
*Tatsuhiro Misumi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文数值检验了一种新提出的连续手征费米子与插值格点规范场耦合的方案，该方案采用Pauli-Villars正则化模长和Atiyah-Patodi-Singer η-不变量定义相位；在二维无反常U(1)模型中，验证了其对Polyakov圈、真空极化诱导质量及含零模背景下的多点关联函数的高精度再现能力，证实APS相位对恢复手征对称性和反常抵消的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点QCD中手征费米子耦合的规范不变性与反常匹配问题，特别是如何在离散格点上精确实现连续手征理论的全局拓扑结构（如η-不变量）和局域手征对称性。

Method: 采用二维无反常U(1)手征模型（左手荷3,4；右手荷5,0），在环面与圆柱几何下，对插值自洽的格点规范场施加APS边界条件，数值计算带PV正则化模长与APS相位的费米子行列式及其物理观测量（Polyakov圈、诱导质量、12点关联函数）。

Result: 零磁通下Polyakov圈误差<0.04%（含APS相位）vs ~3%（无APS）；诱导质量m/e=2.82089≈5/√π；单位磁通下12点关联函数模长误差<0.8%，相位误差由1.8–3.0 mrad降至0.2–0.5 mrad；不同三维延拓引起的单粒子相位变化（15–46 mrad）在反常自由组合中完美抵消（<0.2 mrad）。

Conclusion: APS η-不变量作为手征费米子路径积分相位是保持拓扑敏感性、反常匹配和手征对称性的必要且充分构造；该方案在格点上实现了对连续手征理论量子效应的高精度数值重构。

Abstract: We test numerically a recently proposed prescription for coupling continuum chiral fermions to interpolated lattice gauge fields, in which the fermion weight has a Pauli-Villars (PV) regularized magnitude and a phase given by an Atiyah-Patodi-Singer (APS) $\eta$-invariant in one higher dimension. We use the anomaly-free two-dimensional $U(1)$ theory with left-handed charges $3,4$ and right-handed charges $5,0$, whose torus determinant is known exactly. At zero magnetic flux, with gauge fields interpolated from lattice links, the Polyakov loops evaluated on a $16\times16$ grid of flat connections agree with the exact values within $0.04\%$, whereas omitting the APS phase substantially reduces them to about $3\%$ of the exact result. The vacuum-polarization response yields an induced mass $m/e=2.82089$, close to $5/\sqrt{\pi}=2.82095$. At unit flux, where zero modes make the determinant vanish, we calculate a twelve-point correlator on a cylinder with the generalized APS boundary condition. For three smooth backgrounds its magnitude agrees with the exact result within $0.8\%$. Without the $\eta$-invariant its phase deviates by $1.8$-$3.0$ mrad, and with it by $0.2$-$0.5$ mrad. The phase of each charged fermion changes by $15$-$46$ mrad when the gauge field is extended differently into the third dimension, but the change cancels to below $0.2$ mrad in the anomaly-free combination.

</details>


### [198] [Automatic O(a) Improvement for Wilson Twisted Mass Fermions in the Hamiltonian Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16658)
*Carsten Urbach*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文基于Symanzik有效哈密顿量框架，分析Wilson扭曲质量费米子的O(a)改进机制，严格证明了最大扭角下的自动O(a)改进，并导出了适用于clover项壳层改进的O(a)反项结构，指出各向同性欧几里得时空中的clover项在哈密顿形式下分裂为两个独立系数的项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在格点QCD中实现系统性O(a)改进，避免依赖欧氏作用量的Symanzik展开，需建立纯哈密顿量框架下的对称性驱动的有效理论。

Method: 采用基于时间连续、空间离散格点体系的Symanzik有效哈密顿量方法，仅依据格点哈密顿量的对称性（而非欧氏作用量）构造有效哈密顿量；结合规范不变性、手征对称性破缺模式及canonical变换消去壳层冗余项。

Result: 1) 严格证明Wilson twisted mass费米子在最大扭角下自动O(a)改进；2) 给出mass与Wilson平均实现O(a)改进的哈密顿量条件；3) 导出clover壳层改进所需的完整O(a) counterterm结构；4) 发现各向同性欧氏clover项在哈密顿框架下分解为两项，具独立系数；5) 利用canonical变换消除on-shell冗余项。

Conclusion: Symanzik有效哈密顿量框架为Wilson型费米子的系统性O(a)改进提供了对称性本征、非微扰兼容的表述，揭示了欧氏与哈密顿格点正规化之间反项结构的本质差异。

Abstract: We apply the recently developed Symanzik effective Hamiltonian framework to Wilson twisted mass fermions. The effective Hamiltonian is constructed solely based on the symmetries of the lattice Hamiltonian without recourse to the Symanzik effective action in Euclidean space-time. We use it to prove automatic $O(a)$ improvement at maximal twist, as well as $O(a)$ improvement via so-called mass and Wilson average. Moreover, we derive the structure of $O(a)$ counterterms pre-requisite for clover on-shell improvement of Wilson fermions. We show how to remove on-shell redundant contributions using canonical transformations, and find that the well known clover term in isotropic Euclidean space time splits into two terms with independent coefficients.

</details>


### [199] [$NΩ$ Dibaryon near Unitarity from Physical-Point Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16989)
*Liang Zhang,Yan Lyu,Sinya Aoki,Takumi Doi,Tetsuo Hatsuda,Song Zhang,Yu-Gang Ma*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用HAL QCD方法，在物理夸克质量下的(2+1)味格点QCD中研究了NΩ系统的⁵S₂相互作用，发现其具有净吸引力，并支持一个接近幺正极限的浅束缚态；给出中子-Ω和质子-Ω束缚能分别为0.61 MeV和1.25 MeV，为实验上通过强子对动量关联（femtoscopy）搜寻NΩ dibaryon提供了第一性原理基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NΩ系统是理想的六夸克dibaryon候选者，因其核子与Ω重子之间无共同价夸克，可规避价夸克Pauli排斥，从而更易形成束缚态。

Method: 采用HAL QCD方法，在物理π介子质量（m_π ≈ 137 MeV）的(2+1)-味格点QCD模拟中提取⁵S₂ NΩ势，结合Lüscher公式与T矩阵极点分析确定束缚能，并显式计入质子-Ω间有限电荷半径的Coulomb吸引修正。

Result: NΩ⁵S₂相互作用在可分辨距离内呈净吸引力，支持一个浅束缚态；T矩阵极点分析得B_{nΩ} = 0.61(^{+0.41}_{-0.30})(^{+0.60}_{-0.18}) MeV，计入Coulomb效应后B_{pΩ} = 1.25(^{+0.46}_{-0.39})(^{+0.69}_{-0.25}) MeV。

Conclusion: NΩ系统在物理夸克质量下存在弱束缚态，为首个基于第一性原理的定量预言，为RHIC、LHC等实验开展NΩ femtoscopy及dibaryon搜索提供了关键理论支撑。

Abstract: The $N\Omega$ system is a promising dibaryon candidate because its constituents share no valence quarks and therefore avoid valence-quark Pauli repulsion. We determine the $^{5}S_{2}$ $N\Omega$ interaction in (2+1)-flavor lattice QCD at physical quark masses ($m_\pi\simeq137~\text{MeV}$) using the HAL QCD method. The interaction is attractive over the resolved range and supports a shallow state near unitarity. A direct $T$-matrix pole analysis gives $B_{n\Omega} = 0.61\,(^{+0.41}_{-0.30})\,(^{+0.60}_{-0.18})~\text{MeV}$; including finite-charge-size Coulomb attraction yields $B_{p\Omega} = 1.25\,(^{+0.46}_{-0.39})\,(^{+0.69}_{-0.25})~\text{MeV}$. These results provide a first-principles benchmark for future $N\Omega$ femtoscopy and dibaryon searches.

</details>
